Motivation: 决策模型针对固定选项输出概率，而非生成文本。尽管此类模型已有发展，其英语以外的公开评测结果仍不足。论文旨在构建覆盖多种欧洲语言的模型与评测，并分析这种模型设计在不同语言和任务上的表现及局限。

Method: 研究构建了三种规模的模型，分别为四十亿、九十亿和二百七十亿参数。模型冻结开放权重基础模型，仅训练低秩适配器和指针预测头，覆盖二十七种欧洲语言。作者在未用于训练的欧洲综合评测集上测试模型，该评测集包含十一个数据来源、九类任务和二十七种语言；同时通过事后分析考察校准、自动化潜力及基础模型语言覆盖的影响。

Result: 在用于开发和模型选择的评测集上，最大模型准确率为百分之八十七点五，汇总校准误差为零点零一六，按数据来源加权后为零点零三五。误校准主要随任务而变化：知识类任务普遍过度自信，可接受性判断则普遍信心不足，单一温度校准难以同时纠正两者。以百分之五错误率为限的可自动化决策比例，与校正偶然水平后的技能相关性高于与原始准确率的相关性。跨二百一十种语言的分析显示，改变训练过程对同一规模模型的分数影响较小；不在基础模型已记录语言范围内的语言，分数则低二十三至二十八个百分点。此外，该模型在英语公开评测上落后于两种对比模型，包括使用相同基础模型的对比模型。论文还指出，多来源训练数据可能出现标签词冲突，注入式数据增强也可能使模型学到取巧规则。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，多语言决策模型可以在广泛欧洲语言任务上取得较高准确率和较好的整体校准，但其可靠性并非只由语言决定：任务类型会形成系统性校准偏差，而基础模型的语言覆盖对跨语言泛化尤为关键。评估模型实际自动化价值时，校正偶然水平后的技能指标可能比准确率更有参考意义。研究结论基于单次随机种子实验，且评测集参与了模型选择，因此仍需独立数据和多次重复实验验证。

Abstract: Decision models read a document and return a probability for each of a fixed set of options instead of generating text. Several appeared in 2026, but none has published results outside English. We describe Zuse, three decision models (4B, 9B and 27B) that train a low-rank adapter and a pointer head on frozen open-weight bases, targeting 27 European languages, and use them to study how this design behaves across languages and tasks. On a broad European suite of 11 sources excluded from training, covering 9 task groups and 27 languages, a development set that also guided model selection, the 27B model reaches 87.5% accuracy with a pooled expected calibration error of 0.016, and 0.035 when weighted per source. Post-hoc analyses of these single-seed results give three findings. First, miscalibration is structured by task rather than language: on parallel reading and topic items in 26 languages the confidence gap varies about as much as sampling noise, while across sources it spans -0.07 to +0.09, with knowledge tasks overconfident and acceptability judgements underconfident at all three sizes, so one fitted temperature cannot correct both. Second, the share of decisions that can be automated at a 5% error budget follows chance-corrected skill (Spearman 0.97 over 33 source and model pairs) more closely than raw accuracy (0.91). Third, reach beyond the training languages is set by the base model: across 210 languages, changing the training run moved scores by at most about 2 points within a size, while languages outside the base's documented list trail by 23 to 28 points at every size. On English public suites Zuse is behind Jev and Kev, including Kev models on the same bases. We also report two pitfalls in building multi-source training data: label-word collision between topic schemes, and an injection augmentation that taught a shortcut. Zuse can be tried at https://bineric.com/zuse.

</details>


### [38] [Mass Producing Training Data with Surprising Side Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06324)
*Yaowen Ye,Jacob Steinhardt,Sarah Schwettmann,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出自动发现训练数据意外行为副作用数据集的方法，并构建了包含六千五百七十五个数据集、覆盖八类行为的资源，用于研究模型行为如何泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据可能在训练领域之外引发意想不到的模型行为，例如不安全代码数据导致广泛失准，过时鸟类名称则使模型呈现特定历史时期的角色。此类现象过去只有少量零散案例，且多由人工构造或偶然发现，因此难以系统研究和预测。

Method: 论文将这类数据集定义为满足三项目标：能够诱发目标行为、读起来像普通的后训练数据、且令阅读数据的人类审查者感到意外。研究者用大语言模型评估这三项指标，并结合进化搜索、强化学习和潜意识学习方法寻找高分数据集；随后使用留出指标重新评分并筛选结果。

Result: 研究流程产出六千五百七十五个数据集，覆盖八类行为，其中许多数据集引发的副作用能够跨模型家族和模型规模泛化。自动评估指标与机器学习研究者的判断相符；在预测副作用方面，前沿模型的表现优于人类。

Conclusion: 这些数据集为系统研究训练数据引发的行为泛化提供了测试平台，也为模型部署前预测训练副作用奠定基础。论文的主要贡献是将过去零散、偶发的案例发现转化为可规模化的自动化探索流程。

Abstract: Training data can have unexpected side effects on language model behavior outside the training domain: insecure code data can induce broad misalignment, and outdated bird names can induce a 19th-century persona. Understanding such generalization is hard because only dozens of isolated examples exist, each constructed by hand or discovered by accident. We propose automatically discovering such datasets with surprising side effects (DSSEs). We define three objectives for DSSEs: they should (1) elicit a target behavior, (2) read like ordinary post-training data, and (3) be surprising to human auditors reading the data. We operationalize each with LLM judges, and search for high-scoring datasets via evolution, reinforcement learning, and subliminal learning approaches. After re-scoring and filtering with held-out metrics, our pipeline produces a collection of 6,575 DSSEs across 8 behaviors, many of which generalize across model families and sizes. We find our automated metrics track ML researchers' judgments, and frontier models outperform humans at predicting side effects. These datasets form a testbed for studying generalization and lay the groundwork for predicting training side effects before model deployment. Explore them at https://dsse.transluce.org.

</details>


### [39] [Training-Free Character-Length Control in Summarization with Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06371)
*Steffen Castle,Nils Feldhus,Christopher Ebert,Leonhard Hennig,Sebastian Möller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的扩散语言模型推理方法，可更准确地生成符合指定字符数的摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标题和文章预览等场景要求文本严格符合字符数限制，但自回归语言模型难以精确控制长度；现有训练方法需要针对任务微调，零样本提示则通常只能近似满足要求。

Method: 在扩散去噪的每一步估计文本的预期字符数，并动态插入或移除被遮蔽的位置，使生成过程逐步趋近目标长度。该方法无需微调，面向现成的掩码扩散语言模型。

Result: 在三个摘要基准上，该方法将字符数误差降低了一个数量级，同时在自动评估和人工评估中保持了有竞争力的摘要质量。

Conclusion: 通过在推理过程中动态调整序列长度，无需额外训练即可显著提升掩码扩散语言模型的定长摘要能力。

Abstract: Summarization with a fixed character length is important for tasks such as headline and article preview generation, where text must fit an exact space. Autoregressive language models (LMs) struggle with precise character-length control: training-based methods require task-specific fine-tuning, while zero-shot prompting strategies achieve only approximate compliance. We propose DELTA (Diffusion Expected Length Targeting Algorithm), a training-free inference-time method for masked diffusion LMs that estimates expected character length at each denoising step and dynamically inserts or removes masked positions to steer toward a target length. DELTA requires no fine-tuning and is designed for off-the-shelf masked diffusion LMs. Across three summarization benchmarks, it reduces character-length errors by an order of magnitude compared to baselines while maintaining competitive summary quality in both automatic and human evaluation.

</details>


### [40] [Presentation Sensitivity in Typed Decision Models for Subjective Text Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06384)
*Jatin Agrawal,Radhika Mamidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究审计三种自动评估器对呈现方式的敏感性，发现选项位置、背书和决策措辞会显著改变判断；可靠评估需分别检验结果可重复性、呈现稳健性及与人类偏好的吻合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化决策模型可通过选择、评分和概率输出，低成本地自动评估文本，但其判断是否会受到文本以外的展示因素影响尚不明确。研究旨在系统检验这种呈现敏感性，并厘清评估器稳定性与人类偏好一致性之间的差别。

Method: 研究考察杰夫、克莱夫闪选和佩珀斯蒂决策器的原生选择与评分接口，覆盖六个数据集、九千条经多方标注的幽默、帮助性和摘要样本，共收集七十万两千项判断。实验在不改变待评文本的前提下，干预选项位置、标识符、作者身份声明、背书、决策框架和评分标准。

Result: 呈现方式对判断产生显著影响：杰夫对摘要中第二位置选项的概率优势最高达六点一一个百分点；反转读者背书后，佩珀斯蒂决策器在百分之四十点四的幽默文本配对中改变了选择；拒绝式决策框架使克莱夫闪选对摘要的人类偏好一致度下降二十三点三六个百分点。评分格式也会改变分数水平，尽管排序往往仍能保持。

Conclusion: 评估器的可重复性、对呈现方式的稳健性以及与人类判断的一致性是彼此不同的属性。将其用于自动评估流程前，应针对具体依赖的选择结果和分数解释进行验证，不能仅凭输出稳定或评分排序来推断其可靠性。

Abstract: Typed decision models offer structured choices, scores, and probabilities for low-cost automated evaluation. We present a controlled audit of presentation sensitivity in Jev, Clef Flash, and Perplexity Decider across native choice and score interfaces. Across six datasets with 9,000 multiply annotated humor, helpfulness, and summarization items, we collect 702,000 judgments under interventions that vary positions, identifiers, authorship claims, endorsements, decision framing, and rating rubrics while preserving the evaluated texts. Jev's second-position probability advantage reaches 6.11 percentage points on summaries; reversing reader endorsements changes Perplexity's selected humor text on 40.40% of pairs; and rejection framing reduces Clef's summary agreement with human preferences by 23.36 points. Rating formats also shift score levels while often preserving rankings. Across these findings, repeatability, presentation robustness, and human alignment emerge as distinct properties of an evaluator. Reliable use therefore requires validating the decisions and score interpretations on which an evaluation pipeline depends.

</details>


### [41] [Where Should a KV-Cache Repair Budget Go? Distractor Structure and Contextual Compatibility in Non-Prefix Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06397)
*Yuxing Peng,Zhiqing Tang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 非前缀键值缓存复用会显著损害依赖上下文的问题回答能力；有限修复预算通常应优先用于答案所在片段，而非修复其他片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非前缀键值缓存复用会将检索到的片段分别编码后拼接缓存，可能破坏片段之间的上下文关系。研究旨在确定：当修复预算有限时，应修复哪些片段，才能最大程度恢复回答能力。

Method: 研究在三种70亿至80亿参数模型和四个涵盖对话记忆、开放域问答及接口文档的数据集上，改变答案所在目标片段周围的片段，并比较不同缓存修复策略。策略包括重算目标片段、直接插入目标片段的完整预填充缓存，以及依据问题注意力或检索结果选择片段；同时与信息流式重排方法在相同预算下比较。

Result: 相较于完整预填充，缓存复用会使19%至100%的上下文依赖问题无法回答；其他片段与目标片段类型越相似，损失越严重。受损信息分散在其他片段中，因此修复少数其他片段收益有限，优先修复目标片段更有效。在全部七种测试设置中，基于已复用缓存重算目标片段均优于直接插入其完整预填充缓存，但重算后的缓存仍与完整预填充缓存差距较大。问题注意力在开发集问题中最多仅有18.8%能选中目标片段；检索器选中目标片段的频率更高。在开放域问答和接口文档的九种设置中，有六种情况下，以5%的预算将排名最高的片段移至问题旁并重新计算，显著优于信息流式重排。对话记忆任务中，重算片段则从未显著优于各设置选定的词元级选择器。

Conclusion: 有限预算下，修复答案所在片段并使其与已有缓存建立上下文兼容性，是开放域问答和接口文档任务中的有效策略；仅复制该片段的完整预填充缓存并不足够。不过，策略效果依任务类型而异：对话记忆中，片段重算没有显示出相对词元级选择方法的显著优势。

Abstract: Non-prefix KV-cache reuse encodes retrieved chunks independently and concatenates their caches. To find where a small repair budget should be spent, we vary the chunks around the answer-bearing one (the target) across three 7B-8B models and four datasets of dialogue memory, open-domain QA and API documentation. Reuse loses 19-100% of the context-dependent questions that full prefill answers, and it loses more when the other chunks are of the same kind as the target. Because the recoverable damage is spread over these other chunks, restoring a few of them recovers little, and the budget is better spent on the target. This works through contextual compatibility: recomputing the target against the reused cache beats inserting its full-prefill KV in all seven settings tested, although the recomputed KV stays far from the full-prefill KV. Question attention over reused caches, however, selects the target in at most 18.8% of development questions. A retriever finds it more often, and recomputing its top chunk after moving it next to the question significantly beats InfoFlow-style reordering at the same 5% budget in six of nine open-domain QA and API-documentation settings. On dialogue memory, though, recomputing chunks never significantly beats the token-level selector chosen for each setting.

</details>


### [42] [Interpretable Text Embeddings Based on Hierarchical Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06402)
*Kohei Oda,Po-Min Chuang,Kiyoaki Shirai,Natthawut Kertkeidkachorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种层级式问题构造方法，用较少的概括性问题替代大量细粒度问题，从而提升问答式文本嵌入的可解释性，并尽量保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问答式文本嵌入通过一系列是非问题的答案表示文本，具有信息细致、易于解释和任务表现较好的优点，但人工检查大量问题成本较高。此外，问题数量与问题粒度之间如何权衡，尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 构建由粗到细的问题层级：层级越深，问题越具体、数量越多。方法通过递归聚类组织问题，以对比式提示生成问题，并进行问题对齐。在解释文本时，可用较浅层级中相对应的问题替代深层问题，减少人工需要检查的问题数量。

Result: 通用任务实验表明，该方法在保持任务性能的同时提升了可解释性。问题数量受限时，即使数量相同，较浅层级的问题通常也比更深层级的问题带来更高的任务性能。

Conclusion: 问题层级为问答式文本嵌入提供了调节问题粒度与数量的途径。总体而言，较浅层级的问题有助于降低解释成本并维持较好的任务表现；更深层级则提供更具体的描述，可按需求用于细化解释。

Abstract: QA-based text embeddings represent texts as vectors consisting of 1/0 answers to yes/no questions. Although these embeddings provide detailed information for interpretation and achieve high task performance, they often require humans to examine a large number of questions. Furthermore, the relationship between the number of questions and the appropriate level of question granularity has not been sufficiently studied. To address these challenges, we propose a method for constructing hierarchical questions for text embeddings. As the hierarchy deepens, questions become more specific and their number increases. During interpretation, we reduce the number of questions that humans need to examine by replacing questions at deeper levels with aligned questions at shallower levels. The question hierarchy is constructed through recursive clustering, contrastive prompting, and question alignment. Experimental results on general-purpose tasks show that our method improves interpretability while maintaining task performance. It is also found that, when only a limited number of questions are available, questions at shallower levels tend to achieve higher task performance than those at deeper levels, even with the same number of questions.

</details>


### [43] [Ruby-OPD: Enhancing Pronunciation Controllability and Accuracy for Flow-Matching Text-to-Speech via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06403)
*Rui Hu,Xiaolong Lin,Zhenpeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段日语语音合成框架，兼顾汉字读音准确性与发音可控性，并在不依赖配对语音的情况下提升原始文本的发音表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日语文本混合汉字与假名，且存在大量多音字。传统级联系统便于控制读音，却会丢失表记中的词汇语义和韵律线索；直接输入原始文本的零样本语音合成自然度较好，但容易读错多音字，也缺乏明确的发音控制能力。因此，需要同时利用表记信息与目标读音。

Method: 框架分为两阶段：第一阶段研究带有读音旁注的文本表示，并通过扰动表记，使模型同时保留表层书写形式和目标读音，实现可靠控制；第二阶段在大规模纯文本语料上采用在策略蒸馏，提升模型直接处理原始表记文本时的读音准确性，无需配对语音数据。整体方法基于流匹配语音合成。

Result: 在常用汉字读音基准测试中，模型以原始文本作为输入时，读音准确率达到百分之九十七点一三，目标读音音素错误率为百分之二点零二，句子级音素错误率为百分之零点七零，句子级字符错误率为百分之四点二一；同时保持了较强的发音可控性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过结合读音旁注表示与纯文本在策略蒸馏，弥补了级联方法和直接使用原始文本方法各自的不足，表明无需配对语音也能增强日语语音合成的读音准确性，并兼顾自然文本输入与发音控制。

Abstract: Achieving both high pronunciation accuracy and fine-grained controllability remains a central challenge in Japanese text-to-speech (TTS), as Japanese is an unsegmented language mixing logographic Kanji and phonographic Kana with extensive polyphony. Traditional cascaded systems convert text into phoneme sequences via a front-end, ensuring pronunciation control but discarding lexical semantics and prosodic cues in surface orthography. Conversely, recent zero-shot TTS models directly consume raw orthographic text to improve naturalness, yet suffer from polyphone errors and lack explicit pronunciation control. To bridge this gap, we propose a two-stage framework for flow-matching TTS. First, we investigate Ruby text representations with orthography perturbation to jointly preserve surface forms and target pronunciations for reliable control. Second, we introduce On-Policy Distillation (OPD) on large-scale text-only corpora to enhance pronunciation accuracy on raw orthographic text without paired speech. On the Joyo-Kanji-Yomi benchmark, our model achieves 97.13% Accuracy, 2.02% Target PER, 0.70% Sentence PER, and 4.21% Sentence CER under raw text while retaining strong controllability.

</details>


### [44] [Twists and Turns: A Graph-Based Benchmark and Framework for Long-Term Memory in Exploration Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06414)
*Chongyuan Dai,Yaling Shen,Zirui Song,Junfeng Liao,Jinpeng Hu,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出长期探索任务记忆基准与图结构记忆框架。基准包含二百二十四段对话和二千零十八个问答实例；实验显示该基准对现有系统具有挑战性，而所提框架表现更优，并能迁移至外部基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究、软件开发等长期问题求解过程，并非单纯积累事实，而是沿着反复试错的轨迹推进：不同方案会分支，结果会促使方案修订，早期尝试也可能被重新考虑。既有长期记忆研究主要关注事实回忆与更新，忽略了这些探索过程中的关联结构，因此难以评估或支持对探索历程的记忆。

Method: 论文提出长期探索记忆基准，将探索过程表示为图，并通过演化机制逐步提高图结构复杂度，据此构建对话和问答实例。论文还提出图式记忆框架，逐步把对话整理为相互关联的记忆节点，并结合混合检索与邻居扩展来检索证据。

Result: 实验表明，该基准对现有检索式和记忆式系统构成显著挑战。所提图式记忆框架取得更好的表现，并能有效泛化至外部基准。摘要未提供具体分数或比较幅度。

Conclusion: 长期探索任务的记忆需要保留方案、尝试结果、修订与重新考量之间的联系，而不应仅保存孤立事实。以图结构组织记忆并利用关联检索，有望更好地支持这类复杂任务；所提出的基准也为后续研究提供了评测工具。

Abstract: Long-term problem-solving processes, such as scientific research and software development, proceed along an evolving trial-and-error trajectory toward a central goal. We term these \textit{exploration tasks}, which require models to remember not only facts but also how solutions are tested against intermediate outcomes and early attempts are continually revised or revived. However, existing long-term memory research focuses on factual recall and updates, overlooking the interconnected nature of exploration. We observe that exploration processes naturally form graphs, with branching approaches linked by revisions and reconsiderations. Motivated by this, we propose \textsc{LExMem}, a graph-guided benchmark designed to evaluate \underline{\textsc{Mem}}ory in \underline{\textsc{L}}ong-term \underline{\textsc{Ex}}ploration tasks. We model exploration processes as graphs and introduce an evolutionary mechanism to increase their structural complexity, yielding 224 dialogues and 2,018 question-answer instances derived from graph structures. Building on the same insight, we propose \textsc{LExGraph}, a graph-based memory framework that incrementally organizes dialogues into interconnected memory nodes and retrieves evidence through hybrid retrieval and neighbor expansion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textsc{LExMem} poses a significant challenge to existing retrieval- and memory-based systems, whereas \textsc{LExGraph} achieves superior performance and generalizes effectively to external benchmarks. Codes and datasets are available at https://github.com/mbzuai-nlp/LExMem.

</details>


### [45] [EvoGuard: Mitigating Degradation in Online Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06444)
*Yanli Wang,Zichen Bai,Tianyue Jiang,Kunming Shao,Junhao Shi,Xueqin Liu,Jinguo Sun,Muye Li,Tingting Bi,Yanlin Wang,Shaoqiang Zheng,Xuefei Diao,Zhuwei Zeng,Nan Duan,Peng Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvoGuard通过低延迟的成对技能选择，减少在线技能更新造成的性能退化，并提升多个任务基准上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体可将经验整理为自然语言技能并跨任务复用，但现有在线演化方法存在风险与成本问题：直接采用新技能可能导致技能质量逐渐下降；依靠留出集验证则需在每次更新后进行评测，速度慢、成本高。

Method: 研究者迭代运行智能体，构建技能池，并在私有测试集上评估技能以生成效用标签。随后将技能两两配对，通过拒绝采样保留效用差异明确的样本，并利用两个标签词对应的模型对数几率训练选择器。部署时，选择器只需一次前向计算即可判断应采用哪项技能。

Result: 与无筛选地采用每个新技能相比，EvoGuard在家庭环境、科学世界和代码竞赛基准上的成功率分别提高11.0%、5.9%和2.7%。摘要还指出，其单次前向选择比在私有测试集上进行评测显著更快。

Conclusion: EvoGuard以成对选择机制在技能更新速度与质量控制之间取得平衡，能够以较低延迟和成本缓解技能退化，并提升智能体在多类任务中的表现。

Abstract: Skills let language agents reuse experience across tasks as natural-language documents. Recent work evolves skills from interaction, but existing methods have limitations when skills evolve online. Prior methods update a skill offline from a batch of trajectories collected over many tasks. Some then adopt the new skill without any check, which can degrade the skill over time. Others keep it only if it improves performance on a held-out validation set, which is too slow and costly to run after every update. We introduce EvoGuard, a pairwise skill selector that mitigates skill degradation at low latency and cost. We first run the agent iteratively to build pools of skills and score each skill on a private test set to obtain utility labels. We pair the skills and use rejection sampling to keep only pairs with a clear utility gap. The selector is trained on the logits of two label tokens to pick the better skill. At deployment it decides in a single forward pass, which is significantly faster than evaluation on the private test set. Compared with adopting every new skill, EvoGuard improves success by 11.0% on ALFWorld, 5.9% on ScienceWorld, and 2.7% on LiveCodeBench.

</details>


### [46] [LibriSpeech-Real-QA: A Benchmark for Streaming Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06496)
*Zewen Zhan,Hui Lu,Xueyuan Chen,Huimeng Wang,Yikai Huang,Xixin Wu,Shiyin Kang,Bin Xia,Zhiyong Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出用于评估语音大语言模型实时语音交互能力的数据集与基准，并发现现有模型往往要等说话结束后才作答；同时探索了使预训练语音模型处理流式问答的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数语音大语言模型以离线方式处理语音，必须等完整语音片段输入后才开始回答，难以满足边听边交互的实际需求，例如在说话过程中持续统计某个姓名出现的次数。由于缺少相应的基准和评测协议，模型的实时交互能力尚难以系统衡量。

Method: 构建《自由之声实时问答》数据集与基准，从《自由之声》语料中设计多种实时问答任务，评估模型能否在音频分块持续到达时识别目标词。论文还测试了多种主流实时商业模型，并探索不同建模方案，使预训练语音模型能够完成此类任务。

Result: 测试发现，多种主流实时商业模型仍倾向于等到整个语音轮次结束后才作答，尚未充分实现真正的流式交互。论文展示了预训练语音模型完成该任务的可行性，并建立了可供后续研究比较的基线。

Conclusion: 该研究针对实时语音交互评测不足的问题，提供了数据集、基准和初步基线。结果表明，现有模型在边听边答方面仍有明显局限，而所探索的方法为进一步提升流式语音理解与交互能力提供了起点。

Abstract: Most speech large language models (SLMs) still process speech in an offline manner. They only start responding after they receive the complete speech segment. However, many real-world applications require streaming speech interaction, for which the audio continuously flows in and the machine is required to answer questions based on the so far received audio chunks. For example, a user may ask the system to continuously count how many times a person's name has been mentioned while they are speaking. We observe a lack of benchmarks and evaluation protocols for real-time speech interaction. Motivated by this, we propose LibriSpeech-Real-QA, a dataset and a benchmark for real-time speech interaction. We construct different types of real-time question-answer pairs from LibriSpeech to evaluate whether speech LLMs can identify target words in streaming audio. We evaluate several popular real-time commercial models and find that they still tend to respond only after the speech turn has finished. We demonstrate that we can enable a pretrained SLM to perform this task, where we explore several modeling designs and establish a solid baseline for future research.

</details>


### [47] [CRESiST: Self-Enhancing Explorative Policy Learning for Simultaneous Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06501)
*Chenyang Le,Pei Zhang,Yuxiang Chen,Bing Han,Derek F. Wong,Baosong Yang,Yanmin Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于全模态大语言模型的端到端交错式实时语音翻译框架，并通过演员—评论家自增强学习优化读写策略，在翻译质量与延迟之间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时语音翻译需要兼顾译文质量与响应延迟。现有基于大语言模型的方法多采用前缀增量生成，导致键值缓存重复计算，并依赖外部语音活动检测处理无界语音流；同时，固定的离线对齐方式也限制了实时读写策略的学习。

Method: 框架将语音分块与文本词元交错输入，实现键值缓存的完整复用，并支持无需预先切分、可处理无限长度输入的推理。训练方面，提出演员—评论家自增强流程：循环神经网络转导器演员动态采样读写轨迹，基于大语言模型的评论家根据内在语义奖励与延迟奖励评估轨迹，再将优选策略蒸馏回演员。

Result: 在句子级和篇章级基准测试中，该方法取得了新的质量—延迟帕累托前沿，并显著优于现有级联系统与静态对齐基线。摘要未提供具体数值，无法进一步量化提升幅度。

Conclusion: 交错式输入与缓存复用可缓解实时推理中的计算和分段瓶颈；结合基于奖励的策略自增强，则有助于在缺少僵化离线对齐的情况下学习有效的实时读写行为。实验结果表明，该方案具备提升实时语音翻译质量与效率的潜力。

Abstract: Simultaneous Speech Translation (SimulST) requires balancing translation quality and latency. While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in offline translation, current LLM-based SimulST systems predominantly rely on prefix-incremental generation. This paradigm suffers from redundant Key-Value cache recomputation and necessitates external Voice Activity Detection to process unbounded streams. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose an end-to-end interleaved read-write framework built upon an omni-modal LLM. By interleaving speech chunks and text tokens, our architecture achieves full KV cache reuse, natively supporting segmentation-free, infinite-length inference. Furthermore, to learn optimal streaming policies without rigid offline alignments, we introduce a novel Actor-Critic self-enhancement pipeline. An RNN-T Actor dynamically samples read-write trajectories, while an LLM-based Critic evaluates these paths via intrinsic semantic and latency rewards, distilling the optimal policy back to the Actor. Extensive experiments on sentence-level (FLEURS, CoVoST2) and document-level (MuST-C, ACL 60/60) benchmarks demonstrate that our model establishes new state-of-the-art quality-latency Pareto frontiers, substantially outperforming existing cascaded and static-alignment baselines.

</details>


### [48] [NaijaFacts: A Framework for Disentangling Language from Knowledge in Facts about Nigeria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06504)
*Zainab Afolabi,Nikita Kozodoi,Jack Butler,Babs Khalidson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究推出面向尼日利亚事实与统计知识的多语种基准，发现模型在非洲相关知识上的缺口远大于低资源语言带来的性能损失，而且这一问题遍及非洲各国。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非洲语言基准多将通用问题翻译成本地语言，主要测量语言能力，却无法判断模型是否掌握非洲事实。研究旨在区分模型缺少相关知识与模型虽有知识、却难以用低资源语言表达这两类问题。

Method: 研究者与母语者合作，构建关于尼日利亚事实和官方统计数据的问题集，每题均提供英语、尼日利亚皮钦语、约鲁巴语、伊博语和豪萨语版本；并设置英国和美国的相似问题，形成国家与语言交叉矩阵，以分离知识效应和语言效应。研究评测了八种前沿及尼日利亚适配模型，并用表现最好的前沿模型扩展评测至非洲五十四国。

Result: 知识效应占主导：英语提问时，模型在美国和英国问题上的准确率约为百分之四十九至五十，在尼日利亚问题上仅为百分之二十。语言效应较小，但随网络内容代表性下降而加剧；从英语切换到约鲁巴语，尼日利亚问题准确率下降九个百分点，美国和英国问题最多下降二十四个百分点。模型规模提升未能弥补尼日利亚知识缺口：最强模型在英美问题上达到百分之六十六至七十九，在尼日利亚统计问题上却均未超过百分之三十一。扩展评测中，模型在非洲文化和政治问题上的准确率分别为百分之九十六和百分之九十八，在统计问题上仅为百分之二十六。

Conclusion: 模型对非洲的知识不足并非单一国家或语言造成，而是广泛存在，尤其体现在统计知识方面。改进非洲语言模型不能只依赖扩大模型规模或提升翻译、语言能力，还需要补充可靠、具地域代表性的非洲知识与数据。

Abstract: Multilingual benchmarks for African languages mostly translate generic questions into local languages, measuring linguistic competence but not whether models know facts about Africa. We introduce \naija, a benchmark of factual and statistical questions about Nigeria, authored by native speakers and grounded in authoritative official statistics, with every question available in English, Nigerian Pidgin, \yoruba, Igbo, and Hausa. Answering similar questions for the UK and US yields a country by language matrix that disentangles a language effect (the model knows the fact but degrades in a low-resource language) from a knowledge effect (the fact is absent in every language). Across eight frontier and Nigeria-adapted models, the knowledge effect dominates: English-query accuracy is 49--50\% for the US/UK but 20\% for Nigeria. The language effect is smaller and tracks web representation, degrading from English to \yoruba{} (9 points on Nigeria, up to 24 on US/UK questions). We find scale does not help: the strongest models reach 66--79\% on the UK and the US but none exceed 31\% on Nigerian statistics. Extending to all 54 African countries with our best performing frontier model shows the same profile continent-wide, 96\% on culture and 98\% on politics against 26\% on statistics, so the gap is not specific to Nigeria.

</details>


### [49] [Dialect Matters: A Dialect-Aware Baseline for Igala-English Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06517)
*Etuh E.,Eli. A. Jiya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出伊加拉语—英语机器翻译基线，并发现混合方言训练有助于提升不同方言间的表现均衡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊加拉语属于自然语言处理中的低资源语言，现有研究尚未建立其平行语料。该语言存在显著方言差异，同一英语表达可对应二十至六十种伊加拉语形式，因此需要考察方言差异对机器翻译的影响。

Method: 构建含一千一百三十七个句子的伊加拉语—英语语料，其中八百九十九句用于训练，并首次标注方言类别，涵盖伊达、安卡帕、伊巴吉、奥古古及其他方言。研究以一个七千八百万参数的多语种翻译模型为基础进行微调，比较混合方言训练与仅用伊达方言训练的效果，并开展训练轮数消融分析。

Result: 混合方言模型取得均衡的翻译表现，词组匹配指标为十一点六六，字符级指标为四十六点六九。仅用伊达方言训练的模型在伊达方言上的指标为十三点二，在伊巴吉方言上降至八点五。训练轮数分析显示，八轮左右趋于收敛，十五轮时出现过拟合。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了首批开放的伊加拉语机器翻译系统之一，并首次分析方言差异对翻译表现的影响。结果表明，若要提升跨方言公平性，训练数据应纳入多种方言；所建语料与基线可支持后续研究。

Abstract: The massive permeability of intelligent systems in every sphere of human activities have greatly enhanced clarity, ease of work, and increased general global understanding which has significantly awaken the use of intelligent systems by all users. Intelligent systems greatly rely on precise dataset/corpus for the correctness of their output. Despite efforts by researchers to address Low-resource language in the Natural Language Processing (NLP), Igala still has zero parallel corpora despite efforts by researchers. We present a dialect-aware baseline for Igala-English machine translation, among first open systems and first with dialectal variation analysis. Our dataset of 1137 sentences (899 training) is first with dialect tags: Idah 459 (40.4%), Ankpa 297 (26.1%), Ibaji 160 (14.1%), Other 187 (16.4%), Ogugu 32 (2.8%). Same English yields 20-60 Igala forms. Mixed-dialect training achieves balanced BLEU 11.66 versus Idah-only model which drops 4.7 BLEU on Ibaji (13.2 on Idah, 8.5 on Ibaji), proving dialect fairness requires mixing. Fine-tuned from Helsinki-NLP/opus-mt-en-mul (78M), epoch ablation shows convergence at 8 epochs and overfitting at 15. BLEU 11.66, chrF 46.69. Analysis shows 899 sentences on log-linear trend consistent with African low-resource literature. This work provides first dialectal impact analysis for Igala and establishes baseline for future work.

</details>


### [50] [When Do Summaries Become Instructions? Unsupported Policy in LLM Agents' Self-Written Handoff Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06545)
*Wenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，长程智能体的交接摘要偶尔会虚构权限限制或授权；虚假授权可能促使后续实例越权，而明确说明审批要求的来源能显著减少虚假限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程大语言模型智能体会用摘要压缩历史，但摘要可能错误陈述哪些操作需要审批、哪些操作已获许可。这类错误是否会改变后续实例的行为，以及错误从何而来，尚需检验。

Method: 研究者在合成工具使用任务中设置真实权限台账，并检查交接摘要中的权限陈述。两名人工智能评读者仅审阅规则筛出的留出摘要，以识别缺乏记录依据的限制和授权。随后通过同一状态重放，将相关整句替换为中性内容，比较后续实例的行为；另测试改变系统提示、明确说明审批要求来源，能否减少虚假限制。

Result: 两种开放权重模型中，缺乏依据的限制分别出现在可读摘要的百分之一点二和百分之二点四，缺乏依据的授权分别为百分之零点一和百分之一点三。预先设定的重放检验未发现虚假限制显著影响行为；事后追加分析虽显示脆弱效应，但证据较弱。中和虚假授权后，留出测试中的未授权操作从一百二十次运行中的二十四次降至七次；另一组后续测试则从四十八次中的十七次降至十一次。多数开发阶段的虚假限制似乎源于智能体先前推断某操作需要审批。预先设定的提示测试显示，明确说明审批要求的来源后，开发摘要中的虚假限制率从百分之五降至百分之一点八，留出模板中从百分之一点八降至百分之零点三；而对台账确实要求审批的请求几乎没有变化。

Conclusion: 交接摘要中的虚假权限陈述值得审计，尤其是错误授权，因为它可能导致后续实例采取未授权行动。实验还表明，明确交代审批要求依据的提示可显著减少虚假限制，同时保留必要的审批请求。不过，关于虚假限制是否会改变行为的证据并不稳固，且研究使用合成任务，结论仍需在更广泛场景中验证。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents compact their context, replacing most history with a summary. We ask whether handoff summaries state permissions or requirements the record does not support, whether such sentences change what the next instance (the successor) does, and where they come from. Synthetic tool-use tasks carry a ground-truth permission ledger; same-snapshot replay reruns the successor with those whole sentences replaced by neutral filler. Two AI readers, reading only rule-flagged held-out summaries, confirmed unsupported restrictions in 1.2% and 2.4% of all readable summaries from two open-weight models, and unsupported authorizations in 0.1% and 1.3%. For restrictions, a pre-specified replay test was not significant (p = 0.58); a follow-up, planned after it, found a fragile effect (p = 0.027). Neutralizing false approvals cut unauthorized actions from 24 to 7 of 120 held-out runs (post hoc; later set: 17 to 11 of 48). Most development restrictions followed the agent's earlier reasoning that an action needs approval (post hoc). In pre-specified tests, replacing the system prompt's "Some actions need approval." with one saying where requirements come from cut unsupported restrictions from 5.0% to 1.8% of development summaries and, on held-out templates, from 1.8% to 0.3% (both p < 10^-9), while requests for approvals the ledger requires barely changed. Code and data: https://github.com/Chuw1/handoff-audit

</details>


### [51] [Replicability, Reproducibility, and Robustness in Political and Social Science Research with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06639)
*Joan C. Timoneda,Sebastián Vallejo Vera*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本章研究大语言模型输出波动对实证研究可复现性与统计结论的影响，并提出利用开放权重模型堆叠集成提升偏差校正精度的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的输出具有概率性，会随模型及其版本变化，进而影响下游统计分析。若将可复现性理解为每次都得到完全相同的输出，可能并不适用于此类研究，因此需要以结果分布为基础建立评估标准，并诊断模型误差对研究结论的影响。

Method: 本章区分可重复性、可复现性与稳健性，提出基于结果分布的概率性标准；制定标准化提示脚本，检验编码器和解码器模型的内部输出变异，并分析误差结构及不同模型的错误是否一致。研究使用三个数据集评估测量选择对下游回归的影响，并将开放权重模型堆叠集成用于设计型校正，以提高校正估计的精度。

Result: 实验显示，测量方式的选择可能使下游回归系数的符号发生反转。使用带有金标准标签的子样本进行既有设计型校正，可以恢复估计的有效性，但会降低估计精度。采用开放权重模型堆叠集成可提高校正步骤的精度，同时保持估计有效性。

Conclusion: 评估大语言模型研究的可复现性，应关注结果分布及其对实质性结论的影响，而非要求输出完全一致。通过误差诊断和堆叠集成，可以在保留校正有效性的同时改善精度，为使用大语言模型开展实证研究提供更可靠的方法。

Abstract: This chapter addresses the central reproducibility problem of research with large language models. LLM output is probabilistic and varies across models and versions, which can have significant effects on downstream statistical results. The chapter distinguishes between replicability, reproducibility, and robustness, and proposes a probabilistic standard based on the distribution of results, not identical outputs. It introduces a standardized prompt script, tests within-model variation for encoders and decoders, and develops error-structure analysis to diagnose where models err and whether their errors align. Using three datasets, the chapter shows that measurement choice can reverse the sign of downstream regression coefficients. Existing design-based corrections with gold-labeled subsamples restore validity, but come at the cost of estimate precision. The main contribution of the chapter is a stacked ensemble of open-weight models that improves precision in the correction step while retaining estimate validity.

</details>


### [52] [ALTO: African and Levantine Tech-Facilitated Gender-Based Violence Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06706)
*Tianwei Zhang,Ivy Njoki Nyagwencha,Zahraa Dirani,Shalini Joshi,Scott A. Hale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究与约旦、法语西非和肯尼亚的民间社会组织合作，构建涵盖黎凡特阿拉伯语、非洲法语和斯瓦希里语的性别暴力检测数据集，并发现现有模型表现不足，微调后有所改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络技术促成的性别暴力危害广泛，但现有检测模型和标注数据主要来自全球北方语言，难以识别其他地区的文化语境与语言表达，尤其容易漏掉隐蔽形式的伤害。

Method: 与当地民间社会组织合作，采用社区主导的众包方式收集三种语言中具有文化和语言相关性的案例，并通过主动学习进行标注，建立包含三千零二十二条数据的检测数据集；随后开展模型基准测试与微调实验。

Result: 现有有害内容检测模型在该数据集上的性别暴力召回率较低，微调后有所提升。各语言模型都经常漏检隐蔽伤害，其中斯瓦希里语的错误率明显更高。研究同时公开了标注分类体系、数据集和训练模型。

Conclusion: 性别暴力检测需要纳入当地社区知识，并针对不同语言和文化语境进行数据建设与模型适配。研究成果为改善三种语言的检测提供了资源，也揭示了隐蔽伤害识别及斯瓦希里语检测方面仍存在显著挑战。

Abstract: Tech-facilitated gender based violence (TFGBV) is a pervasive online harm affecting women and gender minority groups through its many direct and covert forms. Current models and annotated datasets for gendered harm detection are dominated by data from, and models trained in, Global North languages. In collaboration with civil society organizations (CSOs) from Jordan, Francophone West Africa, and Kenya, we developed a community-led approach to crowdsource culturally and linguistically relevant instances of TFGBV in Levantine Arabic, African French, and Swahili. We annotated the crowdsourced data using active learning to develop a small but performant dataset (N=3022) called ALTO to improve TFGBV detection in the three languages. Through benchmarking and fine-tuning experiments, we show that existing harmful content detection models show poor TFGBV recall within ALTO and improve with fine-tuning. We found that models often miss covert harms across all languages, while error rates are markedly higher in Swahili. We report the experimental results and release the annotation taxonomy, dataset, and trained models.

</details>


### [53] [Reasoning over Non-Parametric Memory in Multilingual LLMs: A Cognitive-Based Analysis of Relational Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06715)
*Zohreh Abbasi Liasi,Batoul Hammoud,Anas Madkoor,Ehsaneddin Asgari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，模型即使能利用提示中直接提供的关系事实，也未必能可靠地进行高阶推理；分析与评价任务尤其困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成依赖语言模型依据上下文中的证据进行关系推理，而非依赖预训练时记忆的知识。为单独检验这种能力，研究将事实直接提供在提示中，排除检索环节的影响。

Method: 研究构建了受布鲁姆认知层级启发的多语言诊断基准，包含基于三十种知识图谱关系生成的四百五十道选择题。题目覆盖记忆、理解、应用、分析和评价五个层级，以及事实、反事实和中和三种知识条件。研究在六种语言、两种多语言设置下评测了四十三个模型，共计二十二万六千八百次预测。

Result: 开放权重模型系列的准确率从应用层级到分析层级均显著下降，某大型模型组降幅达四十七点二个百分点，而两种受测的双子座模型没有出现这一现象。评价任务仍是瓶颈：双子座三点一专业版在其余四个层级平均准确率为百分之九十九点五，在评价层级仅为百分之六十点七。不同语言中的题目与关系难度排序相对稳定；反事实上下文通常比事实上下文更难，而在部分模型中，去除实体身份信息反而能提升高阶任务表现。

Conclusion: 模型能力增强并不必然带来对给定关系证据的可靠推理。该基准揭示了分析和评价层级的突出短板，也显示跨语言共享的难度结构，以及知识条件对表现的影响。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) assumes that language models can reason over relations stated in their context rather than fall back on associations memorized during pretraining. We study this evidence-conditioned relational reasoning in isolation, with the facts supplied directly in the prompt rather than retrieved. \textsc{Rel-Bloom} is a controlled multilingual diagnostic of 450 multiple-choice questions built from 30 Wikidata relations; the same source relations are instantiated at five Bloom-inspired levels (Remember, Understand, Apply, Analyze, Evaluate) and under three veracity conditions (Factual, Counterfactual, Neutralized). Across 43 models, six languages, and two multilingual settings (226,800 predictions), three findings stand out. First, accuracy drops sharply from Apply to Analyze in every evaluated open-weight model series, reaching 47.2 percentage points for the Large Granite bucket, but not for either Gemini model. Second, Evaluate items remain a bottleneck even for models near ceiling on the other task types: Gemini~3.1 Pro averages 99.5\% on the other four levels but 60.7\% on Evaluate. Third, item- and relation-level difficulty rankings are consistently preserved across languages, indicating a difficulty structure partly shared across languages within this benchmark. Across knowledge conditions, Counterfactual contexts are generally harder than Factual ones, while removing entity identities can improve higher-level performance in some model regimes. Greater model capability therefore does not by itself ensure reliable reasoning over supplied relational evidence. Relevant resources are available at: https://huggingface.co/spaces/QCRI/non-parametric-memory-relational-reasoning.

</details>


### [54] [Ranking Agreement Does Not Reveal Paragraph-Edit Agreement in Two Released Paper Acceptance Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06789)
*Shaoliang Yang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，模型对原论文的评分排序较一致，但无法可靠判断哪些改写能提高另一评估器的评分；在五选一任务中，预测器的表现仅略高于随机选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速的接受度分类器可能被用来筛选论文修改方案，交由较慢的评估器进一步评判。但这种用途是否成立，不能仅凭模型对现有论文评分的一致性来推断，还需检验它们能否识别真正能提高目标评估器评分的修改。

Method: 研究选取国际学习表征会议的198篇论文，比较一个基于词语与文档特征的分类器，以及两个由同一款30亿参数语言模型微调并公开发布的文本接受度预测器。研究者为每篇论文生成段落改写，并自动筛查改写是否保持事实、主张强度和归属不变，再比较各模型对原文及改写的评分与排序。预先设定的测试采用较低数值精度；研究者随后以完整32位精度重新评分，作为事后检验。

Result: 对原论文，分类器的排序与一个预测器高度接近，与另一个呈中等程度一致；但对于哪些改写会提高评分，分类器与两个预测器均未显示可检测的一致性。低精度下，舍入误差掩盖了幅度较小的评分变化；改用完整32位精度后，改写选择的一致性仍接近于零。每个预测器从五种改写中选择一种时，其平均原始得分仅比随机选择高不到100分制的2分。

Conclusion: 现有证据不支持仅凭模型对原论文的评分排序一致，就把分类器用于筛选能提升目标评估器评分的修改。结论目前仅适用于文本呈现层面的改写，以及同一模型家族中的两个公开预测器；能否推广到计算成本更高的评估器仍未验证。因此，修改筛选器应直接针对其预定服务的评估器，在实际要排序的修改方案上进行检验。

Abstract: A fast acceptance classifier could select revisions for a slower evaluator. We ask whether agreement on existing papers supports that use. We compare a classifier of word and document features with two released text-only acceptance predictors fine-tuned from the same language model with three billion parameters. For 198 papers from the International Conference on Learning Representations, we compare model rankings of the original papers and of paragraph rewrites screened automatically for unchanged facts, claim strength, and attribution. The classifier ranks original papers closely with one predictor and moderately with the other, but shows no detectable agreement with either on which rewrites raise scores. The tests specified in advance used reduced numerical precision. Rounding error dominated the small score responses. We therefore rescored the released weights in full 32-bit precision as a post hoc check; edit agreement remained near zero. At this precision, when each predictor chooses among five rewrites per paper, its average raw score is under two points out of 100 higher than with random choice. The evidence covers presentation rewrites and two open predictors from one family; transfer to costly evaluators remains untested. Revision selectors should therefore be checked against the intended evaluator on the revisions they rank.

</details>


### [55] [Measuring Risk, Not Harm: Detecting Psychological Harm Risk Factors in LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06806)
*Nil-Jana Akpinar,Eugenia Kim,Natalie Isak,Amanda Minnich,Victoria Westerhoff,Matthew Dressman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的心理安全监测方法：识别大语言模型回复中可观察的风险行为，并将其汇总用于长期监测，而不把风险行为等同于用户实际受到的心理伤害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户心理伤害难以直接监测，因为它可能在多轮互动中逐渐出现，且高度依赖个人情境。因此，研究转而测量模型回复中可观察、可能构成伤害风险因素的行为，为长期监测提供基础。

Method: 研究将八类风险行为操作化，构建包含四万七千零六十二条模型回复、三十七万三千八百七十三个人工标注风险因素标签的合成多标签语料，并以定向人工标注进行验证和评估。随后比较基于嵌入的分类器、直接由大语言模型判定，以及分类器与判定器级联三种方案；所有检测器均只分析模型输出，不依赖模型内部状态，并在多个分布外的人机对话数据集上测试。

Result: 在留出的人类标注数据上，表现最佳的级联方案宏平均精确率为零点八六、召回率为零点七六、调和平均值为零点七九。在野聊数据集上，该方案相较于直接判定减少百分之九十七点一的判定器调用；在妄想评估数据集中，风险因素检测结果呈现对话内聚集现象。

Conclusion: 基于回复级可观察行为的风险因素检测，可作为长期心理安全监测的可扩展测量层。该方法有助于追踪潜在风险，同时明确区分模型行为风险与尚未直接观测到的用户心理伤害。

Abstract: Psychological harms from interactions with large language models cannot be monitored directly because they may emerge over repeated interactions and depend strongly on subjective individual user context. We propose measuring risk, not harm: detecting observable AI behaviors that constitute risk factors for psychological harm at the AI response level and aggregating these signals to support longitudinal monitoring. We operationalize eight such behaviors and construct a synthetic multi-label corpus of 47,062 AI responses and 373,873 response-factor labels, with targeted human annotations for validation and evaluation. We then compare trained embedding-based classifiers, direct LLM judging, and a classifier--judge cascade. All detectors are model-agnostic and operate only on LLM outputs, requiring no access to internal model states. We apply these detectors to several out-of-distribution human-AI conversation data sets from prior research. On held-out human-labeled data, the best cascade achieves macro precision of 0.86, recall of 0.76, and F1 of 0.79. On WildChat, it reduces LLM-judge calls by 97.1\% relative to direct judging. On DelusionEval, risk-factor detections cluster within conversations. Together, our results show that response-level behavioral risk-factor detection can provide a scalable measurement layer for longitudinal psychological-safety monitoring without conflating observable AI behavior with latent user harm.

</details>


### [56] [LLMs Don't Know What They Know: Benchmarking Temporal Self-Knowledge for Financial Backtesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06808)
*Lingfei Qian,Yan Wang,Xueqing Peng,Yupeng Cao,Yuechen Jiang,Zhuohan Xie,Anke Xu,Wenbo Cao,Jiarui Chen,Zehan Li,Jimin Huang,Alejandro Lopez-Lira,Yuehua Tang,Sophia Ananiadou,Jian-Yun Nie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出金融时序知识基准，用行为测试检验大语言模型是否会在回测中泄露训练期内已知的未来信息。结果显示，自我报告、历史日期指令和供应商标注的知识截止时间都不足以证明回测无信息泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融回测只有在模型预测时并未预先知道结果，才具有可信度。实际使用中，人们常依赖供应商声明的知识截止时间、询问模型是否知情，或要求模型假装处于过去日期，但这些防护措施是否可靠尚未得到验证。

Method: 研究构建了金融时序知识基准，收录一九九九年至二〇二六年的二千二百二十六条带日期的美国事实，涵盖月度宏观经济数据、月末股价和美联储决策。每条事实设计四类提问：询问数值、要求模型在声称知晓或猜测的前提下作答，以及要求模型依据指定历史日期预测。研究还控制数据持续性和常见结果，使随机猜测的准确率约为百分之二十七，并比较模型作答准确性与其知识自我报告。

Result: 多数前沿模型低报了自身已掌握的知识。以第五代生成式预训练变换器为例，在受控事实上的答对率为百分之七十六，但仅有百分之三十八的事实被其声称为已知；对于自称不知道的事实，它仍有百分之六十一答对。模型的知晓声明还可能延续到实测知识边界之后，最长达七个季度。要求模型采用历史日期后，先前知晓的声明大幅减少，但准确率没有变化。

Conclusion: 训练窗口内的金融回测，不能仅凭模型自我报告、历史日期指令或声明的知识截止时间来认证为无信息泄漏。研究主张通过行为测试测量模型的时序知识边界，并以此选择回测期间；该基准及其测量流程可为这类评估提供工具。

Abstract: Financial backtests of large language models (LLMs) are meaningful only if the model did not already know the outcomes it is asked to predict. Practitioners guard against this by trusting a vendor's stated knowledge cutoff, by asking the model what it knows, or by instructing it to answer as of a past date. To verify whether any of these safeguards can be trusted, we introduce FinDendro, a benchmark of 2,226 dated U.S. facts from 1999 to 2026, covering monthly macroeconomic series, month-end stock prices and Federal Reserve decisions. Each fact yields four prompts: a value question, Recall and Predict questions that pair each candidate value with a claim of knowing or guessing without a declared date, and a Predict question with a historical declared date. Controls for persistence and dominant outcomes keep chance accuracy near 0.27. Results show that for most frontier models, self-reports understate knowledge. GPT-5 answers 76% of controlled facts correctly but claims to know 38%, and answers 61% of the facts it says it does not know. Knowledge claims can persist beyond the measured knowledge boundary, by up to seven quarters, and a declared date removes most of the claims of prior knowledge while leaving accuracy unchanged. Backtests inside a model's training window therefore cannot be certified leak-free by self-report, instruction or stated cutoff. These findings motivate behavioral measurement of temporal knowledge boundaries, and FinDendro provides a benchmark and procedure for selecting backtest periods based on demonstrated factual knowledge.

</details>


### [57] [SAUCE: Summary Analysis Using Conversation Entailment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06812)
*Man-Ling Sung,Hemanth Kandula,Jeff Ma,William Hartmann,Matthew Snover*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需参考摘要、基于事实核验的跨语言对话语音摘要评估流程，可解释地识别摘要中的事实错误与信息遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话语音摘要往往没有明确的任务目标，尤其在跨语言、转写与翻译可能出错的真实场景中，传统评估方法难以准确衡量摘要质量，也难以说明错误具体在哪里。黑箱评估器和稠密向量指标通常缺乏可核查的解释。

Method: 该方法通过判断对话内容与摘要文本之间的蕴含关系，评估摘要的事实准确性和事实覆盖度。它将评分对应到离散且可验证的事实，使使用者能够定位摘要中的幻觉和遗漏，并与多种常见摘要指标进行比较。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够捕捉转写及翻译错误造成的信息损失，并识别内容失真的摘要；初步研究显示，其评估结果与人类判断高度一致。摘要未提供具体数据或量化比较结果。

Conclusion: 基于事实蕴含的可解释评估有助于系统分析大语言模型的行为，也能为用户核验嘈杂真实环境中的摘要准确性提供可操作依据。

Abstract: With the growing need for evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) and their applications to speech, challenges persist in summarizing and evaluating conversations that lack a clear end goal. We introduce SAUCE -- a reference-free, fact-based evaluation pipeline for cross-lingual conversational speech summarization. It measures the accuracy and the fact coverage of a summary through the entailment between conversation and text. We compare SAUCE against several popular summarization metrics and demonstrate the effectiveness of capturing information loss due to transcription and translation error and identifying broken summaries. Crucially, unlike black-box LLM evaluators or dense embedding metrics, SAUCE is inherently explainable: it maps summary scores to discrete, verifiable facts, allowing users to pinpoint exact hallucinations or omissions. We illustrate how this interpretability helps developers systematically profile LLM behaviors and gives end-users an actionable tool to verify summary accuracy in noisy, real-world conditions. Preliminary investigations show SAUCE strongly align with human judgment.

</details>


### [58] [LooM: Loop-Invariant Memory for Causal Encoder-Decoder Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06835)
*Lele Yang,Muxi Diao,Zhonghao Yan,Jiankang Chen,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao,Wentao Zhang,Kongming Liang,Zhanyu Ma,Weiran Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出循环不变存储机制，使模型可通过重复执行解码层增加计算深度，同时保持缓存大小不变，并支持部署时灵活选择计算预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环式变换器通过重复使用共享层增加有效深度，但通常会使缓存随循环次数增长，计算预算只能按完整模块调整，而且训练后难以更改循环次数。论文旨在消除这些限制，使模型能在不同计算预算下灵活运行。

Method: 在因果式编码器—解码器架构中，只循环执行首层之后的解码层。这些层读取每个词元仅写入一次的全局存储，并且每层只在首次处理某个词元时发布其键值缓存条目，因此缓存不随循环次数增加。各解码层独立循环，使每个词元可对应三至二十一次层执行。训练时随机采样计算预算，使单一模型无需路由器即可适配这些预算。

Result: 在循环次数足够时，该方法相较于使用相同数据训练的单次前向基线，在语言建模、零样本准确率和长上下文困惑度方面表现更好，同时缓存大小相同。分析还发现，训练时采用的预算分布会影响额外深度带来的收益。

Conclusion: 因果式编码器—解码器适合采用循环结构：存储只需写入一次，模型深度则可作为部署时选择的计算预算。该方法在不增加缓存开销的前提下提供了更灵活的推理计算量。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase effective depth by re-executing shared layers, but three limitations hinder their adoption: memory grows with the loop count, compute can only be adjusted in whole-block steps, and the loop count is hard to change after training. We propose LooM (Loop-invariant Memory), which addresses each in turn. In a causal encoder-decoder (CED), LooM loops only the decoder layers after the first, which read a global memory written once per token, and each of them publishes the key-value (KV) entry for a token only on its first visit, so the cache does not grow with the loop count. These layers loop independently, providing every budget from 3 to 21 layer executions per token, and one model trained on randomly sampled budgets covers all of them without a router. With enough loops, LooM outperforms a single-pass baseline trained on the same data on language modeling, zero-shot accuracy and long-context perplexity, while using the same cache size. Our analysis further shows that the training budget distribution decides what extra depth buys. These results suggest that a CED is a natural place to loop: memory is written once, and depth becomes a budget chosen at deployment.

</details>


### [59] [Before You Continue: Evaluating Time-Sensitive Safety Awareness in Conversational Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06839)
*Peidi Dong,Yunze Xiao,Yu-Lin Tsai,Yichen Zheng,Heyuan Huang,Weihao Xuan,Mark Dredze,Jen-tse Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出对话式时间安全基准，发现模型即使能正确计算经过时间，也常未能据此调整建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在后续请求中可能不再重复时间相关限制，但这些限制仍会影响行动是否安全。研究旨在评估对话助手能否识别并应用此前约束，而不只是进行时间计算。

Method: 研究构建了对话式时间行动安全基准，包含由一百二十八个模板生成的三千四百一十八条输入，以及用于比较时间计算能力与约束应用能力的三十三个独立情境，并评测了十三种模型。

Result: 当建议行动违反规则时，模型仅在百分之三十点五的案例中考虑了时间约束，同时很少发出不必要的警告。更关键的是，即使正确计算出经过时间，模型仍有百分之六十二点一的案例未能应用相应约束。明确告知时间仅能解决约一半此类失败。

Conclusion: 当前模型的主要短板并非单纯的时间计算，而是将计算结果转化为对话建议中的安全约束。提升时间推理能力不足以解决问题，还需增强模型在多轮对话中识别、记忆并落实约束的能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) assistants increasingly help users with everyday activities that involve time-dependent constraints. However, users may not mention these constraints in later requests, requiring assistants to recognize and adjust their advice accordingly. To evaluate such capability, we introduce TASC (Temporal Action Safety in Conversation), a benchmark that evaluates whether LLMs apply time-dependent constraints when responding to conversational requests. TASC consists of 3,418 inputs derived from 128 templates, along with a separate set of 33 scenarios designed to compare models' ability to calculate elapsed time with their ability to apply that information. Across 13 models, assistants account for time-dependent constraints in only 30.5% of cases where the proposed action violates a rule, while rarely warning users unnecessarily. More importantly, models fail to apply these constraints in 62.1% of cases despite correctly calculating the elapsed time. Explicitly providing the timing resolves only about half of these failures, revealing a gap between temporal reasoning and its application in conversational advice.

</details>


### [60] [Persona Perspectives: Adopting Viewpoints Reorganizes Representations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06848)
*Austin C. Kozlowski,Avital Mintz,Madison Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 政治人格提示会系统性重塑大语言模型潜在空间中的语义关系，使未针对人格条件训练的安全监测器对真伪和道德判断产生政治倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针和稀疏自编码器等常用监测方法通常假设特征在不同语境中保持稳定。但大语言模型能够采用不同人格视角，这可能改变概念之间的关系，进而影响特征提取与安全监测的可靠性。研究旨在检验政治人格提示是否会改变模型内部表征的几何结构及其安全影响。

Method: 研究提取模型在自由派、保守派政治人格以及两种中性基线条件下，对九千多个常见词和三十三条语义轴的表征；比较词语在语义轴上的投影，以及语义轴彼此之间的对齐关系。随后，使用未经过人格条件训练的线性探针、基于稀疏自编码器的分类器，以及依据人工挑选词语表征构建的分类器，评估争议陈述的真实性与道德性。

Result: 政治人格提示会系统性改变词语在语义轴上的投影，也会重组语义轴之间的对齐方向，且变化与所指定的意识形态相符。应用于政治人格条件下的模型时，三类未针对人格训练的分类器给出的真伪和道德评价出现分歧，并呈现政治倾向。

Conclusion: 模型采用不同人格时，内部表征及其几何关系可能发生变化，因此在中性条件下提取或训练的特征监测器未必能可靠迁移到人格条件下。安全监测需要考虑人格对表征的影响，并检验监测方法在不同提示与观点条件下的稳健性。

Abstract: Common methods for monitoring features in large language models (LLMs) such as linear probes and sparse autoencoders (SAEs) assume that features maintain a consistent representation across contexts. LLMs, however, can flexibly adopt different perspectives or "personas" that suggest different underlying systems of conceptual relations. In this paper, we examine whether prompting an LLM to adopt a persona alters the representational geometry of the model's latent space. We extract representations of over 9,000 common words and 33 semantic axes from models adopting liberal and conservative political personas and compare these against two neutral baselines. We find that persona conditioning systematically alters both the projections of words onto semantic axes and the alignments among the axes themselves in directions consistent with the specified ideology. We then test the implications of this reorganization for two safety-relevant features: truthfulness and morality. Linear probes, SAE-based classifiers, and classifiers built from manually selected word representations, all trained without a persona, produce divergent results when applied to models conditioned on political personas, producing politically slanted ratings of truth and morality on controversial statements. These findings suggest that internal representations, as they are conventionally extracted, can exhibit different geometric relations when the model adopts personas, potentially undermining monitoring efforts.

</details>


### [61] [SpecCompact: Accelerating LLM Agent Context Compaction with Slot-Parallel Semantic Speculative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06876)
*Yulu An,Yifei Feng,Xiangyang Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种槽位并行语义推测框架，加速大语言模型智能体的交互历史压缩；实验显示压缩速度显著提升，但下游效果存在模型与任务相关的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体的交互历史不断增长，需要通过摘要压缩来管理上下文；然而，使用大型模型处理长历史会带来明显延迟。压缩既要尽量保留关键信息，也要控制耗时。作者观察到，结构化摘要槽位若独立生成，仍能保留信息，因此探索并行生成槽位以降低延迟。

Method: 该框架对小型起草模型进行微调，使其并行提出多个摘要槽位。目标模型逐行验证提议内容，并修复未通过验证的部分；后续起草则依据更新后的槽位前缀继续生成。此外，系统层面的优化减少了重复处理上下文和模型调用。

Result: 在应用世界与τ³基准、使用两种目标模型的实验中，该方法相较赫尔墨斯式摘要实现了2.04至5.63倍的压缩加速。单独的下游评估显示，性能取舍取决于模型和任务。在赫尔墨斯系统内开展的用户研究中，压缩时延中位数加速达8.68倍。

Conclusion: 槽位并行语义推测可显著加快交互历史压缩，并通过目标模型验证与修复来约束生成质量。不过，加速并不意味着下游效果必然提升；实际应用仍需根据模型和任务权衡压缩速度与信息保留及任务表现。

Abstract: LLM agents use context compaction to manage growing interaction histories, but summarizing long histories with large models can introduce substantial delays. Compaction therefore requires balancing information retention against latency. We observe that structured summary slots can retain information when generated independently. Based on this observation, we present SpecCompact, a framework that accelerates compaction through slot-parallel semantic speculation. SpecCompact fine-tunes a small drafter and generates slots in parallel. The target model verifies each proposed line and repairs rejected content, while subsequent drafting uses the updated slot prefix. System optimizations further reduce repeated context processing and model calls. Across AppWorld and $τ^3$ benchmarks with two target models, SpecCompact achieves $2.04$--$5.63\times$ compaction speedups over Hermes-style summarization. Separate downstream evaluations reveal model- and task-dependent performance trade-offs. A user study within Hermes further shows an $8.68\times$ median compaction speedup.

</details>

### [62] [Constraint-Preserving Flow Matching for Spectral Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05436)
*Anita Yang,Ken-ichi Kawarabayashi,Yohan Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受拉普拉斯结构约束的谱图生成方法，在生成过程中同时保持特征向量正交性、零空间约束和特征值正性，并支持按目标图属性引导生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱图生成通过图拉普拉斯矩阵的特征值与特征向量表示图，具有表示紧凑、变化连续的优点。但生成的特征系统必须符合拉普拉斯矩阵的结构。已有方法虽能保持特征向量正交，却未约束拉普拉斯矩阵的零空间，因此可能生成与图拉普拉斯结构不一致的结果。

Method: 提出拉普拉斯约束的斯蒂费尔流匹配方法，刻画同时满足拉普拉斯零空间条件的受约束斯蒂费尔流形，并在该流形上构建流匹配生成过程。生成时同时保持特征向量正交性、零空间条件与特征值正性；此外扩展该方法，使生成过程能够朝指定图属性引导。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在谱层面和图层面的评估指标上均取得更好的表现，并能使生成结果与目标属性更好地一致。

Conclusion: 将拉普拉斯零空间约束纳入谱图生成，有助于确保生成的特征系统符合图拉普拉斯结构。该方法兼顾结构有效性与属性引导，为受约束的谱图生成提供了新的方案。

Abstract: Spectral graph generation models graphs through the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of their Laplacians, providing a compact and continuous alternative to direct graph construction. However, these eigensystems must retain the structural properties of graph Laplacians. Existing approaches preserve eigenvector orthonormality but do not enforce the Laplacian nullspace constraint, potentially producing eigensystems inconsistent with Laplacian structure. To address this, we introduce Laplacian-Constrained Stiefel Flow Matching (LaSFM), which preserves the nullspace condition alongside eigenvector orthonormality and positive eigenvalues during generation. We characterize the underlying constrained Stiefel manifold and formulate flow matching on it. We further extend LaSFM to property-guided generation, steering samples toward desired graph properties while maintaining these constraints. Experiments demonstrate improved performance on both spectral and graph-level metrics, along with better alignment with target properties.

</details>


### [63] [Learning Implicit Solves for Dynamic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05439)
*Chawit Kaewnuratchadasorn,Jiaji Wang,Xin Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式神经算子求解器，将预训练神经算子嵌入纽马克贝塔时间积分，在无需动态轨迹监督的情况下实现准确、高效且可复用的动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真动力学模拟对科学研究和工程设计至关重要，但传统数值求解计算成本高。已有神经求解器往往依赖动态模拟数据训练，且在准确性、效率或跨条件复用方面存在限制。

Method: 先利用静态平衡解预训练神经算子，使其学习隐式平衡问题的求解过程；再将其嵌入纽马克贝塔积分。每个时间步由积分器构造等效平衡问题，神经算子预测下一时刻的解场，随后通过纽马克关系更新动力学状态。该方法支持动态条件变化，并可复用于不同空间和时间离散设置。

Result: 在线性与非线性弹性动力学算例中，该方法无需动态轨迹监督即可准确预测完整场变量；零样本评估的预测精度高于基于动态模拟训练的神经求解器基线。在非线性模拟中，相较顺序执行的经典有限元求解器，速度提升超过四千六百倍；此外还展示了稳定运行百万步的能力。

Conclusion: 该方法将静态预训练的神经算子与隐式时间积分结合，形成可复用的神经隐式求解器，在动力学建模中兼顾精度、效率与鲁棒性，并展现出跨条件及离散设置复用的潜力。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulation of dynamical systems is central to scientific discovery and engineering design, yet remains computationally demanding. Here we introduce the Implicit Neural Operator Solver (INOS), which learns the implicit solve and encodes a time integrator. INOS pretrains a neural operator from static equilibrium solutions and reuses it within Newmark-$β$ integration. At each time increment, the integrator forms an effective equilibrium problem, the pretrained neural operator infers the next-step solution field, and the Newmark relations advance the dynamic state. Numerical examples in linear and nonlinear elastodynamics demonstrate accurate full-field predictions without dynamic trajectory supervision. Comparative results show that INOS, evaluated zero-shot, achieves higher predictive accuracy than neural solver baselines trained on dynamic simulations. In addition, INOS delivers over 4,600$\times$ speedup in nonlinear simulations over sequential classical finite-element solvers. INOS natively accommodates changes in dynamic conditions and enables reuse of the pretrained operator across spatial and temporal discretizations. Further evaluation demonstrates a stable million-step rollout. These findings establish INOS as a reusable neural implicit solver for accurate, efficient, and robust dynamic modeling.

</details>


### [64] [Matched but Not Better: An Equivalence-Tested Benchmark of Quantum Machine Learning on Six Clinical Datasets and the QML-Health-READY Reporting Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05442)
*Mohammed Siraj B,Gousia Habib,Ruban S*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，经过正确调参的量子分类器在临床预测中接近但未超过经典模型；有限测量次数带来的估计误差是主要实际成本，配置失误则可能显著改变结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习虽被用于临床预测，但与经典模型的比较往往缺少配对评估，也较少检验两者是否等效。因此，研究旨在以严格、成对的实验设计，评估量子分类器相对于经典模型的预测表现、实际代价与报告规范。

Method: 研究在六个公开临床数据集上比较量子核支持向量机和变分量子分类器与五种经过调参的经典模型。所有模型使用相同的四维输入，并采用二十五组交叉验证划分。研究通过配对检验、经多重比较校正的等效性检验及随机效应汇总评估曲线下面积差异，并进一步分析消融、噪声、有限测量次数和实现故障。作者还提出涵盖八个领域的量子机器学习健康应用报告框架。

Result: 两种量子模型均未显著优于任何经典对照。汇总结果显示，量子核支持向量机相较于核函数匹配的径向基函数支持向量机，曲线下面积差异为负百分之零点六，且在正负零点零二的界限内等效；变分量子分类器相较最佳经典模型低百分之零点七。量子核支持向量机在六个数据集中有四个与径向基函数支持向量机在正负零点零五范围内等效，但在美国国家健康与营养调查二〇一七至二〇一八年数据上显著较差。无需纠缠的简单乘积态量子核表现不逊于纠缠量子核。每个核矩阵元素仅测量一千零二十四次时，量子核支持向量机的曲线下面积下降百分之五点四；未调节带宽则使表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 在正确配置下，量子分类器能够接近调优后的经典模型，但本研究未显示其具有预测优势。有限测量次数是主要实际负担，而门噪声影响相对次要；带宽等配置错误可能使性能大幅下降并导致错误判断。作者提出的报告框架旨在提升相关研究主张的可信度与可复现性。

Abstract: Background: Quantum machine learning (QML) is promoted for clinical prediction, but comparisons with classical models are rarely paired or tested for equivalence. Methods: We compared a quantum-kernel support vector machine (QSVM; 4 qubits, tuned bandwidth) and a variational quantum classifier (VQC) with five tuned classical models across six public clinical datasets. All models used identical four-component inputs and 25 cross-validation splits. Paired AUROC differences were assessed using a corrected resampled t-test, Holm-adjusted equivalence tests (margins: +/-0.02 and +/-0.05), and random-effects pooling. Ablation, noise, finite-shot, and implementation-fault analyses were conducted. We propose QML-Health-READY, an eight-domain reporting framework. Results: Neither quantum model significantly outperformed any classical comparator. Pooled across datasets, QSVM differed from the kernel-matched radial basis function (RBF) SVM by -0.006 AUROC (95% CI: -0.017 to +0.004), equivalent to +/-0.02. VQC differed from the best classical model by -0.007 (95% CI: -0.019 to +0.004). QSVM was equivalent to RBF SVM within +/-0.05 in four of six datasets but was significantly worse than the best classical model on NHANES 2017-18 (-0.076). A classically trivial product-state kernel performed as well as the entangling kernel. With depolarizing noise (p = 0.01) and 1,024 shots per kernel entry, QSVM AUROC decreased by 0.054 (95% CI: 0.036 to 0.071). Untuned bandwidth produced near-chance AUROC (0.48-0.59). Conclusions: Correctly configured quantum classifiers approached but did not exceed tuned classical models. Finite-shot estimation, rather than gate noise, was their main practical cost, while configuration errors could reverse conclusions. QML-Health-READY defines reporting requirements for credible claims.

</details>


### [65] [Objective-Conditioned Active Preference Learning for Multi-Objective Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05506)
*Duc Anh Vu,Thong Nguyen,Hoang Pham,Ponhvoan Srey,Luu Anh Tuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向多目标偏好对齐的主动学习方法，利用策略模型自身的隐式梯度挑选最有价值的标注任务，并发现部分偏好冲突能够提供有益训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时针对有用性、无害性和诚实性等多个目标对齐大语言模型，通常需要分别收集各目标的偏好判断，标注成本会随目标数量增加。现有方法要么缺少针对多目标的查询选择机制，要么将偏好冲突一概视为有害信息并从训练数据中剔除。

Method: 提出目标条件化梯度覆盖方法。该方法将每个“回答对—目标”组合视为一个独立查询，不依赖外部奖励模型，而是根据策略模型自身的隐式梯度评估查询的信息量，并选择能够最大程度扩展各目标梯度覆盖范围的查询。

Result: 在目标数量不同的实验设置中，在标注成本相同的条件下，该方法优于现有主动偏好学习方法。进一步分析显示，偏好冲突并非总是有害，其中具有信息量的冲突可以为策略训练提供有用信号。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过目标感知的梯度覆盖来选择偏好标注，可提升多目标对齐中的标注效率；同时，训练数据中的偏好冲突不应被一概排除，应评估其信息价值。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with several objectives, such as helpfulness, harmlessness, and honesty, typically relies on preference judgment for each objective, growing the annotation cost linearly with the number of objectives. Active preference learning can reduce this cost by selecting the comparisons whose labels are expected to change the policy most. Existing remedies either lack an objective-aware query selection mechanism for the multi-objective setting or regard conflicting preferences as harmful, therefore excluding them from the training data. We introduce OGC-DPO (Objective-conditioned Gradient Coverage DPO), an active learning framework that treats each (response pair, objective) combination as a separate query and measures its informativeness through the policy's own implicit gradient, without relying on an external reward model. OGC-DPO then selects the queries that most expand each objective's gradient coverage. Empirical experiments across varying numbers of objectives demonstrate the effectiveness of OGC-DPO over existing active preference learning methods under the same annotation cost. Further analysis demonstrates that informative conflicts, rather than being uniformly harmful, provide a useful policy training signal.

</details>


### [66] [TSFM Meets LLM: Context as Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05515)
*Hemanth Ram,Sai Shankar Narasimhan,Shubhankar Agarwal,Sandeep Chinchali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“上下文作为协变量”预测框架：让大语言模型将文本信息转化为数值预测与置信度特征，再由时间序列基础模型利用这些特征进行预测；在多类真实基准上，平均绝对误差最高改善百分之十二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实时间序列常伴随新闻、报告和注释等文本，这些信息包含仅凭数值历史难以推断的预测线索。大语言模型擅长理解文本但数值预测精度有限，时间序列基础模型则缺乏文本推理能力；同时，配对的文本与时间序列数据不足，阻碍了两类模型的协同。

Method: 首先使用大语言模型为真实时间序列生成文本标注，构建可扩展的合成多模态数据集。随后提出“上下文作为协变量”框架：由大语言模型把文本推理提炼为有数值依据的预测值和置信度特征，作为协变量输入时间序列基础模型。训练分为两阶段：先通过组相对策略优化微调大语言模型，并以协变量对预测性能的提升作为奖励；再使用该模型生成的协变量微调时间序列基础模型。

Result: 在金融、经济、天气、交通和安全等领域的真实基准测试中，基于合成多模态语料训练的模型能够迁移泛化。采用通义千问四十亿参数指令模型与Chronos2组合的方案，相较于先进的时间序列基础模型及大语言模型预测器（包括GPT-4o），平均绝对误差最高降低百分之十二。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将文本推理转化为可供时间序列模型使用的数值协变量，是结合大语言模型语义理解能力与时间序列基础模型数值预测能力的一种有效途径。可扩展的合成数据构建与分阶段对齐训练，有望缓解多模态配对数据不足的问题。

Abstract: Textual information is ubiquitous in real-world time series datasets, appearing as news, reports, and annotations that hold key predictive signals not deducible from numerical history alone. Large Language Models (LLMs) interpret text but lack numerical precision, while Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) are quantitatively accurate but cannot reason about the effects of text. Moreover, the lack of large-scale paired multimodal datasets limits progress in bridging this gap. In this work, we address this challenge by providing a scalable approach for generating realistic multimodal datasets by annotating real-world time series with LLMs. Building on this, we propose Context as Covariates (CoCo), a forecasting framework in which an LLM distills its textual reasoning into numerically grounded forecast and confidence covariates that guide a TSFM backbone, thereby exploiting the strengths of both LLMs and TSFMs. We align the two models in stages, first fine-tuning the LLM via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a reward tied to covariate-induced improvement in forecasting performance, and then fine-tuning the TSFM on covariates generated by the aligned LLM. Our experiments show that models trained on our synthetic multimodal corpus generalize to real benchmarks spanning finance, economics, weather, traffic, and security. Notably, CoCo with Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 as the LLM and Chronos2 as the TSFM achieves up to 12% MAE improvement over state-of-the-art TSFMs and LLM forecasters, such as GPT-4o. Code, checkpoints, and the evaluation datasets are publicly available.

</details>


### [67] [The Confidence Score: a New Measure on Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05528)
*Elizabeth Hofer,Martin v. Mohrenschildt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“置信度分数”这一核度量，允许保留类内结构的核获得较高评价；实验显示，它比若干已有核度量更能反映分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多核度量（如与理想核对齐）倾向于奖励将同类样本彼此视为相似的核。然而，分类任务中的类别可能包含显著的内部结构，因此要求同类样本高度相似过于严格，也可能低估适用的核。

Method: 提出“置信度分数”，通过衡量真实标签的一种编码与该标签经核投影后的表示之间的对齐程度来评价核。与理想核对齐类方法不同，该方法允许核保留类内结构，而不必将同类样本统一压缩为相似表示。

Result: 在真实世界和合成数据集上，针对多种核的实验表明，相较于若干其他核度量，置信度分数与分类准确率具有更高的相关性。

Conclusion: 置信度分数为评估核的分类适用性提供了另一种思路，能够更好地考虑类别内部结构，并在所测试的数据集和核上更有效地预测分类表现。

Abstract: Across a wide range of applications, kernel measures are used to tune hyperparameters, select among candidate kernels, and assess the suitability of kernels for specific learning tasks. For classification, kernel measures are used to predict which kernels may produce accurate kernel classifiers. Many existing kernel measures, such as alignment to the ideal kernel, are designed to reward kernels which captures all samples from the same class as similar. While effective in many settings, this requirement is unnecessarily restrictive for classification, where classes may exhibit substantial internal structure. In this work, the "Confidence Score", a new kernel measure, is proposed. Unlike alignment-to-the-ideal kernel measures, the Confidence Score measures the alignment between an encoding of the ground truth and that ground truth projected by the kernel, allowing kernels that preserve intra-class structure to achieve high scores. Through experiments, it is demonstrated that the Confidence Score exhibits a higher correlation with classification accuracy across a variety of kernels on both real-world and synthetic datasets, compared to select other kernel measures.

</details>


### [68] [Recasting Visual Language Models as EEG Foundation Models for Clinical Neurodiagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05538)
*Xin Zhou,Elena Sergeeva,Nan Bi,Wenyu Song,Lijun Yin,Hao Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，通用视觉语言模型即使没有大规模脑电图预训练，也能有效处理多种临床脑电图诊断任务，并在少样本适应和模型扩展方面展现优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动脑电图分析依赖针对特定任务、使用人工标注数据训练的模型；脑电图基础模型虽尝试以未标注信号预训练统一表示，但其效果受限于特定模态数据的可用性。与此同时，通用视觉语言模型具备较强的少样本学习和任务适应能力，因此研究者探讨它们能否通过视觉方式读取并解释脑电图，近似临床医生观察脑电模式的过程。

Method: 作者评估了未经过大规模脑电图预训练的通用视觉语言模型在一组临床神经诊断任务中的表现，并将其与专用基础模型进行比较。评估重点包括跨任务表现、少样本学习中的任务适应能力，以及模型规模扩大后性能的变化。

Result: 摘要称，通用视觉语言模型在多项临床神经诊断任务中表现具有竞争力；在少样本场景下，其对不同任务的适应能力优于专用基础模型。此外，随着模型规模扩大，其性能能够提升，而专用模态模型未表现出同样的扩展趋势。摘要未提供具体数据集、指标或数值结果。

Conclusion: 通用视觉语言模型有望成为脑电图自动分析的一条补充路径：它们可利用视觉理解能力处理脑电图任务，并凭借少样本适应和规模扩展能力减少对大规模脑电图专属预训练的依赖。不过，摘要提供的是初步论证，具体临床可靠性与适用范围仍需结合完整实验和临床验证判断。

Abstract: Historically automated electroencephalography (EEG) systems was done using task-specific supervised models trained on purposely annotated data. A more recent modeling paradigm relies on creating a unified representation, or a 'foundation model', for multiple modality-specific tasks through large-scale domain-specific pretraining on the unannotated EEG signal. The quality of this unified representation directly depends on the availability of the modality-specific datasets. At the same time, generalist Vision Language Models (VLMs) exhibit broad few-shot learning capacities as well as good fine-tuning adaptability to different visual and language tasks. Can VLMs learn how to read and interpret EEG visually, emulating to a degree the way human clinicians inspect the EEG patterns? In this work we make a case that they can: we show that generalist VLMs without large-scale EEG pretraining perform competitively across a collection of clinical neuro-diagnostic tasks, can adapt to different tasks in a few-shot learning setting better than specialist foundation models, and exhibit better performance with model scaling when modality-specific models fail to exhibit.

</details>


### [69] [FedSharp: Byzantine Robust Decentralized Federated Learning Under Noisy Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05546)
*Ojaswi Sinha,Amin Farajzadeh,Animesh Yadav,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向去中心化联邦学习的鲁棒聚合框架，通过筛查可疑模型、利用多候选模型平均降低信道噪声，并结合噪声自适应正则化与近端训练，应对拜占庭攻击、噪声累积和数据异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习虽不依赖中心聚合服务器，但仍易受非独立同分布数据、拜占庭节点及信道噪声影响。模型在节点间传播时，噪声会逐步累积，并在学习率衰减后变得更加显著；攻击者还可利用累积噪声，伪装成诚实模型的平均结果，从而欺骗基于损失的模型选择。

Method: 所提框架让每个节点保存其前方二乘以拜占庭节点上限数量的前序节点模型，以确保在攻击节点数量不超过上限时至少有一半候选模型诚实。框架先根据各坐标相对于中位数的偏差筛除可疑模型，再对损失最低的若干候选模型取平均，使新产生的信道噪声方差降低至原来的相应候选数量分之一。此外，框架采用噪声自适应的梯度范数正则化和近端训练，以减轻累积噪声及非独立同分布数据造成的模型漂移。

Result: 在包含一百个节点、其中三十三个为拜占庭节点的环形网络实验中，该方法在手写数字数据集上的准确率为百分之九十三点五至百分之九十四点九，明显高于单模型选择基准的百分之二十九点四至百分之三十八点一；在彩色图像数据集上达到百分之三十二点八至百分之四十八点八，高于基准的百分之十至百分之三十点五。优势涵盖高斯攻击和隐蔽攻击，以及独立同分布与非独立同分布数据设置。手写数字实验中，其准确率也与无信道噪声基准相差约一个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合候选模型筛查、多模型平均和噪声及数据异质性适配训练，可以显著增强去中心化联邦学习抵御拜占庭攻击与信道噪声的能力，并在所测试场景中大幅优于单模型选择基准。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) eliminates the central aggregation server but remains vulnerable to data heterogeneity, Byzantine attacks, and channel noise. We show that noise accumulates as models propagate across nodes, becoming increasingly significant as the learning rate decays. Moreover, accumulated noise enables Byzantine attackers to exploit loss-based model selection by mimicking honest model averages. To address these challenges, we propose screened, hop-aware, ranked, proximal averaging, a robust DFL framework (FedSharp). In this approach, each node maintains models from its 2b predecessors, ensuring at least b honest candidates when at most b nodes are Byzantine. FedSharp screens suspicious models using coordinate-wise median deviations, averages the b lowest-loss candidates to reduce fresh channel-noise variance by a factor of b, and applies noise-adaptive gradient-norm regularization and proximal training to mitigate accumulated noise and non-IID drift. Experiments on a 100-node ring with 33 Byzantine nodes show that FedSharp achieves 93.5-94.9% accuracy on MNIST, compared with 29.4-38.1% for a state-of-the-art single-model-selection benchmark, and 32.8-48.8% on CIFAR-10, compared with 10.0-30.5%, across Gaussian and hidden attacks under IID and non-IID data. On MNIST, FedSharp also achieves accuracy within approximately one percentage point of the benchmark operating without channel noise.

</details>


### [70] [Support Vector Machines Directly Minimise KL Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05557)
*Ivan Silajev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明，支持向量分类与支持向量回归的对偶目标，都可精确表述为两个高斯概率测度之间受约束的库尔贝克—莱布勒散度最小化；放宽约束并采用平方范数正则化后，同一框架还可得到核岭回归的目标函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支持向量机主要以几何间隔最大化来解释，缺乏主流的概率解释，因此难以与概率机器学习模型在统一理论框架下比较或组合。论文旨在弥合这一理论隔阂，并为统一理解核方法提供信息论视角。

Method: 作者建立支持向量分类和支持向量回归对偶目标与高斯概率测度间相对熵最小化问题的精确对应关系，并进一步分析移除定义域约束、采用平方范数正则化时该散度形式的变化，以推导核岭回归的目标函数。

Result: 研究表明，支持向量分类和支持向量回归的对偶目标，分别等价于特定约束下两个高斯概率测度之间的库尔贝克—莱布勒散度最小化；在放宽约束并施加平方范数正则化后，该框架可精确恢复核岭回归的目标函数。

Conclusion: 论文为标准核机器提供了统一的信息论解释：其优化目标可直接理解为相对熵最小化。这一结果有望推动更多基于核的算法纳入概率理论框架，但摘要未说明该统一视角是否带来实际性能提升。

Abstract: Support vector machines (SVMs) are fundamentally grounded in geometric margin maximisation and currently lack a mainstream probabilistic interpretation. This keeps them theoretically isolated from probabilistic machine learning models that are naturally comparable and combinable. In this paper, we bridge this gap by proving that the dual objectives of both support vector classification and support vector regression are exact, constrained Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence minimisation tasks between two Gaussian measures. We further show that this same divergence formulation recovers the exact objective function for kernel ridge regression when domain constraints are removed and $L^2$ regularisation is applied. By demonstrating that SVM duals directly minimise relative entropy, our results provide a unified information-theoretic model for standard kernel machines and lay the foundation for unifying broader kernel-based algorithms under probability theory.

</details>


### [71] [Readable Is Not Steerable: Pre-Registered Tests of Physical Structure in Learned Fluid Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05577)
*Manvendra Modgil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，自监督世界模型的潜变量并不会自动围绕物理量组织；潜变量中包含的信息，也不等于能够通过该潜变量有效控制输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督世界模型常被认为能够在潜在表示中形成与物理量相对应的结构，但这种说法需要通过预先登记、独立留出数据的实验检验。研究进一步关注两个不同问题：物理信息是否存在于潜变量中，以及是否能沿特定潜在方向稳定地控制生成结果。

Method: 研究针对二维衰减纳维—斯托克斯流开展三项预先登记实验，并各自在留出数据上检验。第一项比较预测流场的联合嵌入预测模型、未训练同构网络、非物理线性动力学模型和静态不变性模型。第二项分析普通自编码器及加入方差与协方差正则的自编码器，考察潜变量与涡量、涡量平方积分等流体量的关系及其线性可解码性。第三项通过分路结构构建可操控潜在方向，并以无标签规则寻找控制整体尺度和频谱形状的“旋钮”，同时检验潜变量信息与实际操控效果是否一致。

Result: 第一项中，预测流场的模型未能达到预先登记的标准，无法证明其潜变量比未训练网络更好地组织流体量；非物理动力学模型和静态模型表现相当或更好。第二项中，普通自编码器的最慢变化潜在方向突出编码了涡量平方积分，相关性约为零点八零至零点八六，优于未训练模型的零点三八；加入方差与协方差正则后，这种突出程度在各项比较中均下降，但对涡量的下降未达到预设幅度，同时线性可解码性有所提高。第三项中，八个模型均找到整体响度旋钮；五个新随机种子中有三个找到频谱形状旋钮，未达到预设的四个标准，且两类对照模型均未找到形状旋钮。探索实验还发现，几乎完美读取频谱形状的监督方向，在操控时几乎不改变输出；整体尺度旋钮必须依据输出效果而非数据方差来识别。

Conclusion: 结果不支持“自监督训练必然使潜变量围绕物理量组织”的普遍主张。普通自编码器可以在特定方向上显现物理量，但正则化可能改变这种结构；而信息可读性与可操控性是不同性质，不能相互替代。研究提供了受控且可复现的初步证据，同时其结论限于二维衰减流及所测试的模型和任务。

Abstract: Self-supervised world models are often said to organize their latents around physical quantities. We test this on 2D decaying Navier-Stokes flow in three pre-registered studies, each tested once on held-out data. In Study 1, a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) trained to predict the flow fails the pre-registered criteria for organizing its latent around fluid quantities better than an untrained network of the same architecture (not supported); a JEPA trained on non-physical linear dynamics and a static invariance model that never sees time do as well or better. In Study 2, plain autoencoders that render the field make enstrophy prominent in their slowest unsupervised latent directions (best r 0.80-0.86 vs 0.38 untrained; fresh seeds, sealed data). Adding VICReg lowers this prominence in every comparison, though not by the pre-registered margin for vorticity, while raising the autoencoders' linear decodability (partially supported). In Study 3, we build steerable directions ("dials") by routing: the decoder gets overall scale only through a small conditioning code, while its other pathway carries a degraded copy of spectral shape. Label-free rules find a loudness dial in all 8 trained models and a shape dial in 3 of 5 fresh seeds on sealed data, against a pre-registered bar of 4; neither control shows a shape dial (partially supported). Across the studies, what a latent contains and what can be controlled through it come apart. In exploration, a supervised direction reading spectral shape almost perfectly moved nothing when steered. During the build, the loudness dial had to be found by its effect on the output (closest to a pure rescaling), not by data variance; for shape, a variance rule and an effect-constrained rule each worked in 3 of 5 seeds. The program used about 70 logged hours on one consumer GPU; all pre-registrations, deviations, code and outputs are public.

</details>


### [72] [Data-Efficient Scaling-Law Discovery for Recommendation Systems via Coupled Hyperparameter Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05643)
*John Martabano Landy,Louis C. Tiao,Yao Xuan,Luc Kien Hang,Rupinder P. Khandpur,Arvind M. Vepa,Srinidhi Raghavendran,Anirudh Agrawal,Xiaoyi Leo Liu,Jiang Liu,Peggy Yao,Alireza Vahdatpour,Djordje Gligorijevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一套自动化方法，用少量实验发现异构排序模型的计算量—效果关系，并据此指导算力分配；在广告排序与检索系统中取得了离线和线上收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模排序与推荐模型和大型语言模型一样，通常能从更多计算资源中获益，且效果随规模变化大致呈幂律关系。但排序模型的架构差异较大，难以直接套用统一的扩展规律；逐一穷举架构配置又耗费大量实验资源。因此，论文希望高效发现任意排序模型的扩展规律，并衡量相关方法在整个模型群中的改进。

Method: 论文设计了“扩展层”，将基础架构参数归纳为语义一致的参数组，并规定这些参数如何协同变化，从而把低维超参数映射到完整架构空间。在此基础上，系统利用扩展规律构建代理模型，通过基于后验分布的采集准则选择有信息量的实验配置，并生成帕累托前沿；随后对前沿上的各模型运行点进行验证，避免全面穷举。论文还提出群体级评估流程：在固定的代表性模型集合和一致的试验预算下评估改进。

Result: 在两个开发周期中，该流程应用于某大型互联网平台广告排序与检索系统的34次模型迭代，平均带来0.66%的离线归一化熵改善，以及0.36%的累计线上提升。摘要未提供对照方法、实验波动或统计显著性等细节。

Conclusion: 该工作将模型架构参数化、扩展规律建模、不确定性感知的实验选择和群体级评估整合为自动化流程，可在异构排序模型生态中以较少实验学习模型特定的复杂度—效果关系，并为算力分配提供依据。

Abstract: Large-scale ranking and recommendation models of Web platforms benefit from increased compute much as large language models do, and their scaling behavior is broadly power-law in form. Yet ranking models have heterogeneous architectures which makes their scaling law less straightforward than that of LLMs. We address how to discover the scaling law of an arbitrary ranking model efficiently. We introduce a scaling layer, which groups primitive architecture parameters into semantically coherent groups and defines how they co-vary, inducing a mapping from a low-dimensional space of hyper-hyperparameters into the full architecture space. On top of it, Axia fits scaling-informed surrogates, selects configurations by a posterior-based acquisition criterion, and returns a Pareto frontier from which per-model operating points are validated -- replacing exhaustive sweeps with a small number of informative runs. A fleet-level evaluation protocol, computed on a fixed representative model set under matched trial budgets, makes refinements to either component measurable. Our contribution is an automated system that learns model-specific complexity-quality relationships and gives uncertainty-aware guidance for allocating compute across a heterogeneous model ecosystem. Across 34 model iterations in two development cycles on Meta's ads ranking and retrieval system, the workflow yielded a mean 0.66% offline normalized-entropy improvement and 0.36% cumulative online lift.

</details>


### [73] [Exploring the Privacy--Size Tradeoff for Differentially Private Self-Attentive Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05582)
*Rohit Sadavarte,Dhruvi Kothari,Sparsh Gupta,Eric Van Oeveren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种结合自注意力、变分自编码器与差分隐私训练的表格合成数据模型，并发现增加训练数据通常能提升下游任务效用；两种边际分布机制在数据量增加时更能抵消严格隐私预算带来的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据共享有助于数据发挥价值，但有用数据往往受隐私义务限制。差分隐私合成数据可在一定程度上兼顾共享与隐私，但其效用受隐私预算和训练数据量共同影响，因此需要系统研究二者对合成数据质量的影响，并探索更适用于表格数据的生成模型。

Method: 提出一种面向表格数据的自注意力变分自编码器，使用差分隐私随机梯度下降训练。研究在两个公开数据集上，将该模型与两种边际分布机制进行比较，同时改变隐私预算和数据集规模，并以分类器使用合成数据相较于真实数据时的效用损失作为评估指标。此外，研究采用两种独立的成员推断攻击，并以实测的零假设基线进行判断。

Result: 在相同差分隐私保证下，增加训练数据通常能提高效用。对两种边际分布机制而言，增加数据量可以抵消严格隐私预算造成的大部分效用损失；基于噪声缩放的分析则表明，差分隐私随机梯度下降以数据量换取隐私预算的效率较低。两种成员推断攻击均未发现总体层面的成员信息泄露信号。

Conclusion: 合成数据的效用不仅取决于隐私预算，也显著受可用训练数据量影响。论文提出的模型将注意力机制、变分自编码器与差分隐私结合用于表格数据，但摘要所述结果也提示，不同隐私机制在利用额外数据改善效用方面存在效率差异；成员推断实验未检测到总体成员信号，但这不等同于证明不存在任何隐私风险。

Abstract: Data tends to be most useful when it is shared, and the data most worth sharing is often the data that privacy obligations restrict.
