Synthetic data from a generator trained under differential privacy (DP) is one way to reconcile the two, but its utility depends on the privacy budget $\varepsilon$ and on how much data the generator sees - a parameter that most data sharing applications of synthetic data control. We present SAVANT, a self-attentive variational autoencoder for tabular data trained with DP-SGD that, to our knowledge, is the first to combine attention, a VAE, and DP in one tabular model. We evaluate it against two marginal mechanisms, PrivBayes and MST, jointly over the privacy budget and the dataset size on two public datasets, measuring the utility a downstream classifier loses by training on synthetic rather than real data. We find that, under the same DP guarantee, more data generally means more utility. For the marginal mechanisms it absorbs most of what a strict budget costs, and a noise-scaling argument suggests DP-SGD exchanges data fo budget at a worse rate. Two independent membership-inference attacks, judged against a measured null, found no aggregate membership signal.

</details>


### [74] [C-GRPO: Conformal Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05591)
*Arya Fayyazi,Seyedarmin Azizi,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种经校准的组相对策略优化方法，通过按任务难度动态决定生成次数，在维持准确率的同时减少训练计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型训练常需对每个问题进行多次策略采样。传统方法为所有问题固定采样组大小，导致简单问题耗费过多计算，困难问题却探索不足。已有动态预算方法虽能提高效率，但停止规则多依赖启发式判断，缺乏有限样本下的校准保证。

Method: 该方法以任务相关的非一致性分数取代固定采样预算，并利用保形校准阈值决定何时停止采样。对于可直接判定答案的推理任务，采用类似自适应预测集的校准方式；对于依赖执行结果评估的代码任务，使用首次成功分数。方法还会自动选择误覆盖水平，并定期重新校准。论文证明，该停止规则具有有限样本覆盖保证；在策略单调改进的假设下，重新校准可使期望训练预算不增加。

Result: 在涵盖数学、研究生层次科学和代码生成的六个基准上，相较固定预算的组相对策略优化，训练采样次数平均减少百分之四十九至百分之七十，同时保持较强的固定预算准确率。该方法在不同数据集上的单次采样通过率优于所比较的静态与动态基线；迁移至其他模型系列时，采样次数最多可减少百分之八十二。

Conclusion: 研究将保形校准引入训练时的动态采样预算控制，为停止规则提供有限样本保证，并在多类任务和模型上展现显著的计算节省潜力。其效果仍以论文设定的校准方式及策略单调改进假设为背景，实际应用中需通过定期重新校准适应训练分布的变化。

Abstract: Reasoning-oriented language model training increasingly relies on multiple on-policy rollouts, but standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) uses a fixed group size, wasting compute on easy prompts and under-exploring hard ones. Existing adaptive-budget methods alleviate this inefficiency, yet their stopping rules are heuristic and do not provide a finite-sample calibration guarantee. We propose CGRPO, which replaces the fixed rollout budget of standard GPRO with calibrated conformal thresholds over task-specific nonconformity scores, combining Adaptive Prediction Set (APS)-style calibration for closed-form reasoning, a first-success score for execution-based code, automatic miscoverage selection, and periodic re-calibration. We prove finite-sample coverage for the resulting stopping rule and show that, under monotone policy improvement, recalibration induces a non-increasing expected training budget. Across six benchmarks spanning mathematics, graduate-level science, and code generation, CGRPO reduces mean training rollouts by 49-70% relative to fixed-budget GRPO while retaining strong fixed-budget accuracy, achieves the strongest pass@1 among evaluated static and dynamic baselines on different datasets, and transfers across model families with up to 82% rollout savings.

</details>


### [75] [Relevance Propagation as Entropic Optimal Transport on Filippov Neural Networks: Sinkhorn Propagation and Region Relevance in Image Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05594)
*Olivier Gibaru,Eric Nyiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将层级相关性传播从启发式方法重新解释为一个变分问题的解，并据此统一多种传播规则、提出新规则；实验显示相关性守恒成立，且不同规则在解释特性上存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关性传播常被视为经验性启发式规则，缺少统一的数学基础，也难以解释不同传播规则为何成立、彼此有何关系。论文旨在建立严格的理论框架，说明如何将模型输出以守恒方式分配到网络中的计算节点。

Method: 作者把前馈网络形式化为由菲利波夫算子构成的有向无环图，并用广义梯度处理分段光滑或不连续结构。将相关性传播定义为活跃计算图上的无散流：在满足守恒条件的流中，选择相对于前向激活所诱导的反向转移规律、相对熵最小的解。通过改变参考流，统一导出零规则、正向规则和αβ规则，并推广为权重可随节点变化的广义规则。作者还提出逐边计算熵的辛克霍恩相关性传播；此外，将反向传播解释为拉格朗日松弛问题的伴随方程。

Result: 在整流线性单元和最大池化网络上，零规则等价于梯度乘输入；在折点处，它会出现显式跳变，而正向规则保持连续。在视觉几何组网络上的实验中，守恒恒等式精确到浮点舍入误差。不同规则在相关性图的正负号、分配给偏置节点的输出比例、忠实度、定位能力和类别依赖性方面表现不同。在另一种卷积网络上，广义规则能够守恒输出，其赫布式节点权重在所比较规则中取得最高的扰动评估得分。

Conclusion: 论文为层级相关性传播提供了统一的变分与概率解释，使多种既有规则成为同一框架下的不同选择，并拓展到不连续网络及新的传播方法。对非负参考流进行区域聚合，还能精确追踪哪些隐藏单元承载证据；条件化后的流可解释为薛定谔桥。结果支持该框架的守恒性质，同时表明规则选择会影响解释的具体特征。

Abstract: Relevance propagation, which redistributes a network's prediction backward through the layers, is usually treated as a heuristic. We show that it is the solution of a single variational problem: Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) is the divergence-free flow on the active computational graph of zero relative entropy to the backward transition law of the forward activation flow; for a non-negative reference, this is a relative entropy between Markov measures on backward paths. The rules LRP$_0$, LRP$_{z^+}$ and LRP$_{α,β}$ follow from this principle by the choice of a reference, and are instances of one rule, LRPN$_{α,β}$, whose weights may vary from node to node, for instance with the node's activity, in the spirit of Hebb's principle. The principle rests on a measure-theoretic formalization of feed-forward networks as directed acyclic graphs of Filippov operators, which covers piecewise-smooth and discontinuous architectures (e.g. hard-routed Mixture-of-Experts) with a Clarke-Filippov generalized-gradient calculus; it also characterizes backpropagation as the adjoint equation of a Lagrangian relaxation. On networks of ReLU and max-pooling nodes, LRP$_0$ is Gradient$\times$Input, with an explicit jump across a kink where LRP$_{z^+}$ stays continuous. Taking the entropy edge by edge yields a new rule, Sinkhorn Relevance Propagation. Aggregated over an input region, the flow of a non-negative reference tells, with exact accounting, which hidden units carry its evidence, the conditioned flow being a Schrödinger bridge. On VGG-16, the conservation identities hold to round-off, and the rules are told apart by the sign of their maps, the share of the output left to the bias nodes, faithfulness, localization and class dependence; on ConvNeXt-Tiny, LRPN$_{α,β}$ conserves the output and its Hebbian weights give the highest ABPC of the rules tested.

</details>


### [76] [RIDGE: State-Conditioned Reward Blending for Behavioral Coverage in Deep RL Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05601)
*AL Muqshith Mohammed Shifan,Kevin Christopher Chua,Ali Neshati,Loutfouz Zaman,Cristiano Politowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种单智能体架构，通过依赖状态的奖励混合与按角色分设的价值评估，使智能体能在同一局游戏中灵活切换行为风格；初步实验显示其可兼顾固定风格基线无法共同掌握的任务分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定角色奖励会使强化学习智能体的行为僵化：探索型智能体可能在饥饿时仍持续探索，生存型智能体则可能在空地图上囤积资源。若为每种风格分别训练智能体，自动化测试成本随风格数量增长；若在游戏过程中直接改变奖励，奖励非平稳又可能导致近端策略优化性能退化。论文旨在同时避免这两类问题。

Method: 提出单智能体架构 RIDGE，采用两项设计：一是状态条件化的奖励混合器，根据变化较慢的状态变量计算经归一化的权重，并据此组合不同角色的奖励；二是多头价值评估器，为每种角色分别训练一个价值头，使各价值头只学习自身稳定的奖励信号。这样无需直接以非平稳的混合信号训练单一价值头。

Result: 在克拉夫特环境的四种角色设置中，一个 RIDGE 智能体同时学会了木材等级相关的制作与战斗两条任务分支，而固定角色基线未能共同掌握这两条分支。权重尖锐度消融实验也表明，收益主要来自状态条件化。各条件仅使用三个随机种子，因此结果属于对混合机制动态的初步展示。

Conclusion: 研究表明，状态条件化的奖励混合结合分角色价值评估，可能使单个智能体在不同情境下表现出多种风格，并缓解奖励切换带来的训练不稳定。不过，现有证据规模有限；更大范围的多随机种子实验仍是后续工作。

Abstract: A deep RL agent trained on a fixed persona reward plays one way: an Explorer keeps exploring while starving, a Survivor hoards on an empty map. Since each persona fixes its own reward, automated playtesting trains N agents for N styles. Changing reward composition mid-episode runs into the switching-stability dilemma: PPO degrades under non-stationary rewards. We present RIDGE, a single-agent architecture that sidesteps the dilemma with two design choices: a state-conditioned blender that computes softmax-normalized sigmoid weights from slow-moving state variables, and a multi-head critic that trains one value head per persona on its own stationary signal. On Crafter with four personas, one \ridge agent acquires both branches of the wood-tier crafting/combat split that no fixed-persona baseline solves jointly, and a sharpness ablation confirms state-conditioning drives the gain. We position these results as demonstrations of the blending mechanism's dynamics across n=3 seeds per condition; larger multi-seed sweeps remain future work.

</details>


### [77] [Transport along Local Annealing Paths: Wasserstein Geometry and Multimodal Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05605)
*Han Chen,Sifan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，几何退火路径可能在瓦瑟斯坦空间中绕远路并造成优化变慢，因此提出按混合分量分别退火和学习传输映射的方法，以改善多峰分布的采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用于多峰采样的变分传输学习容易发生模式坍塌。沿预设中间分布进行几何退火虽可缓解这一问题，但当概率质量需要在不同模式间转移时，退火路径可能显著长于起点与终点之间的距离，从而增加传输学习难度并拖慢总体优化。

Method: 提出分量逐一退火传输方法：从包含模式信息的混合参考分布出发，将目标分布精确分解为混合分量，并沿各分量各自的退火路径分别学习传输映射，以减少模式间的质量转移。分量权重单独估计，并证明即使学习得到的流并不精确，权重估计仍保持一致性。此外，设计依据传输所需努力自适应调整的退火进度。

Result: 理论分析揭示了退火路径几何与传输学习性能的联系：较长路径会减慢总体优化，而满足足够正则性条件的路径可实现快速收敛。合成多峰分布和贝叶斯推断实验表明，该方法提升了传输学习效果与采样效率。

Conclusion: 多峰传输学习的效率不仅取决于端点分布，也受中间退火路径几何影响。通过利用模式信息进行分量级分解、分别学习传输并自适应安排退火，可减少不必要的跨模式质量移动，缓解模式坍塌并提升采样表现。

Abstract: Variational approaches to learning transport maps for multimodal sampling can suffer from mode collapse. One way to mitigate this problem is to learn transport along a prescribed path of intermediate distributions defined by geometric annealing. We show that annealing can take a substantial detour in Wasserstein space: when mass moves between modes, the path can be much longer than the distance between its endpoints. We further link path geometry to transport learning, showing that long annealing paths can slow population optimization, while sufficiently regular paths permit rapid convergence. Motivated by these findings, we propose componentwise annealed transport (CAT) to reduce the need for mass transfer between modes. Starting from a mode-informed mixture reference, CAT constructs an exact mixture decomposition of the target and learns separate transport maps along componentwise annealing paths. We estimate the component weights separately and show that these estimates remain consistent even when the learned flows are inexact. We also develop adaptive annealing schedules guided by transport effort. Experiments on multimodal synthetic distributions and Bayesian inference problems demonstrate improvements in transport learning and sampling efficiency.

</details>


### [78] [Alternatives to Shallow Autoencoders for Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06610)
*Mario Mallea,Angela Nebot,Francisco Mugica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，传统浅层自编码推荐模型主要利用物品关系，提出引入用户关系及融合用户—用户、物品—物品信息的模型；实验显示新模型在多个基准上优于原模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同过滤依赖用户与物品的交互数据来预测偏好。现有浅层自编码方法虽有较强的排序表现，但主要依据物品之间的关系构建高维线性映射，可能难以刻画细粒度且彼此相似、但并不完全相同的用户关系，从而限制推荐准确率。

Method: 在既有浅层自编码模型的基础上，提出一种利用用户—用户信息的替代建模方式，并进一步构建混合模型，同时整合用户—用户与物品—物品的邻接分布。

Result: 在多个常用推荐基准数据集上的实验表明，所提出的模型优于原有浅层自编码模型。摘要未提供具体数据集、评价指标或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在浅层自编码协同过滤中纳入用户关系具有实际价值；结合用户侧与物品侧信息，有望提升推荐排序效果。

Abstract: Collaborative filtering (CF) is a cornerstone of recommender systems and plays a relevant role in many modern applications. CF uses user-item interaction data to discover future preferences. Although deep learning models have shown promise in CF, Embarrassingly Shallow Autoencoders for Sparse Data (EASE) has gained attention for its outstanding ranking accuracy provided by its closed-form solution. EASE relies primarily on relationships among items to fit a full-rank high-dimensional linear mapping. We hypothesize that this design limits its capacity to capture similar but not equivalent fine-grained user relationships, consequently limiting its recommendation accuracy. This paper introduces an alternative formulation based on EASE that takes advantage of user-user information. Furthermore, we propose a hybrid model that combines both user-user and item-item adjacency distributions.
  Our experiments reveal that the proposed models outperform EASE on well-known recommendation benchmarks, highlighting the significance of including the user alternative in shallow autoencoder studies for CF.

</details>


### [79] [On the Generalization Limitation of On-Policy Distillation for Post-RL Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05609)
*Yichen Wang,Yufa Zhou,Siqi Zhu,Haozheng Luo,Jingzhi Bao,Zhichao Liu,Zijun Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，多教师在策略蒸馏虽能较好复现训练分布内能力，却可能无法保留教师的分布外泛化能力；通过弥补教师能力与数据覆盖不足，并采用几何修补和保留基础模型的回放，可改善分布外表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究报告在训练分布内的蒸馏效果近乎完美，但这可能掩盖能力整合不完整的问题。论文旨在检验蒸馏能否将教师的外推与组合泛化能力一并迁移，并揭示造成分布外性能下降的因素。

Method: 研究先在合成推理任务中初步检验单教师在策略蒸馏的泛化迁移，再使用千问三系列模型，在数学推理、代码、智能体和长上下文能力上评估单教师及多教师蒸馏的外推与组合泛化。研究进一步分析教师能力和数据覆盖这两类潜在瓶颈，并考察模型更新的几何结构；据此提出基于更新几何的修补方法，以及保留基础模型能力的回放方法。

Result: 在外推任务中，蒸馏模型的训练分布内得分达到教师的百分之九十六点零四至百分之一百零二点七三，但分布外得分仍比基础模型低百分之二点四五至百分之十六点二五。在三类组合任务中，多教师蒸馏虽提升了训练分布内表现，组合任务上的分布外表现仍比最佳教师低百分之十八点一九至百分之二十四点三四。联合缓解教师能力与数据覆盖瓶颈后，实验中的分布外遗忘得以消除；相较强化学习基线，蒸馏平均也能减少分布外遗忘。其模型更新平均更小、频谱更集中；多教师蒸馏的更新还呈现更强的共享结构。所提修补与回放方法可在大体保持训练分布内表现的同时改善分布外性能。

Conclusion: 在策略蒸馏及其多教师扩展存在一个容易被训练分布内高分掩盖的盲点：能力整合并不等于泛化能力得到保留。要实现可靠迁移，算法需要显式关注分布外能力的保持与泛化；教师能力、数据覆盖和更新结构都是重要切入点。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) extends on-policy distillation (OPD) to consolidate capabilities from multiple teachers. Although prior work reports near-perfect in-distribution (IND) distillation performance, we find that this can mask incomplete capability consolidation beyond the supervised distribution. A preliminary study on synthetic reasoning shows that OPD does not fully transfer teachers' generalization capabilities. We then extend this investigation to real-world tasks, evaluating extrapolative and compositional generalization across mathematical reasoning, coding, agent, and long-context capabilities using Qwen3 models. Across extrapolative settings, OPD and MOPD reach 96.04-102.73% of their teachers' IND scores. Yet their out-of-distribution (OOD) scores remain 2.45-16.25% below the base model's. Across three compositional settings, MOPD improves IND performance over the base model, but its performance on composed tasks as OOD remains 18.19-24.34% below that of the best teachers. To understand these gaps, we examine teacher capability and data coverage as two potential bottlenecks. Jointly alleviating these bottlenecks eliminates OOD forgetting in our experiments and suggests the potential for generalization beyond the teacher. (M)OPD also reduces OOD forgetting on average relative to its reinforcement learning (RL) baselines. Analysis of the associated update geometry reveals smaller and more spectrally concentrated updates on average than those of the RL baselines, along with greater shared update structure under MOPD. Motivated by these findings, we propose geometry-based patching and base-preserving replay to improve OOD performance while largely preserving IND performance. Our findings identify a blind spot in capability consolidation via (M)OPD, highlighting the need for algorithms that explicitly support OOD retention and generalization.

</details>


### [80] [LACS: Distribution-Sensitive Label-Agnostic Confidence Selection for Dynamic Data Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05616)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种按群体相对置信度筛选训练样本的方法，可减少部分训练计算；实验显示其在若干任务中维持或改善效果，但当前神经网络实现尚未实现墙钟时间加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模数据训练中，逐个样本投入完整计算代价高。预先设定保留配额无法根据样本分数分布灵活调整，而固定绝对置信度阈值又受分数尺度影响。论文因此探索一种能随整体分数分布共同调整阈值与保留数量的筛选方式。

Method: 提出标签无关置信度选择器，以样本的最大预测置信度作为分数，无须在筛选时使用标签或计算逐样本梯度。方法以群体分数的对数均值指数作为参照，并加上间隔；置信度达到或超过该阈值的样本被移除。论文分析了筛选对分数差距和高分样本占比的敏感性，并给出精确的不剪枝条件。对于参数介于零与一之间的比例参数，被移除样本比例不超过该参数，且至少保留一个样本。由于分数不带方向信息，被移除样本既可能是高置信度正确样本，也可能是高置信度错误样本。

Result: 七类评估涵盖神经网络训练、语言建模和提升方法。神经网络实验中，反向传播所需处理的样本行数减少百分之十至三十五；在柯西斯坦十分类任务中，反向处理行数减少百分之二十九，准确率均值为百分之八十九点七七，与完整交叉熵训练的百分之八十九点八二接近。在反向计算预算和优化器更新次数相同的比较中，五个随机种子下，该方法准确率比小批量训练高一点零个百分点，比随机选择高一点二个百分点。十四个表格数据集上的二次提升方法将平均测试误差从百分之二十三点三六降至百分之二十二点九六；完整训练回放中拟合样本行数约减少百分之三十，但这一计数口径与按验证集选择的准确率对应不同训练阶段。一个径向基核支持向量机案例中，耗时下降百分之三十七，误差略低。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了依赖群体分布、无需标签和逐样本梯度的训练时样本筛选规则，并在多种设置中展现出减少计算工作量且保持或改善效果的潜力。不过，减少反向传播样本数不等同于缩短实际运行时间：当前神经网络实现为获取最新分数额外执行了一次前向计算，因此尚未带来墙钟时间加速。论文指出，这一开销来自具体执行方式，而非筛选规则本身的必然要求。

Abstract: Training on large datasets motivates selecting which examples receive expensive training computation. Fixed quotas determine the amount of pruning in advance, while absolute confidence cutoffs depend on the score scale. We study a population-relative alternative that adapts the threshold and retained count together. We introduce LACS (Label-Agnostic Confidence Selector), a training-time rule based on maximum predictive confidence, requiring neither labels at selection time nor per-example gradients. LACS removes examples at or above a log-mean-exp reference plus a margin. We characterize its sensitivity to score gaps and mass, and derive an exact no-pruning condition. For $0<ρ<1$, the removed fraction is at most $ρ$ and at least one example remains. The unsigned score permits both confidently correct and confidently wrong examples to be removed. Seven evaluation settings include neural training, language modeling, and boosting. Neural execution studies reduce backward-active rows by 10-35%; on CIFAR-10, 29% fewer backward rows accompany accuracy means of 89.77% versus 89.82% for full CE. At a matched backward budget and number of optimizer updates, LACS is 1.0 pp above small-batch training and 1.2 pp above random selection over five seeds. Across 14 tabular datasets, quadratic boosting reduces mean test error from 23.36% to 22.96%. Its full training replays use about 30% fewer fitting rows; these counters span a different horizon from validation-selected accuracy. In one RBF-SVM case study, elapsed time falls by 37% with slightly lower error. These findings separate selective-work reduction from realized acceleration: the current exact-current-score neural implementation uses an additional scoring forward, so its backward sparsity does not yet translate into wall-clock acceleration. This overhead is an execution choice rather than a requirement of the selection rule.

</details>


### [81] [When Are Two SAE Latents the Same Feature?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05655)
*Alexandra Suvorikova,Ilgam Latypov,Anton Trukhan,Yuriy Dorn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套比较稀疏自编码器的统一统计框架：以能量加权最优传输同时衡量字典差异、分解潜变量贡献，并估计对应关系的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较不同稀疏自编码器时，既要识别潜特征之间的对应关系，也要概括整个字典的差异，并考虑有限评估数据带来的不确定性。以往这些问题往往分开处理，难以统一解释特征拆分、吸收及跨模型差异。

Method: 在指定的相似度几何下构造能量加权最优传输方案，并用同一方案定义字典级差异、分解各潜变量的贡献，以及衡量每个潜变量的传输质量在不同对应特征间如何分布。论文进一步分析解码器相似度、激活相似度与一阶因果相似度之间的关系，并通过理想化模型解释特征拆分和吸收。对于固定训练好的稀疏自编码器，作者推导传输代价与传输比例的联合渐近推断，纳入激活相似度及由同一批文档估计的两侧能量边际；同时采用按文档进行的联合自助法构造渐近有效的同时置信带。

Result: 在四个语言模型上的实验定位了不同随机种子训练所得稀疏自编码器之间的差异，并区分了依赖上下文而变化的特征差异。摘要未报告具体数值或显著性结果。

Conclusion: 该框架将特征匹配、字典整体差异、潜变量贡献分解及有限样本不确定性纳入同一最优传输视角，为比较稀疏自编码器提供了可解释且具统计推断依据的方法。

Abstract: Comparing sparse autoencoders (SAEs) requires connecting feature correspondence, differences between whole dictionaries, and uncertainty from finite evaluation data. We develop a framework that connects these tasks through an energy-weighted optimal-transport plan under a specified similarity geometry. The same plan defines a dictionary-level discrepancy, decomposes it into latent contributions, and quantifies how each latent's mass is distributed across counterparts. We characterize relationships between decoder, activation, and first-order causal similarities; idealized models clarify the interpretation of splitting and absorption. For fixed trained SAEs, we derive joint asymptotic inference for transport costs and fractions, accounting for activation similarities and both energy marginals estimated from the same documents. A joint document bootstrap gives asymptotically valid simultaneous confidence bands. Experiments across four language models locate cross-seed discrepancy and separate context-dependent changes.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond Accuracy: Information-Theoretic Decoder Evaluation Under Latent Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05658)
*Tasha Kim,Benjamin Ballyk,Anson Lei,Ingmar Posner,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，辅助脑机控制中的解码器不能只按单次分类准确率评估；最差目标对的可区分性更能预测长期规划表现，并据此提出可由混淆矩阵快速计算的后悔值预测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助脑到机器人控制具有连续决策特性：规划器需要随时间整合间歇且含噪的解码结果，因此单次分类准确率未必能反映最终任务表现。研究旨在建立更贴近下游控制效果的解码器评估与选择方法。

Method: 将脑到机器人控制形式化为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，并证明存在清晰的相变：若所有目标对均可由解码器区分，则整个决策时域内的后悔值一致有界；否则后悔值会随时域线性增长。研究进一步指出，最差目标对的切尔诺夫信息决定这一边界，并推导出闭式后悔值预测指标，可从任意混淆矩阵以二次时间复杂度计算。

Result: 在具有正交信息通道的两个领域中，该指标对累积规划后悔值的预测能力超越了准确率。在真实脑电数据中，两个解码器的验证准确率均为百分之三十五点一，但留出数据上的后悔值相差九点六倍；该指标能够预测这种差异。同信号控制实验也验证了其优势，尤其适用于采用独立特征通道的解码器。

Conclusion: 研究表明，解码器评估应关注目标之间是否可区分，而非仅关注单次准确率。提出的后悔值预测指标可作为现有评估流程中的直接诊断工具，辅助在序列式控制中选择传感器与解码器。

Abstract: A central challenge in assistive brain-to-robot control is decoding a user's latent intent from complex and noisy brain signals. Prior approaches evaluate decoders as single-trial classifiers with accuracy-based metrics, but this neglects the inherently sequential nature of assistive control. Since a planner must integrate intermittent, noisy decoder outputs over time, single-step accuracy can only be a proxy when predicting downstream task performance. We formalize brain-to-robot control as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), and prove that there is a sharp phase transition: regret is uniformly bounded across the horizon as long as pairs of goals are all decoder-distinguishable, but grows linearly otherwise. We show that a single channel statistic, the worst-pair Chernoff information $Γ_{\min}$ sets this boundary. Then, we derive a closed-form regret predictor $U_{\mathrm{id}}(C)$, that can be computed from any confusion matrix in $O(K^2)$ time. Across two domains spanning orthogonal information channels, we show that $U_{\mathrm{id}}$ predicts cumulative planning regret beyond what accuracy can explain. We then highlight a pathological accuracy trap, in which two decoders have identical validation accuracy 35.1% on real EEG data (BNCI2014001 subject 5) but result in a difference of $9.6\times$ in held-out regret, which $U_{\mathrm{id}}$ predicts. Same-signal control confirms $U_{\mathrm{id}}$'s advantage with decoders that span independent feature channels. Ultimately, our work provides a drop-in diagnostic to evaluate and select sensors and decoders in sequential assistive control.

</details>


### [83] [Adaptive Domain Decomposition for Finite Basis Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05659)
*Sarah Helfert,Wanxin Chen,Amanda A. Howard,Panos Stinis,Alexander Heinlein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种自适应域分解有限基物理信息神经网络，可根据解的特征自动学习计算域划分，在多个偏微分方程问题上比固定划分方法更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准物理信息神经网络在复杂问题中的近似精度可能不足。有限基物理信息神经网络通过将计算域划分为子域来提高精度，但传统划分依赖人工设定，难以适应解的局部结构与多尺度特征。因此，论文旨在实现能够依据解的特征自动调整、且稳定并具有几何可解释性的域分解。

Method: 该方法将分区统一网络与有限基物理信息神经网络结合，构成自适应域分解有限基物理信息神经网络。分区统一网络采用可分离结构，从数据中学习计算域的划分；有限基物理信息神经网络则在这些子域上求解方程，以提升近似效率和精度。

Result: 论文在二维亥姆霍兹方程、具有多尺度特征的二维泊松方程以及黏性伯格斯方程上进行了测试。摘要报告称，该方法相较于采用固定域划分的有限基物理信息神经网络取得了更高的精度，但未提供具体数值或误差指标。

Conclusion: 研究表明，根据解的特征自适应学习域划分，有望改善有限基物理信息神经网络的求解效果，并减少对人工划分的依赖。该方法在多类方程上的实验结果支持其有效性；其适用范围与计算成本仍需结合更广泛的基准和定量结果进一步评估。

Abstract: In this work, we present adaptive domain decomposition finite basis physics-informed neural networks (ADD-FBPINNs). The proposed method integrates Partition of Unity Networks (POUNets), a data-driven approach for learning domain partitions, with finite Basis physics-informed neural networks (FBPINNs) to perform more efficient domain decomposition and achieve more accurate approximations. FBPINNs improve upon the accuracy of standard physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) through user-defined subdivisions of the computational domain. By adapting the domain decomposition based on the solution's features using a separable POUNet, the framework can automatically learn partitions that are stable and can be geometrically interpretable. We test this approach on the two-dimensional Helmholtz equation, two-dimensional Poisson equation with multiscale features, and viscous Burgers equation and observe improved accuracy compared with FBPINNs with fixed domain decomposition.

</details>


### [84] [Revisiting Critic Estimation in Soft Actor-Critic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05666)
*Jan Felix Kleuker,Koen Ponse,Aske Plaat,Thomas M. Moerland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了软演员—评论家算法中的六种评论家设计，发现优势函数及广义优势估计并未带来收益，而从回放轨迹片段训练 λ 回报评论家能提升表现，尤其是在连续控制任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软演员—评论家算法通常采用一步时序差分目标学习软动作价值函数，但优势函数表示、多步回报目标，以及利用经验回放数据训练多步评论家等设计尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在系统比较这些选择，并检验它们能否改善算法表现。

Method: 固定策略优化目标，组合三项评论家设计选择，形成六种变体：基于一步转移学习的优势评论家与决斗式优势评论家，以及 λ 回报评论家和软广义优势估计评论家；后两类分别使用当前策略生成的数据或经验回放中的轨迹片段训练。研究在十四项任务上进行完整的随机超参数搜索，并将超参数选择视为算法表现的一部分。

Result: 以软优势函数替代软动作价值函数没有提升表现；软广义优势估计评论家也没有优于更简单的 λ 回报评论家。相比之下，使用经验回放中的轨迹片段训练 λ 回报评论家能够改善表现，在连续控制任务上尤为明显，而且无需重要性加权或截断多步轨迹。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在经验回放中使用未经校正的多步目标具有实际价值，并将这一结论扩展到包含熵项的完整软 λ 回报。由于所研究的设计并非软演员—评论家算法所独有，这些发现也为其应用于更广泛的强化学习算法提供了依据。

Abstract: Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) is a widely used deep reinforcement learning algorithm that combines entropy regularization with an actor-critic architecture. Standard implementations rely on one-step temporal-difference learning of Q-functions, yet alternative critic design choices remain underexplored. In this work, we revisit three such choices: learning soft advantages instead of soft Q-values, the use of multi-step return targets, and the training of multi-step critics from replay buffer data. Keeping the policy objective fixed, we compare the six critic variants these choices generate: (i) advantage and (ii) dueling-advantage critics learned from one-step transitions, and $λ$-return and soft Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE) critics learned either (iii, iv) on-policy or (v, vi) from replayed trajectory segments. Each variant is evaluated across an entire random hyperparameter search on fourteen tasks, treating the hyperparameters as part of the algorithm. Learning soft advantages instead of soft Q-values does not improve performance, and the soft GAE critic adds nothing over the simpler $λ$-return critic. What does improve performance, most clearly on continuous control, is training a $λ$-return critic on replayed trajectory segments, even without importance weighting or trace cutting. This extends earlier evidence that uncorrected multi-step targets work well under replay to the full soft $λ$-return, entropy terms included. By design, the underlying ideas studied here extend beyond SAC, and our empirical findings aim to encourage their application to a broader class of reinforcement learning algorithms.

</details>


### [85] [CoReFlow: Consistency-Relaxed Flow Maps for Probabilistic Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05715)
*Pengnan Chi,Zhiguo Zhang,Xiaoliang Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向概率交通预测的单步生成模型，在降低推理成本的同时提升预测准确性，并保持有竞争力的不确定性建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流模型通常依赖多步采样，推理较慢；而其为刻画复杂多模态分布所采用的建模方式，与条件交通数据分布的复杂度并不匹配，可能导致性能不及确定性预测基线。论文旨在提高生成式概率预测的效率与准确性。

Method: 提出一致性松弛流映射模型，利用概率流常微分方程的解算子实现单步生成。训练时，通过有限时间区间的插值端点构造简洁的监督目标；不同于维持轨迹一致性的自蒸馏或均值流恒等式方法，该目标借助条件均值约束一致性，以适应概率交通预测任务。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，该模型取得当前领先的预测准确性，优于近期基线；其不确定性建模能力具有竞争力，且相较现有生成式基线显著降低推理成本。

Conclusion: 研究表明，针对交通数据特点放松一致性约束，并采用单步流映射生成，可以兼顾概率预测准确性与推理效率，为智能交通系统中的可靠交通预测提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic traffic forecasting is essential for reliable Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), as it captures uncertainties in complex road networks. Recent diffusion- and flow-based generative models have facilitated uncertainty modeling through a multi-step sampling process. However, beyond slow inference, this process is designed to capture complex multimodal distributions, which misaligns with the limited complexity of the conditional traffic data distribution, causing models to underperform deterministic baselines. To address this, we introduce the \underline{Co}nsistency-\underline{Re}laxed \underline{Flow} Map (CoReFlow) model, presenting the first effort to leverage the one-step generation capability of flow maps, known as solution operators of probability-flow ordinary differential equations. Besides, we improve accuracy using a concise training objective that supervises the model via interpolation endpoints over finite time intervals. Unlike existing methods (e.g., self-distillation and MeanFlow Identity) that maintain trajectory consistency to model multimodal distributions, our objective leverages conditional mean to restrict the consistency, thereby achieving higher accuracy in the probabilistic traffic forecasting task. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets show CoReFlow achieves state-of-the-art forecasting accuracy. It surpasses recent baselines, provides competitive uncertainty modeling, and reduces inference costs compared to existing generative baselines.

</details>


### [86] [Give the Drafter a Second Chance to Guess: Parallel Reconsideration for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05724)
*Hoang Anh Duy Le,Huibing Dong,Alireza Khadem,Ziteng Sun,Xiang Song,Yizheng Huang,Soroush Radpour,Anshumali Shrivastava,Ye He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出并行再考虑机制，让起草模型针对自己不确定的猜测进行并行复核，从而提高猜测接受率和推理加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码的加速效果取决于目标模型接受起草模型猜测的比例。现有特征级起草器对所有词元都只进行一次前向计算；其中一些被拒绝的猜测，恰恰是起草器自身也不确定、但目标模型能够明确判断的词元。

Method: 提出并行再考虑：依据起草器自身的不确定性，筛选犹豫的猜测，并通过额外计算轮次进行复核；这些计算与常规单轮起草并行执行，因此延迟增加很小。论文还联合设计了训练方案和推理时的起草算法，将复核后的猜测作为精炼候选加入原有起草树。

Result: 在多种模型和基准测试上，该方法相较基线平均提升接受长度百分之五点三至百分之十点二，并使推理加速比提高百分之三点五至百分之九点二。

Conclusion: 利用起草器的不确定性，有针对性地并行复核难以判断的猜测，能够以较小延迟开销改善推测解码效果。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference with a small drafter, whose per-guess acceptance rate determines the achievable speedup. Feature-level drafters share a design choice: every token, easy or hard, is drafted under a single forward pass. Many of the tokens the target rejects are ones the drafter is itself unsure about yet the target answers decisively. To address this, we introduce Parallel Reconsideration: guided by its own uncertainty, the drafter reconsiders its hesitant guesses with extra passes that run in parallel with the one-pass draft, adding negligible latency. A co-designed training recipe and serving-time drafting algorithm append these reconsidered guesses to the original draft tree as refined candidates. Across models and benchmarks, on average, Parallel Reconsideration improves over the baselines by 5.3% to 10.2% on acceptance length and by 3.5% to 9.2% on speedup.

</details>


### [87] [Do Language Models Know Which Constraints Belong Together?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05726)
*Junbo Jacob Lian,Kaixuan Chen,Hanzhang Qin,Huiling Chen,Chaoyu Zhang,Chung-Piaw Teo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究如何高效找出一组必须联合使用、才能显著强化混合整数规划线性松弛的约束族，并提出结合语言模型预测与分阶段验证的搜索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丹齐格—沃尔夫重构通过对选定约束族进行凸化来强化线性松弛，但每种候选选择都需要运行列生成，因而搜索成本高。若某些约束族只有联合凸化才有效，逐个添加的贪心方法看不到任何单独收益，可能直接停止。论文所关注的背包、网络设计和设施选址等经典不等式都具有这种“零边际收益”特征。

Method: 论文首先证明：若每次评估最多凸化ｓ个约束族，而总共有ｋ个约束族，那么仅依据界值进行搜索，找到一个由ｗ个约束族组成的隐藏有效组，最坏需要数量级为ｋ的ｗ次方的评估；这一结论也适用于实际的丹齐格—沃尔夫松弛。若预测漏掉了ｅ个成员，分阶段搜索可将评估量降至数量级为ｋ的ｅ次方，且无需预先知道ｅ。论文还结合两类预测：基于最优线性规划对偶解的一步前瞻，以及依据模型名称和文本的语言模型预测；联合框架对语言模型预测进行验证，并与前瞻搜索交错执行。

Result: 在松弛界确实能够改善的米普利布非线性基准模型中，约四分之一存在必须联合使用才能强化界值的约束组。对这类模型，最强语言模型识别此类约束组的频率大致与对偶前瞻方法相当；教科书式模型的预测主要依赖约束族名称，而增加推理步骤可使同一模型的准确率翻倍。对于含有大量干扰约束族的大型模型，对偶前瞻能在一次、每次评估三个约束族的搜索中找到目标组；只读模型文本的语言模型通常无法指出该组，但一个能够读取全部系数的推理模型几乎总能识别目标，并且在寻找二元约束组时所需评估少于前瞻方法。

Conclusion: 联合有效、单独无效的约束组会使传统逐项搜索失灵，而仅依据界值的搜索在最坏情形下具有高昂的组合复杂度。可靠预测可显著降低搜索成本；分阶段验证能把预测遗漏转化为额外评估量，并使节省的计算量反映预测所掌握的信息。将语言模型与对偶结构信息结合，有望更有效地自动选择丹齐格—沃尔夫重构中的凸化约束族。

Abstract: Dantzig-Wolfe reformulation strengthens the linear relaxation of a mixed-integer program by convexifying a chosen set of constraint families, and every candidate costs a column generation run. The choice is hard when families strengthen the bound only together, because a search that adds one family at a time then stops at the linear relaxation. The classical inequalities of lot sizing, network design and facility location rest on such zero-marginal groups. We prove that when each evaluation convexifies at most $s$ of $k$ families, any method that observes only bounds needs a covering number of evaluations, of order $k^w$, to find a hidden group of $w$ families, also on actual Dantzig-Wolfe relaxations. A prediction that misses $e$ members reduces the cost to $Θ(k^e)$, through one staged search that does not need to know $e$, so the evaluations a prediction saves measure what the predictor knows. We study two sources of predictions. A one-pass lookahead at an optimal LP dual is provably blind to groups whose families share no variable, and a language model reads the names and text of the model. JointDW verifies the prediction of the language model with the staged search and interleaves it with the lookahead at a bounded loss. Groups that strengthen the bound only jointly appear in a quarter of the MIPLIB-NL models whose bound can be improved. There the strongest language models identify them about as often as the lookahead, their predictions rest on the names for textbook models, and reasoning about doubles the accuracy of the same model. On larger models with distracting families, the lookahead finds the group in one evaluation of three families. Language models given only the text seldom name it, while one reasoning model given every coefficient names it in all but one answer and then needs fewer evaluations than the lookahead for pairs of families.

</details>


### [88] [RESHPart: Feedback-Reshaped Spectral Embedding for Hypergraph Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05729)
*Rongjian Liang,Zhuo Feng,Brucek Khailany*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种通过反馈调整谱嵌入几何结构的超图划分框架，在基准测试中降低了划分割数，并能在单块图形处理器上快速完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可扩展的拉普拉斯求解器已逐渐成熟，如何更有效地将其用于高质量超图划分仍是关键问题。传统谱划分的效果与嵌入几何及投影式划分之间的契合程度有关，但这一几何结构如何形成、如何调控，尚未得到充分解释。

Method: 作者分析电路超图和可控合成图，发现谱嵌入存在有助于解释投影式划分效果的射线状结构，并指出可通过设计拉普拉斯算子和重新加权超边来系统调整嵌入几何。在此基础上，提出反馈重塑式谱划分框架，通过几何反馈和搜索反馈迭代优化谱嵌入，使其更适合投影式划分。

Result: 在泰坦二十三基准集上，与超图划分工具超米蒂斯相比，该方法在平衡容差为百分之二和百分之二十时，平均割数分别降低百分之十三点零九和百分之二十五点二一，超过此前报告的百分之十二点一六和百分之二十二点五三。所有基准测试均在单块图形处理器上于一百二十秒内完成。

Conclusion: 研究表明，谱嵌入的几何结构是影响投影式超图划分的重要因素，而且可通过反馈机制加以塑造。该框架在割数质量和运行效率方面均取得有竞争力的表现，验证了将嵌入几何与划分过程协同优化的价值。

Abstract: With scalable Laplacian solvers now available, the next frontier is to exploit them more effectively for high-quality hypergraph partitioning. In this work, we revisit spectral hypergraph partitioning from the perspective of spectral embedding geometry. Through a systematic analysis of circuit hypergraphs and controlled synthetic (hyper)graphs, we identify a ray-like embedding structure that helps explain the effectiveness of projection-based partitioning. We further show that the geometry of spectral embeddings can be systematically influenced through Laplacian design and hyperedge reweighting. Guided by these insights, we propose FRESHPart, a feedback-reshaped spectral partitioning framework that refines the spectral embedding through Geometry Feedback and Search Feedback, producing embeddings that are better aligned with projection-based partitioning. On the Titan23 benchmark, FRESHPart achieves average cut reductions over hMETIS of 13.09% at $ε=2\%$ and 25.21% at $ε=20\%$, surpassing the strongest previously reported results (12.16% and 22.53%, respectively), while completing every benchmark in under 120 s on a single GPU.

</details>


### [89] [Self-Supervised Learning of Universal Latent Spaces for Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05735)
*Sepehr Mousavi,Levi Lingsch,Siddhartha Mishra,Laura De Lorenzis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可跨任务复用的函数表示：先用无标签合成函数预训练功能自编码器，再冻结表示并训练各任务的神经算子。该方法在多类物理任务上兼具精度、速度与显存优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子通常针对每个任务，利用标注数据重新学习函数表示，导致表示难以跨任务共享，训练也耗费时间和内存。论文借鉴自然语言处理与计算机视觉中的自监督预训练思路，希望将通用表示学习与具体算子学习解耦。

Method: 作者在线合成解析函数，不使用求解器或标签，预训练功能自编码器，使其能够在任意点云上表示并重建函数。该表示对平移、尺度变化和振幅变化严格等变，预训练后将潜在空间及表示映射冻结并跨任务复用；训练神经算子时，先缓存数据的潜在表示，再仅基于这些表示进行训练。推理时还可在潜在空间中滚动计算，并通过分块处理超大规模函数。

Result: 在流体动力学、固体力学、声学以及亥姆霍兹方程和泊松方程等领域的二十八项任务中，该方法的精度可超过先进的端到端架构。训练吞吐量最高提升约十倍，显存节省使消费级显卡能够容纳近百倍规模的模型；潜在空间滚动推理最高快约十倍，分块推理则可在单张消费级显卡上处理超过五亿个点。表示还能重建未见过几何形状上的偏微分方程解与自然图像，并展现出可解释的几何性质。

Conclusion: 研究表明，任务无关且可复用的函数表示能够有效替代每个任务单独构建表示的做法，在保持甚至提升精度的同时显著降低计算与存储成本。该潜在空间不仅适合训练和推理，也具有结构性：其整体可解码为有效场，能够保留物理场之间的距离次序，支持沿测地线插值物理演化，并允许编辑方向跨场迁移。

Abstract: Neural operators fit their representation of functions to the task they are trained on, so every new task rebuilds a reduced space from labeled data. Following the self-supervised pre-training that made representations shareable in natural language processing and computer vision, we decouple representation learning from operator learning. A functional autoencoder is pre-trained on analytic functions synthesized on the fly, with no solver and no labels, to represent functions on arbitrary point clouds, and the latent space it induces is frozen and reused across tasks. The representation is exactly equivariant to translation, dilation, and amplitude, and reconstructs solutions of partial differential equations (PDEs) and natural images on geometries it never saw. Being task-agnostic, the latent space can be interpreted in its own right. The whole space is used and decodes to valid fields, it preserves the ordering of distances between physical fields, its geodesics interpolate physical evolutions, and edit directions transfer across fields. With frozen representation maps, a dataset is encoded once and a neural operator is trained entirely on cached embeddings, which saves memory and training time. On 28 tasks in fluid dynamics, solid mechanics, acoustics, and the Helmholtz and Poisson equations, this paradigm can exceed the accuracy of state-of-the-art end-to-end architectures, with up to an order of magnitude higher throughput and memory savings that fit models nearly two orders of magnitude larger on consumer-grade GPUs. At inference, latent rollouts run up to an order of magnitude faster than regular rollouts, and chunked inference processes functions of over half a billion points on a single consumer-grade GPU.

</details>


### [90] [Text-Only Domain Adaptation for Low-Resource Medical ASR: Shallow Fusion and Density Ratio Rescoring for Hausa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05744)
*Oche David Ankeli,Hassan Adelani Luqman,Theodora Ngozi Egbunike,Joshua Gottschalk,Bonaventure F. P. Dossou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，仅在解码阶段融合语言模型，就能提升哈乌萨语医疗语音识别效果；但医疗领域语言模型相较通用语言模型带来的额外收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈乌萨语等低资源语言缺少医疗领域语音数据，语音识别系统因此难以准确识别专业词汇。研究希望在不额外收集医疗语音的情况下，探索仅利用文本改善识别效果的可行性。

Method: 固定使用一个多语种声学模型，并在解码时采用浅层融合与密度比方法。医疗领域语言模型是基于约一万九千条哈乌萨语健康文本训练的四元语法模型；密度比方法还使用约七万五千条通用文本训练的模型作为来源先验。评估数据包括十名说话者对二十五条经医生审阅提示语的二百五十段录音，并采用按提示语划分的五折交叉验证。

Result: 贪心解码的词错误率为百分之二十九点五六；浅层融合与密度比方法分别降至百分之二十三点五五和百分之二十三点三三，二者均显著优于贪心解码（显著性检验概率小于百分之零点一），但两种融合方法之间差异不显著（概率为百分之四十二点三）。仅使用通用领域语言模型的浅层融合，词错误率也达到百分之二十三点九六。医疗术语错误率在融合后有所改善，但不能据此确认医疗领域模型具有明确的额外优势。

Conclusion: 轻量级、仅依赖文本的语言模型融合，可以在无需新增医疗领域语音数据的情况下改善哈乌萨语医疗语音识别。不过，结果显示，改善主要来自语言模型融合本身；医疗领域模型相较通用模型的增益较小，尚不足以证明显著的领域特定优势。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems often struggle with specialised medical vocabulary, especially in low-resource languages where domain-specific speech data are scarce. We investigate text-only domain adaptation for Hausa medical ASR using the fixed MMS-1B-FL102 acoustic model and two decoding-time language-model fusion methods: Shallow Fusion (SF) and the Density Ratio Approach (DRA). A 4-gram KenLM trained on 19,421 Hausa health sentences serves as the medical target model, while a 75,372-sentence general-domain model provides the source prior for DRA. Evaluation uses 250 recordings of 25 physician-reviewed prompts from 10 speakers across six northern Nigerian states. Under prompt-disjoint five-fold cross-validation, SF and DRA reduce WER from 29.56% to 23.55% and 23.33%, respectively. Both significantly outperform greedy decoding (p < 0.001), while their difference is not significant (p = 0.423). However, general-LM-only SF achieves 23.96% WER, indicating that the medical LM's additional improvement over a general LM is modest. Medical Term Error Rate also improves under fusion, but does not establish a distinct medical-domain advantage. These findings demonstrate that lightweight, text-only language-model fusion can improve Hausa medical ASR without additional domain-specific speech collection.

</details>


### [91] [Optimizing Recourse Under Predictive Multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05774)
*Shahin Jabbari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向预测多重性的稳健反事实建议方法，在给定行动预算下，寻找对一组竞争模型均尽可能有效的行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表现相近的模型可能对同一个体给出不同预测，因此针对单一模型设计的建议，在其他模型上可能失效。论文旨在提升建议面对模型差异时的可靠性，并考察这种要求对不同群体的影响。

Method: 将稳健建议建模为预算约束下最小化竞争模型集合中最坏有效性损失的问题，允许模型来自不同架构或假设类别。提出一种高效利用预言机的算法，并给出其收敛的充分条件。

Result: 在一个合成数据集和五个真实数据集上，实验涵盖线性模型、神经网络、混合模型及决策树。结果显示，优化间隙界逐步减小，许多设置下建议有效性较高；相较于针对特定模型类别或架构设计的基线，表现具有竞争力。研究还量化了统一行动要求对给定预算下建议成功率的影响，并分析不同人口群体间的差异。

Conclusion: 该方法为模型预测存在分歧时生成稳健建议提供了优化框架，并通过理论分析与实验展示其可行性。跨模型一致的行动可能改变建议成功率，且其影响在不同群体间并不一定相同，因此需要同时评估稳健性与公平性。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse identifies modifications that can change an unfavorable model prediction into a favorable one. Under predictive multiplicity, similarly performing models can disagree on individual predictions, so recourse valid for one model may fail for another. We formulate robust recourse under predictive multiplicity as a budget-constrained minimization of the worst-case validity loss across a competing set of models, which can include models with different architectures or from different hypothesis classes. We propose an oracle-efficient algorithm called ORPM and prove sufficient conditions for its convergence. We evaluate ORPM on one synthetic and five real datasets, and competing sets consist of linear, neural, mixed, and decision-tree models. Our experiments show decreasing optimization-gap bounds and high validity in many tested settings, with favorable performance against baselines designed for specific model classes or architectures. We also quantify how requiring a common action across competing models changes recourse success at a given budget and examine disparities in this success across demographic groups.

</details>


### [92] [Pathwise-Budgeted LLM Routing: Online Allocation with K-Bit Tail-Cost Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05835)
*Hanzhang Qin,Sophie H. Yu,Yuewei Zhang,Zhi-Hai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向固定预算的大语言模型任务路由策略，在控制调用成本的同时尽可能多地完成任务；在多个基准测试和紧张预算下，该策略优于所比较的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业在固定预算下调用大语言模型时，需要选择模型，并决定失败后是否重试。调用费用只有在响应完成后才揭晓，较长的单次响应可能耗尽预算，使后续任务无法处理。因此，核心目标不是单纯提高单次成功率或降低平均成本，而是在服务停止前最大化已解决任务数。

Method: 提出一种上下文赌博机策略，针对任务学习各模型的成功概率，并利用响应长度的粗粒度标签估计成本。具体而言，该方法将响应长度划分到含有二的若干次方个区间的分位网格中，构造偏保守的成本估计；再根据实际支出和剩余任务可分配的预算调整成本惩罚，从而权衡成功可能性与费用。理论分析在校准性、局部正则性和网格覆盖等条件下，界定保守成本估计的额外误差：样本量固定时，增加区间数会减小离散误差项，但会增大估计误差余量。

Result: 在最紧的测试预算下，该策略解决的数学与科学问答任务数量超过最佳实现基线的两倍；在路由器基准测试的十四种预算设置中，有十种取得最高平均累计得分。在代码生成基准测试的三个最低预算设置中，该策略在默认模式和节省成本模式下均优于已部署的商业路由器。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，结合任务级成功率学习、保守成本估计和动态预算调节，可以更有效地应对调用费用事后才可知的预算约束场景。方法在实验中表现突出，并给出了成本估计误差与网格精细度之间的理论权衡；其理论保证则依赖于文中提出的若干条件。

Abstract: Firms using large language models under fixed budgets must decide which model to call and whether to retry a failed task. Because a call's charge is revealed only after completion, one long response can exhaust the remaining budget and leave later tasks unserved. Our objective is to maximize the number of tasks solved before service stops. We develop a contextual bandit policy, ADP-HT, which learns each model's task-specific success probability and constructs conservative cost estimates from coarsened response-length labels on a quantile grid with $2^K$ intervals. The policy weighs predicted success against these costs, adjusting the cost penalty to realized spending and the budget available per remaining task. Under calibration, local regularity, and grid-coverage conditions, we bound the excess allowance in the policy's conservative cost estimates. At a fixed sample size, increasing $K$ reduces the discretization term but increases the estimation allowance. At the tightest tested budgets, ADP-HT solves more than twice as many MATH500 and GPQA tasks as the best implemented baseline. It achieves the highest mean cumulative score at ten of fourteen RouterBench budgets. On LiveCodeBench, it outperforms a deployed commercial router in both default and cost-saving modes at the three lowest test budgets.

</details>


### [93] [PEARL: Prediction Error-Adaptive Regularized Learning for Selective Forgetting under Partial Preference Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05842)
*Rashmi Gangadharaiah,Balakrishnan Narayanaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种依据预测误差进行选择性遗忘的个性化推荐方法，在偏好发生局部变化时重新分配模型不确定性，提升推荐效果，同时保留未变化偏好上的有效知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户需求、情境与口味会随时间变化，导致个性化推荐、大语言模型回复生成及基于人类反馈强化学习训练的奖励模型逐渐失准。现有非平稳情境老虎机方法通常对所有维度均匀遗忘，但现实中的偏好变化往往只涉及部分维度。神经情境老虎机的格拉姆矩阵会不断累积信心，却缺乏在预测出错处定向恢复不确定性的机制。

Method: 提出“预测误差自适应正则化学习”方法：当模型在某些特征方向上出现超出预期的预测误差时，沿这些方向反向调整格拉姆矩阵更新，以恢复相应的不确定性；对预测仍然可靠的方向则保留已有信心。理论上，论文分析了偏好变化后的遗憾下界、固定探索率的局限，并给出了该方法的累积遗憾界。

Result: 理论结果表明：任何不具备自适应能力的策略在偏好变化后都会遭受线性遗憾；不存在一种固定探索率能同时在平稳和非平稳环境中实现低遗憾；该方法的累积遗憾取决于发生偏好变化的用户数，而非用户总数。在个性化大语言模型回复选择基准上，该方法比贪心策略高出四个百分点，比最佳神经老虎机基线高出二点五个百分点，差异具有显著性；偏好稳定时则与贪心策略表现相当，并领先滑动窗口和折扣方法三至十个百分点。其选择模型约有十万个参数，可在中央处理器上毫秒级运行，无需更新大语言模型权重，也适用于基于应用程序接口调用的模型。

Conclusion: 面对用户偏好的局部漂移，按预测误差定向遗忘比对所有信息统一遗忘更有效。该方法兼顾偏好变化后的适应能力与平稳时期的性能，且计算开销较低，适合在不修改大语言模型权重的情况下部署。

Abstract: User preferences shift as needs, context, and tastes evolve. This matters for personalized recommendation, LLM response generation, and RLHF-trained reward models, which can become miscalibrated as preferences drift. Existing non-stationary bandit methods forget uniformly across all dimensions, but real preference shifts are typically partial: some dimensions go stale while others remain valid. Neural contextual bandits accumulate confidence monotonically via the Gram matrix, with no mechanism to direct uncertainty where predictions have gone wrong. We propose PEARL (Prediction Error-Adaptive Regularized Learning), which reverses the Gram update along feature directions where predictions are surprisingly wrong, while leaving confidence intact elsewhere. We prove that (i) any non-adaptive policy suffers linear regret after a preference shift, (ii) no fixed exploration rate achieves low regret in both stationary and non-stationary regimes, and (iii) PEARL's cumulative regret scales with the number of shifted users N_s, not the total population N. On PRISM, a personalized LLM response selection benchmark (1,500 users, 21 LLMs), PEARL beats greedy by +4.0 points and the best neural bandit baseline by +2.5 points (p<0.001), while performing on par with greedy when preferences are stable. It beats sliding-window and discounted approaches by 3 to 10 points, showing that selective forgetting outperforms uniform forgetting. The selection policy is a ~100K-parameter MLP that runs in milliseconds on CPU, leaves LLM weights frozen, and works with any API-based model.

</details>


### [94] [A low-rank decoder bottleneck bounds reliability in foundation-model perturbation prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05849)
*Zheyu Zhang,Yuanhao Huang,Fan Feng,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，扰动响应预测的可信度与其和训练响应的相似程度有关，并提出一种可逐条预测使用的置信度评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型通常以总体准确率评估扰动响应预测，但实验优先级排序需要知道每条预测是否可靠。论文旨在审计状态转移解码器的可靠性边界，并建立可用于单条预测的置信度指标。

Method: 研究考察了状态转移解码器在三种编码器和四种细胞系的基因抑制数据上的表现。作者发现，解码器首层注意力输出具有低秩特征；将其投影到由训练激活估计出的四至三十二个固定方向，仍能维持平均的逐扰动皮尔逊变化相关性。随后提出“预测近邻距离”：计算预测响应与最近训练响应之间的余弦距离，并将其作为单条预测的置信度评分。

Result: 预测近邻距离与逐扰动皮尔逊变化相关性的平均绝对斯皮尔曼相关系数为零点八一，接近使用真实观测响应得到的零点八八。基于该评分校准的阈值可迁移至不同编码器和细胞系；该评分对药物扰动的预测表现也具有区分能力。

Conclusion: 解码器的低秩注意力瓶颈限制了预测可靠性，且预测可靠性会随响应偏离训练数据而下降。预测近邻距离为跨模型、跨细胞系评估单条扰动预测提供了实用的置信度依据，可辅助实验筛选与资源分配。

Abstract: Foundation-model predictors of perturbation response are evaluated primarily by aggregate accuracy. Using their predictions to prioritize experiments requires assessing each prediction's reliability. We audit the state-transition (ST) decoder used in STATE across three encoders (SE-600M, scGPT, and Tahoe-x1) and four cell lines under CRISPRi. The decoder's first attention layer has a low-rank output. Projecting this output onto 4-32 fixed directions estimated from training activations preserves mean per-perturbation Pearson delta correlation (PDC). This low-rank attention bottleneck bounds the decoder's reliability by distance to training responses: PDC declines as the cosine distance from a perturbation's observed response to its nearest training response increases. We propose predicted-NN-distance, the cosine distance from a predicted response to its nearest training response, as a per-prediction confidence score. The score has a mean absolute Spearman correlation of 0.81 with PDC, close to the 0.88 obtained with the observed response. Thresholds calibrated on this score transfer across encoders and cell lines, and the score remains predictive of PDC for drug perturbations.

</details>


### [95] [On Convergence of Gradient Temporal-Difference Learning with Regularized Corrections and Eligibility Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05869)
*Jingyi Jin,Martha White,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明带资格迹的约束式正则化校正时序差分算法在非独立同分布的离策略数据下可收敛，并提出具有鲁棒性的胡伯损失版本；充分强的正则化可在均值时序差分矩阵正稳定时保证收敛，无须矩阵正定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序差分学习结合离策略学习、自举和函数逼近时可能发散。现有正则化校正时序差分算法虽具有理论可靠性且实现简单，但收敛理论仅覆盖独立同分布采样下的一步情形；此外，胡伯贝尔曼误差尚未扩展至资格迹算法。研究旨在弥补这些理论空缺，并降低大贝尔曼误差对学习的影响。

Method: 研究建立带资格迹的约束式正则化校正时序差分算法，允许步长递减、非独立同分布的离策略数据，以及依赖状态或历史的资格迹参数；并提出胡伯损失版本，在保留原算法不动点的同时增强对大贝尔曼误差的鲁棒性。两种算法均采用随机逼近中的常微分方程弱收敛方法分析。针对胡伯版本的非线性均值动力学，进一步给出保证目标平衡点唯一且全局渐近稳定的积分小增益条件。

Result: 研究证明了两种约束算法的收敛性，并推导出可计算的充分收敛条件。分析还揭示，线性算法与胡伯损失版本存在共同的正则化收敛机制：当均值时序差分矩阵正稳定时，只要正则化足够强，两种算法均可收敛，而不要求该矩阵正定。

Conclusion: 本文将正则化校正时序差分的收敛理论扩展至资格迹、非独立同分布离策略数据及状态或历史相关参数，并通过胡伯损失提升对异常大的贝尔曼误差的稳健性。结果表明，适当正则化能够在较弱的矩阵条件下保障收敛，为更广泛的时序差分学习场景提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Temporal-difference (TD) learning underlies many prediction and control algorithms, but can diverge when combined with off-policy learning, bootstrapping, and function approximation. TD with Regularized Corrections (TDRC) is theoretically sound and practically simple, but existing convergence theory covers only the one-step case under i.i.d. sampling, while the Huber Bellman-error formulation has not been extended to TDRC or eligibility traces. In this work, we establish convergence of constrained TDRC($λ$) with diminishing stepsizes and non-i.i.d. off-policy data, for state-dependent and history-dependent $λ$-parameters. We also propose TDRC($λ$)-Huber, which preserves the TDRC($λ$) fixed point while reducing sensitivity to large Bellman errors. For both algorithms, we apply an ODE-based weak-convergence method from stochastic approximation theory. For the nonlinear mean dynamics of TDRC($λ$)-Huber, we give an integral small-gain condition that guarantees uniqueness and global asymptotic stability of the target equilibrium. We then establish convergence of the constrained algorithm, derive computable sufficient conditions for convergence, and identify a regularization-based convergence mechanism shared by linear TDRC($λ$) and TDRC($λ$)-Huber. When the mean TD matrix is positive stable, both constrained algorithms converge under sufficiently strong regularization without requiring positive definiteness.

</details>


### [96] [MIGONet: a multiple-input graph neural operator with solver-free discrete manufactured-solution training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05876)
*Muhammad Hasnain,N. K. Anand,Arun Srinivasa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出多输入图神经算子及离散制造解训练方法，无须求解偏微分方程即可生成训练数据；在矩形域测试中，该方法明显优于对比模型，且数据生成速度大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传输问题的代理模型需要同时处理空间分布的热源以及狄利克雷、诺伊曼边界条件。传统训练数据通常依赖数值求解，生成成本较高；此外，模型还需有效捕捉全局源项与边界信息对温度场的共同影响。

Method: 以稳态热传导为例，提出多输入图神经算子，并采用离散制造解方法构造训练数据：预先指定温度场，再使用与参考有限差分方法相同的离散算子计算源项及狄利克雷或诺伊曼边界数据，从而使每个样本按构造满足离散控制方程。边界条件作为算子输入，可在每个样本的各边界段分别指定。模型先将完整源场和边界数据提供给所有节点，再进行局部消息传递。研究还与不含完整源场编码器的模型及多输入算子网络进行比较，并在多种非矩形域上测试。

Result: 在矩形域的曲线热丝和高斯热斑测试中，所提模型的平均相对温度误差分别为百分之十点三一和百分之十点六四；多输入算子网络分别为百分之四十五点九七和百分之四十八点三四，不含完整源场编码器的模型分别为百分之七十二点二五和百分之六十七点八二。生成一万组样本时，离散制造解将数据生成时间从有限差分迭代标注的四百八十六点六五秒降至三点一五秒。研究还在字母形、圆盘形和环形区域上分别训练并评估模型。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将完整源场与边界信息显式编码并注入各节点，有助于提升图神经算子对热传导问题的预测精度；离散制造解则能在保持离散方程一致性的同时显著降低训练数据生成成本。该方法为多源项、多边界条件及复杂区域上的传输代理建模提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Surrogate modeling of transport requires operators that capture the combined influence of spatially distributed sources and Dirichlet and Neumann boundary conditions, together with training datasets that can be generated at low computational cost. This work presents MIGONet, a multiple-input graph neural operator, and a discrete method of manufactured solutions (MMS) that trains it without solving a partial differential equation, using steady heat conduction as the model problem. In the discrete MMS procedure, temperature fields are prescribed a priori, and the source terms and Dirichlet or Neumann boundary data are evaluated with the same discrete operators as the reference finite-difference method (FDM), so every training sample satisfies the discrete governing equations by construction. Because the boundary data are derived from each field, a Dirichlet or Neumann condition can be assigned to every boundary segment of every sample, and the boundary specification becomes an operator input. MIGONet supplies representations of the complete source field and boundary data to every node before local message passing. On the rectangular domain, it is compared with a version without the complete-source encoder (MIGONet-local) and with the multiple-input operator network MIONet, using the same training data. MIGONet achieves mean relative temperature errors of 10.31% and 10.64% for curved-wire and Gaussian-spot heating, compared with 45.97% and 48.34% for MIONet and 72.25% and 67.82% for MIGONet-local. For 10,000 samples, discrete MMS reduces data-generation time from 486.65 s to 3.15 s relative to matched iterative FDM labeling. Separately trained MIGONet models are also evaluated on L-shaped, disc-shaped, and ring-shaped domains.

</details>


### [97] [Deep Meets Classical: A CPU-Efficient Decision-Fusion Model for Automatic Modulation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05937)
*Zhiyi Yin,Zhizhao Wen,Ruoxin Zhang,Chao Wang,Puyang Xu,Yifan Jiang,Yusi Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出轻量级决策融合方法，结合卷积神经网络与高阶累积量分类器的互补优势，在低信噪比和高信噪比场景中均保持较强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动调制分类对认知无线电中的频谱感知和非协作信号识别十分重要。两类适合中央处理器运行的方法——直接处理同相与正交采样的紧凑卷积神经网络，以及基于高阶累积量特征的传统分类器——以往较少按信噪比条件进行比较。研究发现，前者在低信噪比下更稳健，后者在高信噪比下更准确，因此有必要利用二者的互补性。

Method: 构建轻量级决策融合模型：分别训练一个约含十九点八万个参数的残差卷积神经网络和一个基于高阶累积量特征的梯度提升树分类器，再将两者输出的类别概率拼接，并由逻辑回归元分类器进行融合。元分类器使用与测试集相互独立的验证集训练，以避免测试信息泄漏。

Result: 在相关公开数据集上，采用五个随机种子，并在四万四千帧留出测试集上评估，融合模型总体准确率达到百分之五十五点八。相比最佳累积量特征基线提升五点一个百分点，相比单独卷积神经网络提升三点二个百分点；配对自助法百分之九十五置信区间的提升下界分别为五点零和三点一个百分点，均为正。该方法在高信噪比区域达到最佳传统基线的表现，在低信噪比区域则与卷积神经网络相当。整体计算量约为每帧三百万次浮点运算，可在单个中央处理器核心上训练和运行。

Conclusion: 两类分类器在不同信噪比区域各有优势，融合其类别概率能够带来稳定的整体准确率提升。该方案参数量和计算开销较低，具备在通用及嵌入式处理器上部署的潜力；其结论主要基于所评测的数据集与实验设置。

Abstract: Automatic modulation classification (AMC) underpins spectrum sensing and non-cooperative signal identification in cognitive radio, yet the two standard CPU-feasible approaches to it, a compact convolutional neural network (CNN) on raw in-phase and quadrature (I/Q) samples and a classical higher-order-cumulant feature classifier, are rarely compared against each other conditioned on signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). We show that they are, in fact, complementary: the CNN is markedly more robust at low SNR, where hand-crafted statistics collapse toward chance, while the cumulant classifier is more accurate at high SNR, where higher-order statistics cleanly separate dense constellations. We exploit this complementarity with FuseAMC, a lightweight decision-fusion model that trains a logistic-regression meta-combiner over the concatenated class-posteriors of an approximately 198k-parameter residual CNN and a gradient-boosted-tree cumulant classifier, on a validation split disjoint from test. On RadioML 2016.10a (5 seeds, 44k-frame held-out test set), FuseAMC reaches 55.8% overall accuracy, improving on the best cumulant-feature baseline by 5.1 points and on the CNN alone by 3.2 points (paired-bootstrap 95% confidence-interval lower bounds of 5.0 and 3.1 points, both positive), while matching the best classical baseline in its own strong high-SNR regime and the CNN in its strong low-SNR regime. With about 0.2 million parameters, a model footprint of a few megabytes, and roughly 3 MFLOPs per frame, the whole pipeline trains and runs on one CPU core, targeting general-purpose and embedded processors without hardware acceleration.

</details>


### [98] [BAM: Bivariate Additive Models for Interpretable Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05940)
*Atsushi Koike*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出双变量加性模型，用简单、可视化的二维高斯形状函数提升模型可审计性，同时保持较强预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义加性模型中的特征对交互项虽可通过等高线图展示，但现有方法学到的形状函数往往过于复杂，包含不规则轮廓和剧烈局部波动，难以解释，也难以依据领域知识进行核查。论文希望在保持预测准确性的同时，显著简化这些交互项。

Method: 提出双变量加性模型，将每个特征对的形状函数限制为简单的参数化形式，具体采用非对称二维高斯函数。每个交互项因此呈现单峰等高线图；模型训练时还会自动选择特征对。

Result: 在六个表格数据基准上的实验表明，该模型的预测准确率与标准广义加性模型相当，并接近包含特征对交互项的广义加性模型。实验还展示了准确率与需要检查的模型组件数量之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 通过约束交互项的表达形式，双变量加性模型使模型更易于可视化、解释和审计，同时保留了有竞争力的预测性能。它为在模型准确性与人工核查成本之间取得平衡提供了一种方法。

Abstract: Generalized Additive Models with pairwise interactions (GA$^2$Ms) are regarded as glass-box models, since each pairwise shape function can be visualized as a contour plot. In practice, the shape functions learned by methods such as EBM or NODE-GAM are so complex (irregular contours and sharp local fluctuations) that a practitioner cannot meaningfully interpret them or check them against domain knowledge. Auditability in this stronger sense requires substantially simpler shape functions. We argue that such simplification can match the accuracy of standard GAMs if the right feature pairs are captured. We propose Bivariate Additive Models (BAMs), a class of GA$^2$Ms in which every pairwise shape function is constrained to a simple parametric form, instantiated as an asymmetric bivariate Gaussian. Each component is visualizable as a unimodal contour map, and its feature pair is selected automatically during training. Experiments on six tabular benchmarks show that BAM attains predictive accuracy comparable to standard GAMs and approaches that of GA$^2$Ms. We also show the trade-off between accuracy and the number of components to inspect.

</details>


### [99] [Reward Budgeting Reduces Premature Convergence in Reinforcement Learning for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05942)
*Mengni Jia,Mengyu Zhou,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“奖励预算”方法：为每个提示分配固定总奖励，以调节强化学习中的更新幅度。实验显示，该方法无需显式维持策略熵，也能缓解性能过早停滞并提升推理成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理强化学习常在策略分布逐渐尖锐后较早进入性能平台期。已有研究尝试通过提高策略熵来应对，但论文实验发现，单纯增加熵并不能稳定改变最终平台。因此，作者转而从隐式目标策略和奖励差异如何转化为概率比差异的更新动力学角度，分析过早收敛。

Method: 作者提出尖锐度因子及其有效形式，用于刻画奖励或优势差距对概率比差距的影响，并据此设计“奖励预算”：针对每个提示构造优势，使其总奖励预算固定。对于二元奖励和独立的在线策略采样，未裁剪的得分函数估计器对应一个精确的有限样本数、具有递减收益性质的凹目标；随着正确采样数增加，优势差距会减小。对于一般奖励，在满足特定正则条件时，渐近上可作平移对数均值解释。作者强调，这些理论结论并未证明采用裁剪的实现与该目标严格等价。

Result: 在问文三八十亿参数模型和拉玛三点二三十亿参数指令模型上，奖励预算 consistently 提升了多种强化学习基线的表现；在二〇二三年美国数学竞赛、二〇二四年及二〇二五年美国邀请数学考试等基准上，最高分别提升十二点五和十分。实验还表明，无需显式保持策略熵，也可取得显著收益。

Conclusion: 论文支持一种关于过早性能停滞的解释：关键因素可能是更新动力学及奖励差异转化为策略变化的方式，而不只是策略熵。通过为每个提示设定固定奖励预算，能够形成随正确结果增多而减弱的优势信号，从而改善多种基线的推理表现；但裁剪算法与理论目标之间的严格等价性仍未得到证明。

Abstract: RL for LLM reasoning often reaches an early performance plateau as the policy distribution becomes increasingly sharp. Recent work has studied policy entropy as both a diagnostic and a control signal for this behavior. In our experiments, however, increasing entropy alone does not reliably change the eventual plateau. We analyze RL through its implicit target policy and derive a sharpness factor $κ$, together with an effective counterpart $κ_{\mathrm{eff}}$, that describes how strongly reward or advantage gaps are translated into probability-ratio gaps. Motivated by this view, we introduce \emph{reward budgeting}, a plug-in advantage construction that assigns a fixed total reward budget to each prompt. For binary rewards and independent on-policy rollouts, its unclipped score-function estimator follows the gradient of an exact finite-$G$ concave objective with diminishing returns, while its advantage gap decreases as more rollouts become correct. For general rewards, a shifted log-mean interpretation holds asymptotically under the stated regularity conditions. These results motivate, rather than establish exact objective equivalence for, the clipped implementations. On Qwen3-8B and Llama3.2-3B-Instruct, reward budgeting consistently improves a range of RL baselines, with gains up to +12.50 on AMC23 and +10.00 on AIME24 and AIME25. Overall, our results support an update-dynamics view of premature convergence and show that strong gains can be obtained without explicitly preserving entropy.

</details>


### [100] [On the Design of Hyper-Connections: Simplification, Stability, and Scalability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05984)
*Xiangdong Zhang,Xiaohan Qin,Sunan Zou,Tuo Dai,Bizhao Shi,Zhuo Xia,Yebin Yang,Yizhou Chen,Yuliang Liu,Yu Cheng,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出简化超连接架构，通过取消残差混合并共享记忆访问，在保持模型性能的同时显著降低训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超连接架构已用于先进语言模型，但其组成机制尚未充分理解，且训练开销不可忽视。论文旨在辨明哪些交互机制真正有效，并寻找更简单、高效的设计。

Method: 作者系统分析超连接的门控与混合机制，发现两类方案均有效：仅使用可学习读写门控而不进行混合，或采用不受约束的混合并固定门控。基于此提出简化超连接：移除残差混合，改用共享记忆访问；利用加法结构和共享门控输入，让不同层的门控共享组入口状态，以批量读取并延迟写入，并通过标量校正精确保留前序子层输出的贡献。

Result: 在最大测试规模下，简化超连接相较于普通残差基线仅增加百分之二点四的端到端训练时间。在十五亿至三百亿参数的混合专家模型扩展过程中，其性能与混合超连接相当；标准训练中的梯度变化更平滑，并且在压力测试下保持稳定。分析还指出，重复访问扩展残差状态是训练开销的重要来源，即使不进行混合也会如此。

Conclusion: 研究表明，复杂的残差混合并非实现高性能的必要条件；通过保留有效的残差结构、共享记忆访问并简化执行，可以在接近原架构性能的同时降低开销。该工作倡导在基础模型设计中进一步追求简洁，使规模化性能提升更实用、更易普及。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) architectures are increasingly adopted in frontier language models, yet their components remain insufficiently understood and introduce non-negligible training overhead. In this work, we systematically study the HC architecture to understand its components and identify simpler, more efficient designs. We find that learned read/write gating without mixing and unconstrained mixing with fixed gates form two effective interaction groups: either alone achieves performance comparable to mHC. We also identify repeated access to the expanded residual state as a major source of training overhead, even without mixing. These findings lead to SimpleHC, which simplifies the architecture by removing residual mixing and simplifies execution through shared memory access, using the resulting additive structure and shared gate inputs. Layer-specific gates share a group-entry state, enabling batched reads and deferred writes; scalar corrections exactly preserve preceding sublayer output contributions. Our analyses support both the retained residual structure and the design of shared memory access. SimpleHC incurs only 2.4% extra end-to-end training time over a vanilla residual baseline at the largest tested scale. It maintains performance comparable to mHC when scaling from 15B to 30B MoE models, exhibits smoother gradient dynamics during standard training, and remains stable under stress. We hope SimpleHC will encourage a broader pursuit of simplicity in foundation model design, making performance gains more accessible and practical at scale.

</details>


### [101] [A Comparative Study of Importance Attribution to Columns in Deep Learning over Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05986)
*Hila Levi-Yosefi,Shunit Agmon,Ester Livshits,Agapi Rissaki,Benny Kimelfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较关系数据库深度学习模型的属性重要性解释方法，发现方法差异会随任务类型而变，并可能影响模型解读；在不少场景中，单属性影响法可低成本替代基于夏普利值的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据库上的深度学习模型日益有效，但理解其预测依据的需求也随之增长。属性重要性解释方法众多，研究者希望弄清不同方法会在多大程度上给出不同结论，以及这种差异是否会影响解释质量。

Method: 研究提出一套系统比较属性重要性解释方法的分析框架，分别考察属性重要性排序的一致性、重要性分数分布的差异，以及这些差异与解释忠实度的关系。实证评估涵盖蒙特卡洛夏普利值、核夏普利解释、单属性影响法和掩码学习法，并在多个数据集与任务上测试异质图采样聚合网络和关系图变换器，比较回归与分类任务中的表现。

Result: 方法间的一致性受预测任务类型影响：回归任务中，多数方法的属性排序较为一致；分类任务中的共识明显较弱。掩码学习法的分数分布经常与其他方法不同，而基于夏普利值的方法与单属性影响法往往产生相近解释。这表明在许多场景下，单属性影响法有望以显著较低的计算成本替代夏普利值方法。

Conclusion: 属性重要性解释并非与方法选择无关：不同方法可能影响对关系型深度学习模型的理解，且这种影响会随任务类型变化。本文提供了系统比较方法的分析工具与实证证据，并提示实际应用中应结合任务特性选择解释方法，同时考虑解释一致性与计算成本。

Abstract: Deep learning over relational databases has led to increasingly effective prediction models, motivating a growing need to explain their behavior. We study explanation methods that assign importance scores to individual database attributes, focusing on a fundamental question: to what extent does the choice of importance-attribution method matter? We develop a methodology to systematically compare importance-attribution methods and analyze their similarities and differences. In particular, we study two aspects of the explanations: which attributes they deem important, captured by differences in attribute rankings, and how importance scores vary across attributes, captured by differences in score distributions. We further investigate whether these differences are reflected in the conventional measure of explanation quality, fidelity.
  We apply our methodology in an extensive empirical study of four methods: Monte Carlo Shapley, Kernel SHAP, single-attribute impact, and mask learning. We experiment with multiple datasets and tasks using two RDL architectures: heterogeneous GraphSAGE and Relational Graph Transformers. Our experiments show that explanation methods behave differently depending on the prediction type: regression or classification. In regression, most methods exhibit high agreement in attribute rankings, whereas classification yields considerably less consensus. Notably, while mask learning frequently diverges in its importance distributions, Shapley-based methods and single-attribute impact often converge to similar explanations. This suggests that, in many settings, single-attribute impact can replace Shapley at substantially lower computational cost. Overall, our study provides methodological tools for comparing importance-attribution methods, and empirical evidence that their choice has important consequences for interpreting relational deep learning models.

</details>


### [102] [Decouple to Reuse: Fine-Grained Knowledge Augmentation for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05993)
*Yuqian Wu,Qingxiang Liu,Yuxin Guo,Weifeng Su,Jian Li,Pengfei Xu,Xi Chen,Yutao Yue,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段细粒度知识增强框架，将预训练时间序列知识拆分为趋势与季节性模式，并用于增强冻结的时间序列基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型迁移到下游任务时会受到领域偏移影响。现有检索增强方法通常检索粗粒度的时间窗口，可能引入与目标任务不匹配的上下文噪声。由于有效的趋势和季节性动态可能来自不同上下文，作者认为应在分解后的模式层面复用知识。

Method: 该框架分为两个阶段。第一阶段构建双分支残差码本，分别编码解耦后的趋势信号与季节性信号，并将预训练知识整理为紧凑、任务共享的表示。第二阶段针对目标表示动态检索并合并细粒度模式，通过任务自适应聚合与门控融合增强冻结的基础模型。

Result: 在八个真实世界基准数据集和四种具有代表性的时间序列基础模型上的实验表明，该方法取得了更好的整体效果，并具有较高的计算效率。实验还验证了残差码本能够在不同基础模型之间迁移。

Conclusion: 在时间序列迁移中，按趋势与季节性分解并复用细粒度模式，有助于缓解粗粒度上下文检索带来的噪声。该框架无需调整基础模型本身，便可通过紧凑码本增强其下游预测能力，并展现出跨模型迁移潜力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting is critical for demand prediction and resource management in modern Web services, yet applying pretrained Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) to downstream tasks remains challenging due to domain shifts. Existing retrieval-augmented approaches typically retrieve coarse-grained window contexts and may introduce context-specific noise. To address this problem, we argue that temporal patterns should be reused at a decomposition level, as effective trend and seasonal dynamics may originate from different contexts. We propose \method, a two-stage \underline{F}ine-grained \underline{K}nowledge \underline{A}ugmentation framework that decouples and consolidates pre-training knowledge into compact, task-shared residual codebooks to enhance frozen TSFMs. Specifically, Stage I constructs dual-branch residual codebooks for decoupled trend and seasonal signals, while Stage II dynamically retrieves and merges these fine-grained patterns into target representations via task-adaptive aggregation and gate fusion. Extensive experiments across eight real-world benchmarks with four representative TSFM backbones demonstrate the superior effectiveness and computational efficiency of \method, while further validating the strong transferability of the residual codebooks across different backbones. The source code is available at the anonymous repository: https://github.com/yuppielqx/TSFM-Component-Token-Adapter

</details>


### [103] [How Far Can You Steer? Behavior-Capability Frontiers in Contrastive Activation Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06001)
*Deepanshu Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将激活引导视为受能力损失约束的干预：它能稳定提升目标行为，但效果及副作用取决于模型、行为、引导方向、层级和评估范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导无需更新模型参数即可改变行为，但目标行为提升并不意味着干预可用。对比激活加法可能损害无关能力，因此需要在行为收益与能力退化之间进行明确权衡，并检验基准评估是否足以反映真实风险。

Method: 研究构建行为—能力前沿，在设定能力损失上限的条件下选择行为增益最大的干预。实验覆盖四个指令微调模型、七种行为，以及百分之一、百分之三和百分之五的能力损失预算；同时进行对照实验，并评估验证集效果的泛化、跨行为迁移、次要能力、分布外表现和开放式生成。

Result: 验证集选出的行为效果能够较强地泛化至留出数据，相关系数为零点九四五。非零操作点上，行为与能力同时保持的比例从百分之一预算时的百分之三十七点五升至百分之三预算时的百分之七十二点七，扩大至百分之五后提升有限。对照实验表明，效果依赖已学习的行为方向，而非一般性的激活扰动。表征几何对因果跨行为迁移具有显著但有限的预测力；此外，通过受保护基准的干预仍可能损害未测试能力，且在开放式生成中的迁移并不均匀。

Conclusion: 激活引导不是普遍适用的行为控制旋钮，而是一种受约束的干预。其可用范围取决于模型、目标行为、引导方向、层级与能力损失预算；仅凭受保护基准上的表现，不能保证未测试能力或自由生成质量不受影响。

Abstract: Activation steering can modify language-model behavior without parameter updates, but behavioral efficacy alone does not determine whether an intervention is usable. Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA) steers targeted behaviors using activation directions derived from contrasting examples, potentially degrading unrelated model capabilities in the process. We therefore study CAA through a behavior-capability frontier, selecting interventions that maximize behavioral gain subject to an explicit bound on capability degradation. We evaluate this framework across four instruction-tuned models, seven behaviors, and capability-loss budgets of 1%, 3%, and 5% (absolute percentage points). Validation-selected behavioral effects generalize strongly to held-out data ($ρ= 0.945$), while joint behavior-capability survival among nonzero operating points rises from 37.5% at the 1% budget to 72.7% at 3%, with limited additional improvement at 5%. Control experiments indicate that these effects depend on learned behavior directions rather than generic activation perturbations. Representational geometry is significantly but only modestly predictive of causal cross-behavior transfer. Finally, secondary capability, out-of-distribution, and open-ended evaluations show that interventions satisfying protected benchmarks can still degrade untested capabilities or transfer unevenly to free-form generation. These results characterize activation steering as a constrained intervention whose usable range depends on model, behavior, direction, layer, and evaluation budget, rather than a universal control knob.

</details>


### [104] [Attraction-Ranking Mismatch in Out-of-Distribution Detection: Theory and Evidence from Hamiltonian Feature Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06021)
*Zilin Wang,Lianghai Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，把特征吸引到学得的分布内区域，并不必然提升分布外检测；关键在于分布内与分布外样本在排序边界附近的相对分数变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将样本特征拉向类别区域，直觉上可能增强分布内与分布外样本的区分，但单个类别亲和分数变好，不代表两类样本的相对排序变好。论文旨在厘清这种吸引式特征优化何时有效，以及为何可能适得其反。

Method: 作者以类别条件的哈密顿动力学为可控研究框架，区分驱动向量场与检测分数不一致造成的偏差，以及二者一致时仍存在的排序失败。通过精确分解受试者工作特征曲线下面积，并分析分布内—分布外排序边界的局部变化，刻画性能变化的来源；另以实验检验跨数据划分迁移、微小扰动及质心近似等情形。

Result: 理论分析指出，在满足正则条件时，曲线下面积的一阶变化由排序边界附近分布内与分布外样本的相对分数变化决定。实验发现，与检测分数对齐的规则虽能提升留出源验证集表现，迁移后却会持续降低检测性能，且不需要发生获胜类别改变。另在所测设置中，边界附近的相对分数变化能够反映曲线下面积的小幅变化方向。质心近似与共享尺度还表明，零步分数可联系到一类静态质心检测器，其中包含已有的质心检测方法。

Conclusion: 类别吸引本身不足以证明特征细化有助于分布外检测。评估此类方法时，应关注分布内与分布外样本在排序边界附近的相对分数变化，而不能只看类别亲和度或源验证集上的改善。

Abstract: Attracting features toward learned in-distribution (ID) regions is an intuitive strategy for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, but does such attraction improve detection? We investigate this question using class-conditioned Hamiltonian dynamics as a controlled testbed and identify an attraction-ranking mismatch: improving individual class-affinity scores need not improve the relative ordering of ID and OOD samples. Our analysis separates misalignment between the driving field and the detection score from ranking failure that persists even when they are aligned. An exact AUROC decomposition and a local boundary analysis identify relative score changes at the ID-OOD ranking boundary as the source of first-order AUROC change under regularity conditions. Experiments demonstrate that a score-aligned rule can improve performance on a held-out source validation split yet consistently degrade it after transfer, even without a single winning-class change. A separate perturbation study finds that near-boundary relative score changes track the direction of small AUROC changes in the tested settings. Finally, centroid approximation and shared scales connect the zero-step score to a static centroid family that includes the existing CTM detector. Together, these results explain why class attraction is insufficient to justify feature refinement and motivate evaluating its effect on the ranking boundary.

</details>


### [105] [Who Pays for the Knowledge Models Copy? Model Collapse as Rogers' Paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06022)
*Nam H. Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将生成模型的知识衰退解释为罗杰斯悖论：若群体只复制模型输出、缺少独立学习，知识会逐代陈旧，模型最终无法让群体受益；只有能带来新信息的核查与学习才能打破这一循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有模型崩塌理论通常把外部新数据视为固定、持续可得的供给，却未解释这些新知识由谁产生、为何会持续出现。论文指出，关键问题在于数据供给会受到模型使用方式影响，因此需要把人类学习与数据生产纳入分析。

Method: 论文借用文化演化中的罗杰斯模型，将模型崩塌视为群体依赖模仿而停止自主学习所导致的知识衰退，并分三步检验：用仅基于合成训练的模型拟合结果预测不同新旧数据混合下的稀有知识保留率；研究目标知识随时间变化时，模型谱系对新数据的需求；最后建立人们可向模型取代自主求知的数据供给内生模型，并分析均衡及核查行为的作用。

Result: 罗杰斯模型能够以数个百分点以内的误差，预测从词组三元模型到预训练变换器对稀有知识的保留情况。世界变化越快，持续复制自身过去输出的模型谱系越需要新数据；每代从固定预训练权重重新开始的谱系则较少受影响，因为这些权重携带不会经由复制而衰减的知识。在内生供给均衡中，模型可靠性恰好受制于人们自主查明信息的成本，群体整体并不比不用模型更有利；模型生成文本占比上升，必须由更多人的自主学习来弥补。只筛选看似可信文本的过滤器会强化从众并加速崩塌，而能引入新信息的核查可以打破悖论。

Conclusion: 模型知识衰退不仅是训练数据分布问题，也是知识供给机制问题。要维持模型所依赖的新鲜信息，必须保留并鼓励人的独立学习；单纯复制或筛选既有输出无法替代真实的信息获取。

Abstract: Generative models trained on the output of earlier models lose knowledge across generations, rare knowledge first. The remedies that work all bring in information from outside the loop, and the theory of model collapse treats that supply as given: a fixed archive, drawn from a fixed distribution. We argue that the supply is the problem, and that cultural evolution has the theory of it. We read model collapse as Rogers' paradox: a population that comes to rely on copying ends up no better off than one that never copied, because copies go stale unless someone keeps learning for themselves. We test the identification in three steps. First, Rogers' model, fitted only to fully synthetic training, predicts to within a few percentage points how much rare knowledge language models, from word trigrams to a pretrained transformer, retain under other mixtures of fresh data. Second, when the world itself changes, a lineage that copies its own past needs more fresh data to keep up the faster the world changes, as Rogers' model says it must and as analyses that assume a fixed target cannot; a lineage that restarts every generation from fixed pretrained weights is largely insulated, because those weights are knowledge that is never copied. Third, we close the loop: when the people who write a model's training data can ask the model instead of finding things out, the supply of fresh data becomes endogenous. At equilibrium, the model is exactly as reliable as it is costly for people to find out for themselves, the population is no better off than without the model, and every increase in the share of model-written text must be paid for by more people learning for themselves. Users who check what they are told and relearn when it is wrong break the paradox. Filters that select plausible text are conformist and accelerate collapse; only checks that carry new information act as critical learning.

</details>


### [106] [Practical Estimation of the Bayes Optimal Fairness-Accuracy Tradeoff with Soft Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06027)
*Mohit Sharma,Okan Koç,Amit Deshpande,Takashi Ishida,Gang Niu,Rajiv Ratn Shah,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出公平分类中的贝叶斯误差估计方法，用于评估给定公平约束下可达到的最优准确率，并刻画公平与准确率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类存在由数据分布决定的最低预测错误率。公平分类还要求不同人口群体获得相近的真正例率，因此准确率与公平性之间存在不可避免的权衡。现有方法缺少对这种权衡理论极限的估计，难以判断模型距离最优水平还有多远。

Method: 作者推导了满足指定公平约束时的公平贝叶斯错误率显式表达式，并构造一致的估计器。估计器只使用正类概率等软标签和群体信息，不需要输入特征或额外训练；同时给出估计偏差与样本复杂度的理论界限。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够稳健、低方差地估计最优公平—准确率权衡曲线；即使软标签存在噪声，或由基于硬标签训练的分类模型生成，估计效果仍然良好。

Conclusion: 这项工作将贝叶斯误差分析扩展到受公平约束的分类问题，为评估公平分类器的理论性能上限提供了可计算、具一致性的工具，也有助于判断模型是否已接近最优权衡或存在过拟合。

Abstract: There is a fundamental limit to the best prediction error of any model for a given data distribution. In classification, the Bayes error quantifies this limit and serves as both a benchmark for trained models and a criterion to detect overfitting. The state-of-the-art Bayes error estimation techniques for binary classification use only soft labels (positive class probabilities) and require no input features or auxiliary training. We extend such notions to constrained classification problems, such as fairness. Fair classification is an important case of constrained or multi-objective classification. It requires a model not only to be accurate but also to yield fair outcomes (equal or near-equal true positive rates) across different demographic groups (e.g., race and gender). This leads to an inherent fairness-accuracy tradeoff or a Pareto frontier that is used to compare fair classifiers against each other. The focus of our work is to estimate the fundamental limit for this fairness-accuracy tradeoff. We derive an explicit expression for the fair Bayes error rate (i.e., best achievable error rate subject to given fairness constraints), and construct consistent, instance-free estimators for it using only soft labels and group information. We provably bound the bias and the sample complexity of our estimators. Empirically, our method produces a robust, low-variance estimate of the optimal fairness-accuracy tradeoff curve, even when the soft labels are noisy and derived from a classification model on hard labels.

</details>


### [107] [TRACK: Multiple Instance Learning for Anti-Money Laundering in Transaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06035)
*Seonkyu Lim,Inwoo Tae,Gwangui Hong,Jaehoon Lee,Jeongwhan Choi,Taehoon Kim,Min-Soo Kim,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将反洗钱账户检测建模为多实例学习：把账户时间窗视为实例集合，逐笔编码交易并选择性汇聚，从而在仅使用账户级标签时识别可疑账户及其可疑交易。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反洗钱调查通常以账户为单位，但可疑证据往往只出现在账户的少数交易中。先汇总交易再学习，或在图模型中进一步汇总邻近账户信息，可能稀释这些局部信号。论文将这种粒度不匹配称为“聚合错配”，并研究其对账户级检测的影响。

Method: 将账户时间窗建模为一个集合、其中交易为集合内实例，并以时间窗内至少存在一笔被标记交易作为账户级基准标签。提出一种多实例学习方法：使用共享编码器表示每笔交易，再有选择地汇聚交易表示以作出账户判断。训练期间不向模型提供五个反洗钱数据集的交易级标签，并采用按时间排序的评估协议。

Result: 在所有数据集上，该方法在仅使用账户标签训练的表格与图模型基线中取得最高平均精确率。尽管没有使用交易级监督，其表现仍与直接使用交易标签训练的边分类器具有竞争力；其交易实例评分也能显著优于随机排序，并在不同数据集上接近或超过直接监督的排序表现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，多实例学习能够缓解账户级标签与交易级可疑证据之间的粒度错配。在不依赖交易标签的条件下，该方法既能有效检测账户，也能提供有用的交易级排序，为反洗钱调查提供更细粒度的线索。

Abstract: Anti-money laundering (AML) investigations are organized around accounts, while the suspicious evidence for an account may reside in only a few of its transactions. Yet many account-level detectors summarize transactions before learning, and message-passing models may aggregate once more over neighboring accounts.
