We develop a Tail-Gated Evidence Calibration mechanism (TGEC) to eliminate node-to-window aggregation distortion. It calibrates multi-granular graph responses against normal operational references, strictly preserving benign score rankings while selectively refining extreme anomaly candidates in the tail distribution for reliable window detection and interpretable sensor evidence. Extensive evaluations with two GFM backbones demonstrate that Gfm4Mtsad achieves superior detection performance over existing baselines.

</details>


### [141] [The Alignment Tax Is Graded: Task-Selective Capability Loss Under Length-Normalized DPO with SFT Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06476)
*Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，随着偏好优化的隐式奖励间隔增大，能力损失并非均匀发生：信息抽取基本保持，格式化输出仅小幅下降，路由任务则显著退化；损害主要集中在中等间隔区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注偏好对齐中的“对齐税”是否会在不同任务上均匀出现，尤其是奖励间隔增大时，模型的下游能力会如何变化。研究旨在刻画这种变化及其任务差异，而非提出新方法或比较不同优化目标。

Method: 作者在一个四十亿参数模型上，以稀疏低秩适配进行微调，采用长度归一化的直接偏好优化，并加入监督微调保持项；任务为结构化金融文件处理。研究预先注册，使用八个随机种子，并在冻结后评估六个未见样本；通过考虑数据制品影响的评分和等效性检验，追踪奖励间隔增长期间的信息抽取、格式化输出与路由能力。同时比较量化训练与未量化测量下的奖励间隔及正确答案对数概率变化。

Result: 能力损失具有明显的任务选择性：信息抽取下降零点五五个百分点，且在正负五个百分点范围内达到等效；格式化输出下降三点五二个百分点，但聚类后的题目级区间未能证明等效；路由下降三十个百分点。路由损害主要集中在奖励间隔为一至三的区间，并伴随短暂的格式破坏峰值。奖励间隔增长至少百分之九十六来自对拒绝答案的抑制，而被选答案的奖励基本持平。正确答案对数概率下降也因任务而异，且信息抽取中正确答案相较错误控制项受到选择性保护，八个种子均如此。量化训练与未量化测量得到的奖励间隔最多相差三点四倍，且差异依赖所考察的维度。

Conclusion: 在该模型、训练设置和三类结构化任务范围内，对齐税是渐进且任务相关的，路由能力尤其脆弱；奖励间隔增大主要体现为压低拒绝答案，而非提高被选答案的奖励。结果不能归因于某个单一梯度通道。结论受单一模型、合成且易区分的偏好数据、稀疏适配及含监督保持项的目标限制；无保持项对照和格式对齐度量仍有待研究。

Abstract: Setting. This is an empirical characterization study; we propose no new method and run no comparisons against alternative objectives. We fine-tune Qwen3.5-4B with sparse LoRA and length-normalized DPO plus an SFT retention term (the RPO objective) on structured financial-filing tasks, and track downstream capability as the implicit reward margin grows to $\sim 7$ $β$-scaled units (nf4 training; $\sim 3$ under fp16 measurement on the unquantized base). Question. Does margin growth degrade capability uniformly across tasks, or selectively? Findings. In a pre-registered study (8 seeds, 6 unseen at freeze) with artifact-aware scoring and TOST equivalence testing, the alignment tax is graded and task-selective: extraction is preserved ($Δ= -0.55$ pts, equivalent at $\pm5$ pts), json loses little ($Δ= -3.52$ pts, equivalence fails on a clustered item-level interval), and router degrades severely ($Δ= -30$ pts). Router's loss concentrates in a margin 1-3 damage window containing a transient format-break spike. The margin is accounted for almost entirely ($\geq 96\%$) by suppression of rejected completions, with chosen reward approximately flat, consistent with the retention term anchoring it; we do not attribute the behavioral damage to any single gradient channel. Gold-logp deflation is task-selective (extraction $-0.15$, json $-1.04$, router $-1.47$ nats/token under fp16), with gold selectively protected relative to incorrect controls on extraction (8/8 seeds). QLoRA reward margins differ up to $3.4\times$ between nf4 training and unquantized fp16 measurement, with an axis-dependent gap. Scope. Length-normalized rewards with SFT retention, sparse LoRA on 8/32 layers, one 4B model, easily-separable synthetic preferences, three structured tasks. A no-retention control and a measured format-alignment metric are left to future work.

</details>


### [142] [Model Merging Does Not Require Uniform LoRA Ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06486)
*Zirong Xie,Zainab Afolabi,Nikita Kozodoi,Jack Butler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型合并不必要求各领域专家采用相同的低秩适配器秩；按领域分别选择秩，可提升多领域表现，但效果取决于合并方法是否按参数幅度筛选信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见做法要求所有微调专家使用相同的低秩秩，导致各领域无法采用最适合自身任务的秩。研究旨在判断这种限制是否源于模型合并本身，并解释不同合并方法对异秩专家的适应性差异。

Method: 研究者在数学、指令跟随和多语言三个领域，以四种秩和三种模型规模训练了三十六个专家，并采用六种合并方法进行比较，分别测试统一秩与各领域独立选出的最佳秩。研究进一步分析合并所保留的谱成分，以及这些成分对损失的影响。

Result: 允许专家使用不同秩不会增加平均合并和基于效用的选择的成本，并使多领域准确率最高提升一点八个百分点。冲突消解类方法表现不稳定，最多损失四个百分点。按幅度挑选谱成分会丢弃许多有助于降低损失的成分，且往往几乎舍弃指令适配器，使指令跟随评估下降十六至二十五个百分点。按其对损失的影响选择成分，则能保留三个领域的专家；在八倍秩范围内，准确率仅波动零点七个百分点，而四十亿参数规模下的蒂斯方法波动达六点七个百分点。

Conclusion: 各专家的秩可以按领域独立设定，关键在于合并算子是否依据幅度筛选成分。避免这种幅度筛选、转而依据成分对损失的作用进行选择，有助于保留不同专家的知识，并使性能对秩选择更稳健。

Abstract: Model merging combines separately fine-tuned experts into a single model without retraining. Standard practice trains every expert at the same LoRA rank, which couples the rank choice across experts even though each domain has its own best rank. We show this coupling is a property of the merge method, not of merging. We train 36 experts on three domains (mathematics, instruction following, multilingual) at four ranks and three model scales, then merge with six methods at uniform ranks and at each domain's own best rank. Giving experts different ranks costs averaging and utility-based selection nothing, and gains them up to 1.8 pp of multi-domain accuracy, while the conflict-resolution methods are inconsistent and can lose up to 4 pp. The split has a simple cause. Merging keeps only a fixed share of the pooled spectral components, and methods that choose them by size discard many that would have reduced loss. They also tend to discard the same expert: the instruction adapter has the smallest singular values, so it is dropped almost entirely and IFEval falls by 16 to 25 pp. Choosing components by their effect on loss instead keeps all three experts, and leaves accuracy nearly flat across an 8$\times$ rank range (0.7 pp, against 6.7 pp for TIES at 4B scale). Rank can therefore be chosen per expert whenever the merge operator does not select by magnitude.

</details>


### [143] [Nowcasting Future Performance for Population Pruning in Survival-of-the-Fittest Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06512)
*Rico Städeli,Léo Meynent,Boris Knyazev,Damian Borth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究如何利用训练中间状态预测模型未来表现，并据此提前淘汰较差候选模型，以节省训练计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大量不同超参数、结构或随机种子的模型成本高昂。若仅依据当前准确率提前停止训练，学习曲线交叉可能导致潜在优胜模型被误删，因此需要更可靠的未来性能预测来指导筛选。

Method: 研究者训练预测器，利用准确率与损失历史、权重统计信息，以及将模型检查点编码为潜在序列的变换器自编码器表示，预测模型五个训练轮次后的准确率或最终准确率。随后将预测结果用于“适者生存”式训练：周期性排名并停止预测表现最低的运行。评估时与按当前准确率筛选进行比较。

Result: 权重统计信息最适合预测五个轮次后的准确率，而自编码器表示最适合预测最终准确率。在卷积神经网络模型集合等性能较为接近的群体中，按当前准确率筛选已能保留接近最佳的最终模型；在性能差异较大的残差网络和视觉变换器群体中，单靠当前准确率较难筛选，而依据预测最终准确率的策略几乎弥合了与最佳模型之间的差距。

Conclusion: 中间检查点所含的信息能够支持对模型未来性能的有效预测，并改善训练中的提前淘汰决策。该方法尤其适用于候选模型性能差异较大的场景，可在减少训练开销的同时，更接近保留最终最优模型。

Abstract: Training high-performing neural networks often involves evaluating populations of candidate models with varying hyperparameters, architectures, or random seeds. Training every candidate to completion is computationally expensive, yet stopping seemingly weaker runs early risks discarding models that would ultimately perform better, as learning curves can cross. To address this challenge, we investigate whether intermediate training checkpoints can predict future model accuracy and enable more informed pruning decisions. Our nowcasters predict test accuracy five epochs ahead or at the end of training. They are trained with accuracy and loss histories, weight statistics, and SANE representations as inputs. SANE is a transformer autoencoder that encodes a checkpoint as a sequence of latent tokens. We then use the nowcasters in Survival-of-the-Fittest (SOTF) training. SOTF repeatedly ranks runs during training by predicted accuracy and stops the lowest-ranked ones. Our results show that weight statistics work best for five-epoch forecasts, and SANE representations work best for final accuracy prediction. For evaluation, we compare SOTF with pruning the population by current accuracy. In homogeneous model zoos such as the CNN zoos, pruning by current accuracy already retains a final model only slightly worse than the best. Model zoos with a larger spread in performance, such as the ResNet-18 and ViT zoos, are more difficult to prune by current accuracy alone. In these zoos, SOTF with nowcasted final accuracy almost closes the gap to the best model.

</details>


### [144] [An Interaction-Aware Decision Framework for FDIC-Assisted Distressed Bank Acquisitions: Empirical Evidence from the FDIC Failed-Bank Panel and the SVB-First Citizens Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06514)
*Yousef Nejatbakhsh,Malihe Aliasgari,Parsa Safaee,Sina Shokoohyar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文检验一种结合信号交互关系的框架，能否帮助判断美国上市银行收购受监管机构协助处置的失败银行后，市场是否会作出正面反应。结果显示，该框架没有优于基准模型或随机排序的可靠证据，按其阈值制定的决策也可能造成损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收购失败银行时，资产价值、监管保护、风险、买方实力和交易执行难度并非彼此独立。例如，政府分担损失会降低不良贷款带来的负面影响。若只孤立考察各项指标，可能误判交易的市场吸引力，因此论文尝试以可解释的交互关系改进判断。

Method: 论文提出“蜘蛛网络过滤框架”，将五个维度之间的依赖关系编码为四条可解释的交互规则，并生成图结构启发式特征，纳入逻辑回归模型；再依据模型得分设置“推进、谨慎、回避”的决策规则。研究样本为二〇〇八至二〇二四年由美国存款保险机构协助完成、且收购方母公司在美国上市的五十五笔交易。模型使用失败前监管报告中的六项财务指标，以及监管机构公告披露的两项交易条款。预测目标是公告前后三日的正异常收益，用以衡量市场最初反应，而非交易的长期实际成败。评估采用分组、嵌套交叉验证，并通过重复完整模型选择流程的置换检验检验排序能力。

Result: 加入蜘蛛框架后的模型平均受试者工作特征曲线下面积为零点五九四，低于相同输入下的逻辑回归零点六一五和岭回归零点六三二。置换检验至多只提供较弱证据，无法有力证明这些模型的排序能力优于随机水平；直接使用蜘蛛评分也未能优于随机排序。目标银行困境程度与较高收益之间表面上的关联，在采用银行业基准后消失。此外，为最大化Ｆ一分数而调整的阈值会批准多数最终出现负收益的收购；若负面交易的损失是正面交易收益的三倍，决策效用为负。

Conclusion: 在这组小规模样本和所设定的市场反应指标下，蜘蛛框架并未显示出稳定的预测或决策优势。研究也提醒，公告期市场反应不能等同于收购的长期成败，模型阈值还必须结合错误决策的实际成本评估。

Abstract: Acquirers of failed banks weigh acquisition value, regulatory protection, risk, buyer strength, and execution difficulty jointly, because each signal's meaning depends on the others: high non-performing loans matter less when government loss sharing absorbs the losses. The proposed Spider Network Filtering Framework encodes such dependencies as four interpretable interaction rules among five dimensions, yielding graph-inspired features for a logistic model and a Pursue/Caution/Avoid decision rule. We evaluate it on 55 acquisitions assisted by the Federal Deposit Insurance Corporation in 2008-2024 by acquirers with a parent listed in the United States. Six financial inputs come from regulatory reports published before each failure; two deal terms, which the winning bidder knows when it bids but investors learn at the announcement, come from the regulator's press releases. The outcome, a positive three-day abnormal return around the announcement, records the market's initial reception, not realized success. Under grouped, nested cross-validation, the Spider-enhanced model reaches a mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.594, against 0.615 for logistic regression on the same inputs and 0.632 for ridge regression; permutation tests that repeat the full model selection give at most weak evidence that any of them ranks better than chance. The Spider score used directly does not rank better than chance, and an apparent link between target distress and higher returns disappears against a bank-sector benchmark. Thresholds tuned for the F1 score approve most acquisitions with negative returns and realize negative utility when each costs three times what a positively received acquisition gains.

</details>


### [145] [IDSISA: Influence-Distilled Sharded Training for Efficient Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06519)
*Deeraj SK,Sadhana Devarajan,Tanmoy Hazra,Anupam Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种改进的机器遗忘方法：将易被要求删除的样本集中到少数分片，并通过知识蒸馏弥补分片训练造成的效用损失；删除保证保持精确，且可处理连续删除请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘需要在删除指定训练样本影响的同时保留模型效用。完全重训虽有强保证但代价高昂，近似方法效率较高却缺少形式化保证。现有分片隔离切片聚合训练方法会因随机分片而使删除请求波及多数分片，且各分片独立训练可能降低模型效用。

Method: 提出影响力蒸馏式分片隔离切片聚合方法。首先利用影响函数预测容易被要求遗忘的样本，并将其集中到少数分片，以减少删除时需重训的范围；再借助未受影响分片的知识蒸馏，恢复隔离训练造成的效用损失。该方法继承原有重训步骤的精确删除保证，将蒸馏带来的效用恢复误差单独量化，其期望认证误差不超过百分之一点五。针对连续删除请求中跨分片蒸馏可能引入的依赖关系，论文还提出基于闭包的级联重训程序，以恢复完整聚合模型的删除保证。

Result: 在残差网络与图像分类数据集、双向长短期记忆网络和时序卷积网络与影评数据集，以及全连接网络与服饰图像数据集上的实验显示，相较于基线分片方法，选择性遗忘效果提高百分之十四至百分之九十一；效用保留相对提升最高达百分之五十，其他架构也取得稳定的百分点增益。遗忘耗时变化介于减少百分之六点七至增加百分之六点四之间，具体取决于架构。

Conclusion: 该方法通过有针对性的样本分片降低删除成本，并以蒸馏改善效用，同时保留精确的删除保证；对于连续删除请求，闭包式级联重训可处理蒸馏导致的跨分片依赖。实验表明，该方案在多种模型与数据集上兼顾遗忘效果、模型效用和运行时间。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes the influence of specific training examples to comply with privacy regulations. Full retraining gives strong guarantees but is computationally prohibitive at scale; approximate methods are efficient but lack formal guarantees. SISA (Sharded, Isolated, Sliced, and Aggregated training) achieves certified deletion through data sharding and independent retraining, but random partitioning scatters forget requests across most shards, and isolated retraining degrades utility. We introduce IDSISA (Influence-Distilled SISA), which uses influence functions to concentrate forget-prone samples into few shards and distillation from unaffected shards to recover utility lost to isolation. The deletion guarantee inherited from SISA's retraining step remains exact and requires no probabilistic qualification; the distillation-based utility recovery is a separately quantified, empirically bounded approximation ($ε_{\text{cert}} \leq 0.015$ in expectation). We further formalize certification under sequences of repeated deletion requests, where cross-shard distillation can create dependencies between shards; we prove that a closure-based cascading retraining procedure restores the deletion guarantee to the full aggregated model under any deletion sequence. Experiments on ResNet-18/CIFAR-10, ResNet-18/CIFAR-100, BiLSTM/IMDB, TCN/IMDB, and a fully connected network on Fashion-MNIST show $14-91\%$ better selective forgetting, up to $50\%$ better utility retention in relative terms (with consistent percentage-point gains on the other architectures, where a relative baseline is undefined), and unlearning-time changes ranging from $-6.7\%$ to $+6.4\%$ depending on architecture, relative to SISA.

</details>


### [146] [EvoOPD: Evolving Privileged Information Strategies for Black-Box On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06542)
*Ximo Zhu,Ruiqi Liu,Yi Han,Zhengan Wu,Gengsheng Li,ZiAn Wang,Zhu Yuantian,Jin Jiang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种会随学生模型状态变化而调整教师反馈策略的黑盒在线蒸馏方法，在数学推理和代码生成的六种设置中均优于对比基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有模型虽具备较强的推理与编程能力，但其输出概率分布不可访问，依赖教师概率信息的精细蒸馏方法难以直接用于黑盒模型。教师提供的文本反馈可转化为学生轨迹上的密集监督，但反馈应聚焦何处、包含何种信息、提供多少细节，以及这些选择如何随训练变化，尚不明确。

Method: 提出黑盒在线蒸馏中的演进式特权信息策略方法。该方法建立可演进的策略库，并依据特权信息对当前学生模型的修复效用，动态调整反馈的关注重点、信息类型和细致程度，使教师反馈持续匹配学生的学习需求。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上，使用不同规模的学生模型进行六种设置评测，所提方法在全部设置中取得最佳总体表现。相较各设置中的最强基线，数学任务平均提升2.14个百分点，代码任务平均提升1.30个百分点。分析还发现，不同信息获取策略的相对效果会随学生状态改变。

Conclusion: 特权信息的获取策略本身应被视为可优化对象，而非固定不变的流程。根据学生状态持续调整教师反馈，有助于提升黑盒在线蒸馏效果；固定策略难以在整个训练过程中始终保持最优。

Abstract: Proprietary language models exhibit strong reasoning and coding capabilities, but their output distributions are inaccessible, making fine-grained distillation methods that rely on teacher logits difficult to apply directly to black-box models. We observe that rich textual feedback from a black-box teacher can be treated as privileged information (PI) and converted, through a PI-conditioned privileged teacher, into dense supervision signals on the student's own trajectories. However, what PI should be acquired from the teacher to most effectively support student learning remains unclear. PI strategies that differ in where they focus, what information they request, and how much detail they provide can have substantially different effects on the current student, and their relative effectiveness can further change as training progresses. Based on this observation, we propose Evolving Privileged Information Strategies for Black-Box On-Policy Distillation (EvoOPD), which treats PI acquisition itself as an optimization problem and maintains a PI Strategy Bank that evolves with the student state. EvoOPD uses the measured repair utility of PI on the current student to adapt the focus, information type, and granularity of teacher feedback, allowing the acquired information to continually match the student's learning needs. We systematically evaluate EvoOPD on mathematical reasoning and code generation with Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B students. EvoOPD achieves the best overall performance in all six model-task settings, improving over the strongest baseline in each setting by an average of 2.14 percentage points in mathematics and 1.30 points in code. Further analysis shows that the relative effectiveness of PI acquisition strategies changes with student state, making a fixed strategy difficult to sustain as the best choice throughout distillation.

</details>


### [147] [From Fixed Defaults to Calibrated Activation Policies for Long-Term Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06559)
*Shijie Xu,Boyan Tang,Jianghua Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种基于既往实验、经过校准的激活配置选择策略，可针对不同预测任务调整激活函数及其位置；在多数任务上优于固定配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期时间序列预测需要在观测范围之外提供可靠预测，但现有研究多采用固定激活配置，难以说明不同数据集和预测时域应如何选择激活函数。逐任务进行穷举搜索又成本较高，因此需要利用既往实验经验指导新任务配置。

Method: 研究利用既往实验学习一个可靠的默认配置，并根据具体任务调整激活函数及其在模型中的位置。当证据不足以支持调整时，策略保留默认配置。评估基于信息器模型的1,225次训练运行，覆盖17种数据集变体和51项预测任务。

Result: 与传统固定激活配置相比，该策略使均方误差平均降低7.86%，并在72.55%的任务上取得改进。

Conclusion: 激活函数的选择及其放置位置，是提升现有时间序列预测模型性能的实用途径。积累的实验结果能够帮助新任务作出配置决策，减少每项任务都进行全面搜索的需要。

Abstract: Long-term time-series forecasting supports decisions in energy, transportation, and other applications where reliable predictions are needed beyond the observed data. Although forecasting research has advanced model architectures, fixed activation configurations provide limited guidance on how activation choices should vary across datasets and prediction horizons. This study aims to establish when task-specific activation choices improve forecasting accuracy and how previous experiments can guide the selection of suitable configurations for new tasks. We develop a calibrated selection policy that learns a reliable default from prior experiments, adapts the activation function and its location to each task, and retains the default when evidence for a change is insufficient. In an evaluation supported by 1,225 Informer training runs across 17 dataset variants and 51 forecasting tasks, the policy reduces mean squared error by 7.86% on average relative to the conventional fixed-activation baseline and improves 72.55% of tasks. These findings establish activation choice and placement as practical opportunities for improving existing forecasting models and show how accumulated experimental evidence can guide configuration decisions without repeating an exhaustive search for every task.

</details>


### [148] [Thermodynamics-Informed Learning Captures Hierarchical Hydrogen-Bond Networks in Deep Eutectic Solvents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06590)
*Yanpeng Ye,Chaoran Duan,Mian Xu,S. Mani Sarathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种热力学信息神经网络，用于预测深共熔溶剂中木质纤维素组分的溶解度，并辅助筛选适用于原料分级的溶剂配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深共熔溶剂的溶解能力来自氢键受体、氢键供体与溶质共同形成的多组分氢键网络，难以仅凭单一组分性质准确推断。现有预测方法在复杂体系和稀疏实验条件下存在局限，因此需要能体现溶剂化热力学与分子相互作用的筛选工具。

Method: 研究构建热力学信息神经网络，按层级描述氢键受体与氢键供体的缔合，以及溶质在溶剂中的容纳过程，并以学习得到的自由能路径组织预测。模型使用938条数据训练，并通过消融分析评估溶质特异性稳定作用和氢键相容性对预测的贡献；此外，测量葡萄糖、木聚糖和克拉夫特木质素在酸性、碱性及中性深共熔溶剂中的溶解度，并将模型用于玉米秸秆分级配方筛选。

Result: 模型测试集决定系数为0.917，并能推广至未见过的氢键受体—供体组合及数据稀疏条件。消融分析显示，溶质特异性稳定作用对准确性的贡献最大，而氢键相容性的重要程度取决于溶解机制。实验比较中，该模型将溶解度误差的几何平均倍数从传统计算方法的23倍以上降至约1.5倍。尽管未使用分级数据训练，模型仍能区分对玉米秸秆中木质素和半纤维素去除效果较高或较低的溶剂配方。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将溶剂化热力学与分子相互作用纳入神经网络，有助于提升深共熔溶剂溶解度预测的准确性与泛化能力，并可支持木质纤维素分级的化学机理导向溶剂筛选。

Abstract: Deep eutectic solvents (DESs) offer a tunable platform for lignocellulose refining, but their solvation behavior depends on multicomponent hydrogen-bond networks rather than individual constituent properties. We develop TiNN, a thermodynamics-informed neural network that hierarchically represents HBA-HBD association and solute accommodation through a learned free-energy pathway. Trained on 938 records, TiNN achieves a test-set $R^2$ of 0.917 and generalizes to new HBA-HBD combinations and sparse conditions. Ablation identifies solute-specific stabilization as the largest contributor to prediction accuracy, while the importance of hydrogen-bond compatibility depends on the dissolution mechanism. Experimental measurements of glucose, xylan, and Kraft lignin in acidic, basic, and neutral DESs show that TiNN reduces the geometric mean solubility error from more than 23-fold with COSMO-RS to approximately 1.5-fold. Without training on fractionation data, TiNN also distinguishes DES formulations with high and low lignin and hemicellulose removal from corn stover. These results support chemically grounded solvent screening for lignocellulose fractionation.

</details>


### [149] [A trust layer for fast pedestal-stability surrogates: calibrated error bars, out-of-distribution alarms and few-shot recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06597)
*Amin Vakhshouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文为等离子体稳定性代理模型设计了通用可信度层，结合误差区间、分布外预警、疑难样本回退至物理模拟，以及新装置数据再校准；实验显示，这些机制能缓解分布变化带来的可靠性问题，并显著加快部分筛查任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习代理模型虽能快速预测线性剥离—气球模稳定性，却通常只给出点预测，无法说明预测误差，也难以识别训练分布之外的输入。对设计新型聚变装置而言，相关平衡态往往正是训练数据未覆盖的情形，因此需要评估和管理代理模型的可信度，而不只是追求预测精度。

Method: 提出一种不依赖特定模型结构的可信度层，包含经共形方法构造的误差区间、用于识别分布外输入的预警机制，以及将可疑样本转交物理模拟程序处理的流程；还可利用新装置的少量模拟结果重新校准。研究在预先登记的假设下，使用理想磁流体增长率数据、抗磁阻尼模拟代理模型，以及来自多个装置与线圈配置的平衡态进行测试，并检查自建代理模型的输入问题。

Result: 在类似杰特装置的公开数据中，误差区间在同分布测试下达到目标覆盖率，但等离子体形状变化后覆盖率下降；加入几十个新形状模拟样本进行再校准，或将预警标记的样本转交物理程序后，覆盖率得以恢复。抗磁阻尼代理模型在留出模拟数据上保持校准，预警机制能识别训练中未出现的托卡马克装置，并将一个情景扫描的筛查时间从数小时缩短到数秒。对来自斯帕克模拟库和迪三维装置线圈组的未见平衡态，自建且使用分级模拟标签训练的代理模型优于基线；外部训练的代理模型则出现排序颠倒，并被预警机制判定不宜直接使用。可信度测试还发现自建神经算子模型的两处输入缺陷，修正后其性能差距缩小至接近简单网络。总体上，各代理模型的区间覆盖率接近目标，区间宽度则反映各自的误差水平；但并非所有预先登记的假设都得到支持。

Conclusion: 代理模型的可信部署需要同时处理误差量化、分布外识别、物理模拟回退和新设计再校准。该可信度层适用于多种代理模型，并在若干跨形状、跨装置测试中提升了可靠性与筛查效率。不过，效果依赖预警、校准数据及具体应用条件；论文也指出部分假设未获验证，不能将该方法视为消除所有外推风险的保证。

Abstract: Machine-learning surrogates of linear peeling-ballooning stability now run in milliseconds, but they are mostly deployed as point estimators: they do not say how wrong they may be, or when a query lies outside what they were trained on. For a fusion company designing a new device, every equilibrium of interest is outside the training data of existing surrogates, so this is the case that matters. We describe a model-agnostic trust layer for stability surrogates, made of conformal error bars, out-of-distribution alarms that route doubtful cases to the physics code, and recalibration from a few simulations of a new design, and we test it with pre-registered hypotheses. On a large public dataset of JET-like equilibria with ideal-MHD growth rates, the error bars hold their nominal coverage in distribution and fall short under a shift in plasma shape; a few dozen simulations of the new shape, or routing the cases the alarms flag, restore it. On resistive BOUT++ surrogates, the bars stay calibrated on held-out runs, the alarm flags tokamaks absent from training, and a scenario scan is screened in seconds instead of hours. Across equilibria from the SPARC library and the DIII-D coil set, a surrogate trained on our own graded BOUT++ labels beats the baseline on unseen equilibria, while a surrogate trained elsewhere ranks the same cases backwards and its alarm declines them. Building our own neural-operator surrogate, the harness exposed two input defects whose fix closed its gap to a simple network. For every surrogate tested, the trust layer keeps coverage near target, with a bar width set by that surrogate's own error. Not every pre-registered hypothesis held, and we report those results and the limits of all of them.

</details>


### [150] [The Behavioral Rate of Fine-Tuning: Compression as a Measure of Useful Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06598)
*Joey David,Paul Caillon,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出以压缩适配器保留微调行为所需的最小编码大小来衡量信息量，并发现这一压缩率取决于数据与冻结基座模型的组合；压缩还可能滤除噪声，使受污染训练得到的适配器反而更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 适配器的秩、参数量和权重重建误差，都无法直接说明微调行为需要多少信息才能保存。研究因此希望建立一种以实际行为保留能力为依据的衡量方式，并探明这种可压缩性由什么因素决定。

Method: 定义“行为率”：将适配器压缩并重新加载到冻结的基座模型后，仍能保留预设比例的留出集微调收益时，所需的最小序列化大小。研究在多组低秩适配微调实验中比较不同数据集和基座模型，并考察数据上的修正谱与压缩效果。

Result: 在优化预算固定时，训练内容越多，行为率总体越高；但它无法由数据描述长度、微调收益或权重重建误差等直观指标单独预测。同一数据集在不同基座模型上的行为率差异显著，甚至排序反转，说明可压缩性取决于数据与接收模型的配对；在同一模型内，数据上的修正谱则能对行为率排序。对受污染数据训练的适配器，压缩后在数学基准上的表现可从低于基座模型提升至百分之二十三点七至百分之八十二点九，而基座模型为百分之七十七点六。受污染适配器的损害集中于领先奇异方向，压缩似乎能滤除这些方向上的噪声；在更难的数学任务上，压缩后的适配器保留了基座模型百分之九十七的准确率，干净微调适配器则仅保留百分之四十四。

Conclusion: 适配器压缩率应被视为数据与冻结基座模型共同决定的行为属性，而非数据本身的固有属性。以行为保留为目标的压缩不仅能衡量微调所写入的信息，还可能抑制噪声并改善跨任务泛化。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapts a frozen language model through a compact learned update, making it a natural setting for asking how much information fine-tuning needs to write. Yet adapter rank, parameter count, or numerical reconstruction error do not directly tell us how many bits are required to preserve the acquired behavior. We therefore define an operational behavioral rate $R^\star$ as the smallest serialized size of a compressed adapter that, once reloaded into the frozen base model, retains a fixed fraction of its held-out fine-tuning gain. Across LoRA fine-tuning experiments, this rate increases with distinct training content at fixed optimization budgets, but is not determined by intuitive metrics like source description length, fine-tuning gain, or weight-space reconstruction error. Most importantly, the same datasets exhibit substantially different, and even reordered, rates across frozen base models, showing that fine-tuning compressibility is a property of the dataset--receiver pair rather than of the dataset alone. However, we show that correction spectra measured on the data are able to rank these rates within a receiver. Finally, we study a striking noise-reducing effect of compression: adapters trained on deliberately corrupted datasets fall far below the frozen model, yet after compression score above it (23.7% to 82.9% on GSM8K, against 77.6% for frozen Qwen2.5-7B), consistently across tested families and sizes. The damage is unevenly concentrated in the adapter's leading singular directions, which compression appears to filter. Away from its training task, the compressed corrupted adapter also generalizes better than a clean fine-tune: on harder math it retains 97% of base accuracy, versus 44% for the clean adapter.

</details>


### [151] [Verifier-Guided Self-Consistency: Test-Time Reasoning with Imperfect Generators and Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06606)
*Ekaterina Fadeeva,Xueyan Li,Mrinmaya Sachan,Maxim Panov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种结合步骤正确性与未来结果估计的随机解码方法，在生成过程中引导推理，并通过多条轨迹的答案级自洽性提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的多步推理容易出现看似合理却最终错误的中间步骤。现有方法难以兼顾生成器的多样性与验证器的正确性引导：基于验证器加权投票的方法只能筛选已有轨迹，无法引导生成；束搜索式方法虽能引导生成，却可能过度集中于少数轨迹，并放大验证器错误。

Method: 提出验证器引导自洽解码框架。生成过程中，模型反复提出下一步候选，并结合步骤级正确性信号和对未来结果的估计，通过接受或重新采样来引导轨迹；同时保留多条推理轨迹，最终依据答案级自洽性进行判断。论文还将该方法解释为对经验证器调整的轨迹分布进行近似采样。

Result: 在多个推理基准、不同生成模型和不同验证器类型上的实验表明，该方法的平均准确率高于具有代表性的基线方法。摘要未提供具体数值或逐项对比结果。

Conclusion: 将局部步骤判断与未来结果估计结合，并以随机采样方式引导生成，有望兼顾推理多样性和正确性，缓解单纯投票无法干预生成、或束搜索容易集中并累积验证器误差的问题。

Abstract: Large language models can solve many multi-step reasoning problems, but their reasoning remains brittle: plausible intermediate steps often lead to incorrect answers. Improving reasoning at test time requires balancing two imperfect signals: the generator, which provides diversity, and the verifier, which guides correctness. Existing approaches largely favor one side of this tradeoff. Verifier-weighted voting relies on trajectories sampled from the generator and cannot steer generation toward correct ones. By contrast, beam-style verifier-guided search can steer generation, but may over-concentrate on narrow trajectory sets and amplify verifier errors.
  We propose Verifier-Guided Self-Consistency (VGSC), a stochastic decoding framework that combines step-level correctness and future-outcome estimates to guide reasoning during generation. VGSC repeatedly proposes candidate next steps from the base language model and uses both signals in an acceptance/resampling procedure, while maintaining multiple trajectories for final answer-level self-consistency. We provide a probabilistic interpretation of VGSC as approximate sampling from a verifier-tilted trajectory distribution. Across multiple reasoning benchmarks, generator models, and verifier families, VGSC achieves higher average accuracy than representative baselines.

</details>


### [152] [From Reconstruction to Imputation: Test-Time Optimization for Missing Time-Series Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06612)
*Björn Ludwig,Alexander Windmann,René Heesch,Oliver Niggemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需重新训练的推理时适配方法：冻结已训练的序列重建模型，将缺失值作为优化变量，并依据已观测位置的重建误差进行迭代修正。该方法在多数数据集与模型组合上优于直接重建，但耗时较多，且效果取决于通道间的信息耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整传感器通道失效会导致整段序列缺失，无法依靠局部插值；针对每种缺失模式单独训练插补器又不够实际。论文探讨能否将未采用人工掩码增强训练的序列重建模型冻结后，在推理阶段转化为插补器。

Method: 对每个测试窗口，将缺失位置的数值设为可优化变量，同时固定所有已观测值。通过冻结的重建模型反向传播，仅最小化已观测位置上的重建误差，使缺失值逐步适配当前窗口。论文以缺失输入到已观测残差的交叉雅可比刻画该优化何时能提供有效信息，并在这种耦合较弱时可选用时间平滑先验。

Result: 在五个真实数据集、四种重建架构的实验中，每个测试窗口合成遮蔽一个完整通道。不使用先验时，推理时修正在二十组数据集与模型组合中的十九组降低了平均绝对误差。与采用结构化掩码或匹配的整通道丢弃训练的掩码感知插补器相比，最佳修正模型在两个数据集上误差最低，在另外三个数据集上则不及最强基线。该方法显著增加推理耗时，且对通道间耦合敏感。

Conclusion: 基于已观测值自洽性的推理时优化，可作为冻结重建模型的一种有条件适配方式，无需重新训练或进行人工掩码增强。它能够补充而非取代掩码感知插补方法，其收益取决于观测残差能否为缺失值提供足够信息，并伴随额外计算成本。

Abstract: Complete sensor-channel failures create a difficult form of time-series missingness: local interpolation is unavailable, and deploying a separately trained imputer for every failure pattern may be impractical. We ask whether a sequence reconstructor trained without artificial mask augmentation and then frozen can be converted into an imputer at inference time. Our method treats the missing entries of each test window as optimization variables, clamps all observations, and backpropagates through the frozen model to minimize reconstruction error only on observed coordinates. The resulting observed-self-consistency objective requires neither artificial mask augmentation nor full-channel dropout during reconstructor training. We characterize when this refinement is informative through the cross-Jacobian from missing inputs to observed residuals, and use an optional temporal-smoothness prior when that coupling is weak. Across five real-world datasets with exactly one channel synthetically masked per test window and four reconstruction architectures, no-prior test-time refinement reduces MAE relative to one-pass reconstruction in 19 of 20 dataset-backbone combinations. Compared with mask-aware imputers trained under either standard structured masks or matched full-channel dropout, the best refined reconstructor achieves the lowest MAE on two datasets and trails the strongest baseline on three. These gains require substantially more inference time and are sensitive to cross-channel coupling. Our results establish test-time self-consistency as a conditional inference-time adapter for frozen reconstruction models that complements mask-aware imputation without retraining the reconstructor.

</details>


### [153] [Hallucination Is Linearly Decodable from Mid-Layer Hidden States in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02628)
*Aizierjiang Aiersilan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，开源大语言模型的隐藏状态中存在近似线性可分的真实性信号；在单个中间层使用线性探针即可有效识别幻觉，而不同检测方法的表现取决于其可获取的信息与评估方式是否匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在检验开源大语言模型是否在隐藏状态中编码了可用于区分真实与虚假内容的信号，并确定该信号在哪些网络层最强。作者也比较隐藏状态探针与采样、特征值及注意力熵等检测方法，以厘清它们在同一评估设定下的相对效果。

Method: 作者选取三种经指令微调、参数规模约为七十亿至八十亿的模型，并以四位量化方式加载。在四个幻觉评测数据集上提取各层隐藏状态，比较线性探针、多层感知机探针、基于特征值的检测、生成结果自一致性和注意力熵。评估采用留出数据划分，并分析各模型的最佳探测层。

Result: 单层线性探针在留出数据上的受试者工作特征曲线下面积达到零点九零四至一，明显高于采样类检测方法在同一评估设定下不超过零点五四一的表现。多层感知机通常仅比线性探针高至多零点零一，表明真实性信号近似线性可分。自然语言基准上的最佳层位置较稳定：拉玛与米斯特拉模型约在第三十二层网络的第十三至第十八层，昆文模型约在第二十八层网络的第十九至第二十五层。知识依托任务中，首层注意力熵也提供互补信号，在幻觉问答数据集上的面积为零点八六六至零点九四一，且无需额外推理成本。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态探针，尤其是中间层的线性探针，是该研究设定下识别真实性信号的有效方法。采样方法表现较弱，作者认为这主要源于成对标签评估与这些方法所利用的信息之间存在结构性不匹配，并非采样方法本身必然无效。研究同时提供代码与数据，支持在单张八吉字节显存的图形处理器上复现。

Abstract: We investigate whether open-source LLMs encode a linearly separable truthfulness signal in their hidden states, and at which network depth this signal is strongest. Across three $7$B--$8$B instruction-tuned models (Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, Qwen2.5-7B) loaded in $4$-bit NF4 quantization, we extract per-layer hidden states on four hallucination benchmarks (TruthfulQA, HaluEval-QA, FEVER, and a controlled synthetic set) and compare four detection approaches: linear and MLP probes, INSIDE EigenScore, self-consistency, and attention entropy. A linear probe on a single mid-network layer achieves $0.904$--$1.000$ AUROC on held-out splits, while sampling-based detectors do not exceed $0.541$ AUROC under the same protocol. The truthfulness signal is approximately linear: MLP probes rarely surpass linear probes by more than $0.01$ AUROC. Peak probing layers fall in a consistent band across model families on natural-language benchmarks -- blocks~$13$--$18$ of~$32$ for Llama and Mistral, and blocks~$19$--$25$ of~$28$ for Qwen. First-block attention entropy provides a complementary signal in knowledge-grounded settings ($0.866$--$0.941$ AUROC on HaluEval-QA) at no additional inference cost. The low discriminability of sampling methods under this protocol reflects a structural mismatch between paired-label evaluation and the information these methods access, rather than an inherent limitation of those methods. Code and data are released for full reproducibility on a single $8$\,GB GPU.

</details>


### [154] [Dimension-Free Private Mean Estimation is Impossible Without the Covariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06615)
*Renaud Gaucher,Ephraim Linder,Adam Smith,Jonathan Ullman,Lydia Zakynthinou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明：在差分隐私下，协方差未知会显著削弱高斯均值估计对低维或各向异性结构的利用能力，导致误差相较协方差已知时出现依赖环境维数的额外代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，若协方差矩阵已知，差分隐私均值估计的最优样本复杂度可以只依赖协方差的谱性质，而不必依赖环境维数。论文关注协方差未知时这种优势是否仍能实现，并研究未知协方差带来的估计误差限制。

Method: 作者针对协方差未知的高斯均值估计建立欧几里得误差下界，并按样本量与有效维数的关系分析两种情形。有效维数定义为协方差迹与其算子范数之比；第二种情形还考虑有效维数至多为对数级的对角协方差矩阵。

Result: 当样本量小于有效维数时，协方差未知问题的最优误差可能比协方差已知时高出至少一个与环境维数和样本量相关的因子，量级为维数与样本量之比的平方根。当样本量大于有效维数时，即使协方差为对角矩阵且有效维数至多为维数对数级，未知协方差下的最优误差仍可能高出一个量级为环境维数平方根除以样本量三分之三次方的因子。

Conclusion: 差分隐私下，协方差未知并非只带来轻微代价：即使数据具有低有效维数或明显的各向异性，利用这类结构仍可能需要承担依赖环境维数的误差或样本复杂度。

Abstract: We study the limits of differentially private Gaussian mean estimation when the covariance matrix is unknown. Prior work shows that when the covariance $Σ$ is known, the optimal sample complexity can depend only on its spectrum, rather than on the ambient dimension $d$. We establish new lower bounds for Gaussian mean estimation with Euclidean error which show that private estimators cannot achieve similar guarantees when the covariance is unknown. First, when the sample size $n$ is smaller than the effective dimension $\mathrm{trace}(Σ)/\|Σ\|_{\text{op}}$, the optimal error when the covariance is unknown can be larger than the optimal error when it is known by a factor of $\sqrt{d/n}$. Second, when the sample size is larger than the effective dimension, even for diagonal covariance matrices with effective dimension bounded by $\log d$, the optimal error when the covariance is unknown can be larger than the optimal error when it is known by a factor of $\sqrt{d}/n^{3/2}$. Together, these results show that in the unknown covariance setting, exploiting anisotropic structure under differential privacy can require a dimension-dependent error or sample complexity.

</details>


### [155] [Fingerprinting the Dispatchers behind Distributed Model Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06661)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 仅利用请求到达与流完成时间，即可识别并排序大规模模型窃取活动中的关联账户；固定密钥虽可规避检测，但可能显著降低吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型窃取活动会把请求分散到数千个负载均衡账户中，借此掩盖流量并逃过常规内容分类器。论文指出，内容之外的请求时序可能暴露账户共享的调度基础设施，因此可作为取证线索。

Method: 分析请求到达时间和数据流完成时间，从时序中推断轮询计数器、密钥轮换池等请求分发策略。研究以百分之二十的种子账户为已知样本，对活动中其余账户进行关联排序，并考察攻击者采用随机抖动或固定密钥时的规避效果。

Result: 在二十分钟内，该方法对剩余账户的识别排序达到零点九二至一点零零的曲线下面积表现，并能扩展到数千个真实生产账户。随机抖动难以掩盖轮换式调度；固定密钥能够成功规避检测，但在配额受限时会严重压低吞吐量。

Conclusion: 即使内容过滤器无法发现异常，请求时序仍可揭示活动账户之间共享的调度模式与基础设施关联。时序取证因此能补充基于内容的检测，但攻击者可通过固定密钥规避，代价可能是效率下降。

Abstract: API distillation campaigns try to hide model-stealing traffic by splitting requests across thousands of load-balanced accounts, which standard content classifiers often miss. However, request timestamps, specifically arrival time and stream completion time, reveal the underlying dispatching strategy, such as round-robin counters or key-shuffling pools. Using a 20 percent seed set, this timing analysis can forensically rank the remaining accounts of a campaign with 0.92 to 1.00 AUC within 20 minutes, even across thousands of real production accounts. While adaptive attackers struggle to hide rotating dispatchers using jitter, pinning keys successfully evades detection, though it can severely throttle throughput when quotas bind. Ultimately, timing exposes shared infrastructure patterns even when content filters fail.

</details>


### [156] [Don't Replace, Complement: A Detector-Agnostic Subspace Signal for Near-OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06688)
*Wonseok Shin,Songkuk Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种按类别构建的低维子空间信号，可作为现有分布外检测分数的补充，尤其提升近分布外检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后处理分布外检测方法在远分布外检测上较成熟，但对近分布外样本仍较弱。近分布外样本与训练分布内样本会相似地激活其预测类别的特征方向，因此，针对远分布外设计的检测分数难以区分近分布外样本；单一分数也可能难以同时兼顾两种检测场景。

Method: 为每个类别从稀疏自编码器中选出激活程度最高的若干神经元，并将对应的解码器方向正交化，形成低维子空间。对输入样本，依据其预测类别计算该子空间内经白化的马氏距离，作为补充信号。该信号与经过校准的基础检测分数相加融合，使用固定的全局权重；校准统计量仅由分布内验证数据估计，不针对不同模型或数据集单独调参。

Result: 在涵盖多种方法类别的18种检测器上，该补充信号在126种检测器与骨干网络组合中均提升了近分布外检测的曲线下面积。远分布外检测表现总体维持或提升；仅在基础分数已接近最优时出现幅度很小的下降。

Conclusion: 按类别低维子空间信号能够补充现有检测器对近分布外样本的敏感性，并在总体上保留远分布外检测能力。其固定权重和仅依赖分布内数据的校准方式，使其具有跨检测器、跨骨干网络应用的潜力。

Abstract: Post-hoc out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is mature on far-OOD yet remains weak on near-OOD. These two settings differ in their use of class-characteristic feature directions: far-OOD inputs largely fail to activate those of their predicted class, whereas near-OOD inputs activate them much as in-distribution samples do. Because the axis separating far-OOD leaves near-OOD almost indistinguishable from ID, a score optimized for one setting is misaligned with the other; where a detector already saturates far-OOD, added near-OOD sensitivity is bought partly against it. This divergence motivates a targeted near-OOD signal designed to complement existing scores. We propose PLUS (Per-class Low-dimensional sUbspace Signal). For each class, PLUS selects the $m$ most class-active neurons from a sparse autoencoder, orthonormalizes the corresponding decoder directions to form a low-dimensional subspace, and scores an input by its per-class whitened squared Mahalanobis distance $z$ within the subspace of its predicted class. Designed as a dedicated complement rather than a standalone detector, the signal $z$ is fused additively with any calibrated base score at a single, fixed weight. This weight is a single global constant: no value is adjusted per backbone, per OOD dataset, or per deployment, and all calibration statistics are estimated from ID validation data alone. When applied to 18 existing detectors spanning diverse method families, $z$ consistently improves near-OOD AUROC in all 126 detector--backbone combinations. Far-OOD performance is broadly maintained or improved, with only marginal declines where the base score is near-optimal, making $z$ a robust, detector-agnostic complement across backbones.

</details>


### [157] [Self-Directed Apple Tasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06677)
*Tanmay Devale,Reed Phillips,Arvind Ramaswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究自定向苹果品尝问题，刻画了可实现与非可实现情形下最坏情形错误数或遗憾的增长率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 苹果品尝是一种部分反馈学习问题：学习者只有预测为正类时才能观察真实标签。已有工作研究了在线对抗设定下的刻画，而本文关注学习者能够自适应选择预测样本顺序的自定向设定，旨在揭示不同假设类复杂度如何决定学习难度。

Method: 在可实现情形下，作者依据维度相关的组合参数——VC维、空集中心星数和自定向维度——对任意学习者的期望极小极大错误数进行分类。在非可实现情形下，则依据VC维分析极小极大遗憾的增长率。

Result: 可实现情形下，错误数呈现四种可能的增长率：Θ(1)、介于有界常数与对数增长之间的 O(log n) 且 ω(1)、约为 √n（忽略对数因子），或 Θ(n)。非可实现情形下，极小极大遗憾依VC维而为约 √n（忽略对数因子）或 Θ(n)。

Conclusion: 自定向选择样本顺序会使苹果品尝问题呈现多种截然不同的学习难度。可实现情形需要结合多种维度参数才能刻画错误率；非可实现情形的遗憾则由VC维区分为次线性与线性两类。

Abstract: We study the classical apple-tasting setting, a partial feedback model in which the learner observes the true label only when it predicts $1$. A combinatorial characterization for the rates of this problem in the online adversarial setting has been studied by Raman et al. (2024). We study the self-directed setting, in which the learner can adaptively choose the order of the points it predicts on. In the realizable setting, we establish a tetrachotomy on the expected minimax number of mistakes made by any learner. Depending on the VC dimension, the $\emptyset$-centered star number, and the self-directed dimension, we show that the rates could possibly be $Θ(1)$, $O(\log n) \cap ω(1)$, $\tilde Θ(\sqrt{n})$, or $Θ(n)$. In the agnostic setting, the minimax regret is $\tilde Θ(\sqrt{n})$ or $Θ(n)$, depending on the VC dimension.

</details>


### [158] [FedFiOT: Fisher-Weighted Optimal Transport for Model Merging in One-Shot and Few-Round Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06783)
*Quan Minh Nguyen,Hoang M. Ngo,Bhakti Chowkwale,Yasaman Keshtkarjahromi,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合费舍尔信息与最优传输的联邦学习框架，用于改善单轮及少轮联邦学习中的神经元错配与参数等权聚合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮或少轮联邦学习能降低通信开销，但因客户端间缺少频繁同步，模型中的相似神经元可能位于不同位置；同时，数据异质性使参数对各客户端预测的重要程度不同。直接平均因此可能合并错位神经元，并忽视参数重要性差异，导致性能下降。

Method: 提出费舍尔加权最优传输框架，利用费舍尔信息衡量参数重要性，并据此指导神经元匹配与全局模型聚合。该方法通过交替最小化一个共享的费舍尔加权目标，联合优化神经元对应关系和全局模型参数。理论上证明其优化过程可使逐层合并目标单调下降，并给出刻画客户端异质性与费舍尔权重非均匀性影响的驻点界。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在单轮和少轮联邦学习设置中均持续优于对比基线。摘要未提供具体数据集、基线名称或性能提升幅度。

Conclusion: 通过同时考虑神经元对齐和参数重要性加权，该框架能够缓解低通信频率联邦学习中的模型错配与简单平均局限，并在实验和理论分析中展现出优势。

Abstract: One-shot and few-round federated learning (FL) reduce communication burden by constructing a global model within one or a few communication rounds, but often achieve lower performance than conventional multi-round FL. Without frequent synchronization, client models may drift apart, causing neurons that learn similar features to occupy different positions across clients. At the same time, local data heterogeneity can cause corresponding parameters to differ in their importance to each client's predictions. Together, these challenges cause direct averaging to combine mismatched neurons and assign equal weight to parameters despite differences in their importance. We propose FedFiOT, a Fisher-weighted optimal transport framework for one-shot and few-round FL that leverages Fisher information to quantify parameter importance and guide neuron alignment and model aggregation. FedFiOT jointly optimizes neuron correspondences and global model parameters through alternating minimization of a shared Fisher-weighted objective. Theoretically, we show that FedFiOT's optimization procedure monotonically decreases the layer-wise merging objective and establish a stationarity bound that quantifies the effects of client heterogeneity and nonuniform Fisher weighting.Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over competing baselines in both one-shot and few-round federated learning settings.

</details>


### [159] [Do unlearning algorithms learn the right features?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06681)
*Polina Dolgova,Amartya Sanyal,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，仅比较删除后模型与重训练模型的输出，可能掩盖两者内部特征表示的差异；这些差异会影响后续学习与迁移适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘通常用删除数据后模型与仅用剩余数据重新训练的模型之间的输出差异来评估。然而，输出相似并不意味着模型学到的特征相同，因而现有评估可能遗漏影响后续训练和应用的重要差异。

Method: 论文从理论上证明：若遗忘模型与重训练模型输出一致但所编码的特征不同，只需对预测头进行一次梯度更新，两者的输出一致性就可能被打破；即使删除的数据比例任意小，重训练也可能选择性质不同的特征。作者提出特征恢复度量，分别衡量缺失特征与多余特征，并在八种遗忘方法上进行评估，同时考察后续再次学习和下游适应的表现。

Result: 实验发现，不少在传统指标上表现良好的遗忘模型，仍未能恢复重训练模型所具有的特征。特征不匹配能够预测模型在后续再次学习及下游适应时偏离重训练结果的程度；直接纠正这种不匹配，可系统性地减少偏离。

Conclusion: 评估机器遗忘不能只看删除后的静态输出，还应检查模型内部特征是否与重训练结果相符。特征层面的恢复既揭示了传统评估遗漏的问题，也为改善遗忘模型在后续学习和迁移中的行为提供了有效方向。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is commonly evaluated with standard post-deletion metrics that compare an unlearned model to one retrained on the remaining data. Such snapshot evaluations can conceal consequential differences in the features the two models encode. We prove that if an unlearned and a retrained model agree on their outputs but encode different features, a single gradient step on their prediction heads can break this agreement. We further show that deleting an arbitrarily small fraction of the data can cause retraining to select qualitatively different features, so matching retraining can require learning features that the full model never had. We then introduce a measure of feature recovery that separates missing from extraneous features. Across eight unlearning methods, models that perform well on standard metrics often fail to recover the retrained features. This mismatch predicts deviations from retraining under subsequent relearning and downstream adaptation, and directly correcting it systematically reduces these deviations.

</details>


### [160] [Transforming Integer Grids to Spatial Intuition: Perception, Not Planning, Governs ARC-AGI-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06793)
*Harsidak Singh Banwait*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向陌生视觉交互环境的智能体：通过快速提取物体、几何关系及时间变化，并以程序化动作进行探索，显著提升解题效率；结果表明，空间感知和主动验证是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在陌生环境中高效展现流体智力，要求智能体迅速建立环境模型。但将画面编码为整数网格会破坏空间结构，使模型浪费大量操作重新发现边界和对称性。论文据此提出，交互推理的瓶颈可能在空间感知，而非规划能力。

Method: 提出一种开放权重智能体，使用无需训练参数、复杂度为画面像素数线性阶的感知引擎，在约三十五毫秒内将画面转化为物体清单、跨帧二部图跟踪结果、几何说明和标注画布。说明中区分已证实的不变量与待验证的可供性假设。智能体结合代码即动作的运行机制，以算法假设生成操作，而非逐步输出低层动作；对于单帧无法确定的交互属性，则通过主动探测加以验证。

Result: 在八种展示评测中，加入有依据的空间感知后，评分由二十八分中的三分升至二十五分，令牌用量减少百分之八十九点四。在二十三个留出游戏中，几何不变量的泛化准确率为百分之百；单帧可供性猜测准确率仅为百分之六至五十，说明仍需主动验证。在公开关卡上，关闭说明的运行解出一百八十三关中的一百四十三关，耗时八点二小时，单次通过效率为百分之五十九点四；启用说明后效率升至百分之六十三点五。在单张高端图形处理器、九小时预算下，隐藏评测得分为十六点五三，是首届里程碑获胜方案的十三点七倍。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持以结构化空间感知取代原始网格序列化：明确的物体与几何表示能减少无效探索、降低计算开销，并提升陌生游戏中的泛化表现。不过，稳定的几何规律不能替代对交互机制的检验；可靠的智能体仍须结合主动探测来确认可供性。

Abstract: Sample-efficient fluid intelligence requires agents that rapidly form working models of unfamiliar interactive environments. ARC-AGI-3 tests this across uninstructed visual games scored by Relative Human Action Efficiency, which penalizes exploratory waste quadratically. When frontier models read raw integer grids, serialization destroys spatial topology, and they spend hundreds of actions rediscovering basic boundaries and symmetries. We introduce SARBLOH, an open-weight agent built on the premise that spatial perception, not planning capacity, governs interactive reasoning on ARC-AGI-3. A zero-parameter O(HW) perception engine turns each frame, in 35 ms, into an object inventory with bipartite temporal tracking, a geometric briefing that separates proven invariants from affordance hypotheses, and an annotated canvas. Paired with a code-as-action runtime, the agent writes algorithmic hypotheses instead of low-level moves. Across eight presentations, grounded perception raises the rubric score from 3/28 to 25/28 points while cutting token use by 89.4%. On 23 held-out games, geometric invariants generalize with 100% accuracy, while single-frame affordance guesses fall to 6-50%, so active verification probes remain necessary. On the public set, Run A (briefing off) solved 143 of 183 levels in 8.2 hours at 59.4% single-pass efficiency; Run B (briefing on) reached 63.5%. Within the nine-hour budget on one Kaggle RTX PRO 6000 GPU, SARBLOH scored 16.53 on the hidden Kaggle evaluation, 13.7x the first Milestone winner.

</details>


### [161] [SecureGARD: Dynamic Ontology-Guided Governance for Secure Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06682)
*Guoqin Xiong,Shengtao Wen,Xiang Chen,Xinyi Zeng,Zhaoxin Fan,Wei Ye,Xiaofeng Cao,Sheng-Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该框架通过共享语义表示、可执行本体与任务状态，判定工具调用是可执行、待补足还是禁止；在三项基准测试中，攻击成功率均为零，同时提升了攻击条件下的任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型大语言模型会将输出转化为实际操作，因此间接提示注入不再只是生成内容风险，而是执行安全问题。现有防御常限制工具调用、执行轨迹或信息流，却未充分明确动作语义、状态依赖和证据关系，难以适配不同工具，也不利于安全更新。

Method: 该框架以共享语义表示统一异构工具调用，并结合可执行本体和单次任务状态，确定性地将动作判定为可执行、等待或禁止。等待表示存在尚未满足但可补救的义务，允许在不扩大授权范围的前提下有限重新规划。本体在离线阶段根据经过验证的训练执行结果演化：模型提出与具体实例无关的动作—状态规则，只有在规则能修正训练失败暴露的本体错误、保持逻辑一致性和受保护推论，并通过留出验证后才会被采纳；随后冻结本体。

Result: 在三项基准测试的良性及间接提示注入场景下，该框架的攻击成功率均为零，并且相较现有基线，在遭受攻击时取得更高的任务效用。

Conclusion: 显式的语义治理能够增强工具型智能体的执行安全，同时避免不加区分地拦截操作。通过明确表达动作、状态及义务关系，该方法也为跨工具泛化和可控的安全更新提供了基础。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents translate model outputs into consequential actions, turning indirect prompt injection into an execution-security problem. Existing defenses constrain tool calls, trajectories, or information flows, but often leave action semantics, state dependencies, and evidence relations implicit, limiting generalization across heterogeneous tools and complicating safe updates. We present \ours{}, a dynamic ontology-guided governance framework. \ours{} grounds heterogeneous calls in a shared semantic representation and combines an executable ontology with episode-level task state to derive deterministic ready, waiting, and forbidden decisions. A waiting decision identifies an unmet but recoverable obligation, enabling bounded replanning without expanding the agent's authorization boundary. \ours{} evolves its ontology offline from verified training executions. An offline LLM proposes instance-independent action--state axioms, which are accepted only if they address ontology errors exposed by training failures, preserve logical consistency and protected entailments, and pass held-out validation before the ontology is frozen. We evaluate \ours{} on AgentDojo, ASB, and AgentDyn under benign and indirect prompt-injection settings. \ours{} achieves a 0\% attack success rate (ASR) across all three benchmarks while improving task utility under attack compared with existing baselines. These results show that explicit semantic governance can strengthen execution security without indiscriminate blocking.

</details>


### [162] [Message-passing graph persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06801)
*Jorge Luiz Franco,Arthur Queiroz Moura,Moacir Ponti,Amauri Souza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将消息传递融入图过滤过程的拓扑描述方法，在保持图同构不变性的同时增强表达能力；实验显示其在多数测试数据集上优于其他基于持久同调的描述方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递图神经网络的表达能力受一维魏斯费勒—莱曼测试限制。持久同调能够提供互补的拓扑信息，但既有方法通常独立于表示学习过程计算持久特征，未能充分结合图结构变化与节点表示。

Method: 提出消息传递图持久性方法族。在过滤过程的每个步骤中，依据当前子图更新节点嵌入，并将嵌入与持久性事件关联；同时利用这些嵌入确定持久同调计算中连通分量的代表元。论文还证明该方法对图同构保持不变，且表达能力严格强于其扩展的持久同调描述方法。

Result: 在表达能力基准以及多个图分类和图回归数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多数数据集上优于其他基于持久同调的描述方法。摘要未提供具体数值或显著性检验结果。

Conclusion: 将消息传递嵌入图过滤与持久性计算，可构造兼具拓扑信息和学习表示的图描述符。理论分析支持其同构不变性与更强表达能力，实验结果也显示出相对既有持久同调方法的优势。

Abstract: Message-passing graph neural networks (GNNs) are limited in expressive power by the one-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman test. Persistent homology (PH) provides topological descriptors that can complement GNN representations, but existing approaches typically compute persistence separately from the representation learning process. In this paper, we introduce Message-Passing Graph Persistence (MGP), a family of topological descriptors that incorporates message passing into graph filtrations. At each filtration step, node embeddings are updated using the current subgraph and associated with persistence events. These embeddings also determine the representatives of connected components during persistence computation. We prove that MGP is invariant under graph isomorphism and that it is strictly more expressive than the PH-based descriptors it extends. We evaluate MGP on an expressivity benchmark and on several graph classification and regression datasets. Experiments show that MGP outperforms other PH-based descriptors on most of these datasets.

</details>


### [163] [PINR-ESI: Physics-Informed Self-Supervised Implicit Neural Representation Framework for EEG Extended-Source Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06700)
*Zhiyong Zhou,Sheng Liao,Youshen Xiao,Xiaolin Li,Yakang Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合物理模型与自监督隐式神经表征的脑电扩展源成像方法，无需配对源标签或基于仿真的监督预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展源成像需要定位活跃皮层区域并刻画其空间范围。传统监督反演网络依赖合成脑电与源标签，可能因合成数据和真实数据之间存在差异而性能下降，因此需要减少对监督训练数据的依赖。

Method: 将皮层电流密度表示为由空间坐标条件化的连续场，并利用导联场矩阵刻画源到传感器的体积传导关系，通过预测脑电与实测脑电的一致性进行自监督约束。方法以经典反演结果提供初始化及源活动的空间约束，再通过区域积分投影将连续场转换为离散源系数，以进行正向建模。

Result: 在不同噪声水平、源数量和源范围的模拟实验中，该方法在识别活跃皮层区域方面表现突出，尤其适用于多个源和范围较大的源。癫痫患者真实脑电记录及颅内刺激实验的定量与定性评估也支持了该方法用于源成像的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合脑电物理模型的自监督隐式神经表征具有扩展源成像潜力，可在不依赖配对源标签或仿真监督预训练的情况下重建源活动。

Abstract: EEG extended-source imaging aims to localize active cortical regions and characterize their spatial extent, yet supervised inverse networks typically depend on synthetic EEG-source pairs and may be sensitive to synthetic-to-real mismatch. We propose a physics-informed self-supervised implicit neural representation (INR) framework for spatial source reconstruction without paired source labels or simulation-based supervised pretraining. The lead field matrix encodes the source-to-sensor volume-conduction model and constrains the INR through agreement between predicted and measured EEG. Cortical current density is modeled as a continuous coordinate-conditioned field. A classical inverse estimate provides warm initialization and spatial constraints on source activity, while patch-integrated projection maps the field to discrete source coefficients for forward modeling. Simulations with varying noise levels, source counts, and source extents showed the clearest advantage in identifying active cortical regions, particularly for multiple and larger sources. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations using real EEG recordings from epilepsy patients and intracranial stimulation experiments further supported the feasibility of the framework for source imaging. These findings suggest the potential of physics-informed self-supervised INR for extended-source imaging.

</details>


### [164] [Context--Answer Metamodels: Forecasting LLM Activations Before the First Answer Token](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06837)
*Tommy He,Thomas Jiralerspong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，仅凭模型读取上下文时产生的内部激活，就能较准确地预测其后续答案的内部激活及其变化，并可用于预测答案行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机解码会使同一上下文产生多种答案。要了解这种差异，通常需要进行大量昂贵的自回归生成，而每次生成只能观察一种结果。论文探讨能否直接利用读取上下文时产生的激活，提前预测后续答案的激活。

Method: 研究者训练了多种上下文—答案元模型，将上下文的激活序列映射为答案平均池化激活的点预测或分布预测。实验考察预测精度、对生成变化的刻画能力、训练数据规模的影响、跨数据集泛化、行为预测信息及跨模型迁移能力。

Result: 四类元模型都能高保真地预测答案平均池化激活，分布预测模型还能够捕捉不同生成结果之间的变化。训练数据增加至五十万个提示后，性能继续提升；模型可零样本泛化到域外数据集，预测结果保留了与答案行为相关的信息。此外，一个模型系列的上下文激活能够预测其他模型的答案激活，显示出跨模型迁移潜力。

Conclusion: 上下文激活轨迹可作为答案生成前的预测信号，用于规模化估计语言模型可能产生的答案表征及其变异。这为减少昂贵的重复生成、提前分析模型行为提供了新途径。

Abstract: Under stochastic decoding, a single context can elicit many different answers from a language model. Characterizing this variation typically requires many expensive autoregressive rollouts, each revealing only one realization. We ask whether the activations produced while reading the context can directly forecast the model's rollout activations. We train context--answer metamodels that map a context activation sequence to point or distribution forecasts of mean-pooled answer activations. We find that (i) the four metamodel families we study forecast mean-pooled answer activations with high fidelity, and the distribution metamodels also capture rollout variability; (ii) performance improves as the training set grows to 500,000 prompts, and the metamodels generalize zero-shot to out-of-domain datasets; (iii) their forecasts retain behavior-predictive signal; and (iv) context activations from Qwen2.5 predict mean-pooled answer activations from Qwen3.5 and Gemma 4, suggesting that activation forecasts can transfer across models. Together, these results suggest that context activation traces can support scalable forecasts of behaviors in LLM answers before the first answer token is generated.

</details>


### [165] [Where It Breaks: Diagnosing Perception, Memory, and Decision Failures of a Vision-Language Agent in Pac-Man](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06704)
*Xinglu Wang,Edward Wang,Yong Zhang,Zhenan Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，玩吃豆人表现不佳可能源于视觉感知或长序列决策；两者需要分别评估，单一训练成绩不足以判断智能体能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅使用上下左右四种动作训练智能体玩吃豆人时，若表现不佳，难以判断问题出在读懂画面，还是在连续决策。研究旨在区分这些误差来源，并检验训练与评估中可能被单一指标掩盖的问题。

Method: 研究以千问3.5-9B为基础构建智能体，通过独立的感知测试和基础策略强化学习实验分析其能力。策略实验采用文字形式的棋盘，排除图像转录对决策接口的影响；此外，还比较不同策略优化结果，并检查静态截图识别与实际交互中的表现差异。

Result: 在八个留出迷宫上，策略优化前通关率为0%，某个选定检查点的通关率提升至80.2%，设定为三条额外生命。感知测试显示，静态截图识别准确并不保证交互过程中同样准确；策略比较也表明，进一步优化可能伴随更差的动作选择。

Conclusion: 智能体训练后的评估应分别检验实际使用中的感知能力与决策能力，区分训练成功和完整游戏验证，并考察具体动作及后续表现，而不能仅依赖某个选定检查点的成绩。

Abstract: Training an agent to play Pac-Man with only up, down, left and right actions raises a basic question: when performance is poor, what should be improved? Difficulty can arise from reading the screen or from choosing actions over a long sequence. We examine these problems in a Qwen3.5-9B-based agent through separate perception checks and primitive-policy reinforcement-learning studies. In the policy study, which uses textual boards to remove image transcription from the decision interface, completion on eight held-out mazes increased from 0% before this optimization to 80.2% at a selected checkpoint, with three extra lives. The perception checks show that accurate static screenshot reading can coexist with errors during interaction. A separate policy comparison also shows that further optimization can accompany worse action choices. Together, these findings motivate concrete checks for post-training: evaluate perception in use, distinguish training success from full-game validation, and examine decisions and later performance rather than relying on one selected score. The study provides empirical lessons for improving future agent training and evaluation.

</details>


### [166] [Energy-Guided Transport for Projection-Free Physics-Informed Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06711)
*Giuliano Galadini,Aurelio Uncini,Danilo Comminiello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需重新训练的能量引导输运方法，通过连续耗散力将生成过程中的状态平滑地引向物理约束所定义的物理流形，在保持计算效率的同时提升生成稳定性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成模型通常只依据统计规律生成结果，不显式遵守控制物理系统的方程，因而推理时可能违反物理约束。已有免训练方法常用刚性的代数投影；将这类离散修正用于覆盖整个区域的偏微分方程时，可能扰动生成概率路径、使状态偏离模型学到的流形，并带来较高计算开销。

Method: 提出能量引导输运框架，将非线性物理约束表示为平滑的势能景观。在常微分方程积分过程中，方法不插入离散投影，而是直接对中间生成状态施加连续耗散力，使状态逐步靠近满足物理约束的流形。该力通过反向模式自动微分隐式计算；据摘要所述，算法复杂度为关于状态规模线性的量级，无需重新训练生成模型。

Result: 作者在多种一维和二维物理系统上进行评估，包括具有混沌特性的纳维–斯托克斯方程。摘要报告称，该方法能够稳定生成且不发生结构性发散；相较于现有方法，改善了稳定性与精度之间的权衡，并相较于精确投影策略显著加快推理。摘要未提供具体数值，因此无法进一步比较提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该工作以平滑、连续的能量引导替代刚性离散投影，为生成模型施加非线性物理约束提供了一种免训练方案。其主要价值在于兼顾物理一致性、生成过程稳定性和推理效率；不过，摘要未说明其在不同约束类型及更大规模系统上的适用边界。

Abstract: Deep generative models, particularly Flow Matching frameworks, have emerged as powerful tools for synthesizing complex physical systems. However, standard architectures remain purely statistically driven and agnostic to the underlying governing equations, often violating fundamental physical constraints during inference. Existing zero-shot approaches designed to enforce these constraints typically rely on rigid algebraic projections. While effective for sparse boundary conditions, applying such discrete geometric corrections to dense, full-domain PDEs may disrupt the probability path, \rev{potentially deviating from the learned manifold and introducing substantial computational overhead}. To address these challenges, we introduce Energy-Guided Transport, a novel training-free framework that formulates non-linear physical constraints as a smooth potential energy landscape. Rather than interrupting the ODE integration, our method applies a continuous dissipative force directly to the intermediate generative states, steering them toward the physical manifold. By evaluating this force implicitly via reverse-mode automatic differentiation, our framework preserves the natural dynamics of the flow while maintaining an efficient $\mathcal{O}(N)$ algorithmic complexity. We extensively evaluate our framework across various 1D and 2D physical systems, including the chaotic Navier-Stokes equations. Our method achieves stable generation without structural divergence, significantly improves the stability-accuracy trade-off compared to state-of-the-art methods, and substantially accelerates inference with respect to exact projection strategies. Code available at: https://github.com/ispamm/Projection-free-energy-guided-flow-matching.

</details>


### [167] [Symmetry Breaking at the Edge of Stability via Central Flow Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06718)
*Naima Elosegui Borras,Georgios Arvanitidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络在稳定性边缘附近的中心流动力学如何改变梯度流中的守恒量，并在三类两层模型中分析哪些守恒量保留、哪些发生漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续对称性会使梯度流具有守恒量，例如线性网络和整流线性单元网络中的层平衡性。然而，有限步长的梯度下降未必保留这些守恒律；尤其在稳定性边缘，参数迭代会围绕较平滑的轨迹振荡。因此，理解这类振荡如何影响原有守恒量，是分析稳定性边缘训练动力学的重要问题。

Method: 采用中心流动力学描述稳定性边缘附近的平滑轨迹，并考察曲率修正对梯度流守恒量的影响。研究表明，这种修正会产生有限维漂移，其大小由临界参数方向上的振荡协方差决定。作者通过标量两层模型、两层矩阵分解模型和两层整流线性单元网络进行分析；这些模型中的已知守恒量被证明是完备的。

Result: 分析刻画了中心流框架下守恒量的演化机制：曲率修正可使部分梯度流守恒量发生漂移，漂移受临界方向振荡协方差控制。研究由此初步区分了稳定性边缘处仍然保留的守恒量与被破坏的守恒量，并在三类模型中给出具体分析。

Conclusion: 梯度流中的连续对称性守恒律不能直接推广到稳定性边缘的有限步长训练。中心流中的曲率修正及其诱发的振荡协方差，是理解守恒量变化的关键。本文为分析这一问题迈出初步一步，但结论主要建立在所研究的两层模型上。

Abstract: Continuous symmetries in neural networks lead to conserved quantities that are invariant under gradient flow, such as layer balancedness in linear and ReLU networks. Finite-step gradient descent need not preserve these laws, particularly at the edge of stability (EOS), where the iterates oscillate around a smoother trajectory, which can be described by the central flow dynamics (Cohen et al., 2025). We study how gradient flow conservation laws evolve under the central flow description of this regime. We show that the curvature correction induces a finite-dimensional drift determined by the oscillation covariance along critical parameter directions. We thus take the first steps to analyse which conservation laws remain and are broken at the EOS according to the central flow dynamics by analysing a scalar two-layer model, two-layer matrix factorisation, and a two-layer ReLU network, where the known conservation laws are shown to be complete (Marcotte et al., 2023).

</details>


### [168] [Continual Classification Beyond the Kernel Regime: Harnessing the Sparsity Bias of L2 Regularization in Diagonal Linear Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06759)
*Gilad Karpel,Itay Evron,Ron Meir,Daniel Soudry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究高维情境下的持续学习分类，提出一种加权二范数正则化方法，在任务序列中保留稀疏特征学习偏置；对于足够稀疏的教师模型，该方法在中等任务跨度内比常规二范数或一范数正则化学习更快、表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有持续学习理论主要聚焦线性回归及其二范数正则化，持续学习分类的理论分析相对不足。此外，深层线性模型中的权重二范数正则化在单任务学习时可诱导稀疏预测器，但持续学习通常以先前任务的权重为正则化中心，这会破坏这种有益的稀疏偏置。论文旨在分析分类情形，并解决持续学习中稀疏特征学习偏置难以保留的问题。

Method: 首先，在高维线性预测器的持续逻辑回归设定下，推导最优二范数正则化强度并刻画泛化表现。随后，利用最小化的双层对角线性网络研究深层模型中的正则化效应，并提出加权二范数正则化，使其在持续学习中延续稀疏偏置。论文通过理论分析与实验评估该方法，并与常规持续二范数及一范数正则化进行比较。

Result: 理论分析表明，持续线性分类中最优二范数正则化及其泛化表现与既有持续线性回归结果呈现类似规律。对于足够稀疏的教师模型，所提方法相较于直接学习线性预测器并采用常规持续二范数或一范数正则化，收敛显著更快，在中等任务跨度内性能明显更优；代价是任务跨度较长时会产生一定偏差。

Conclusion: 持续学习中的常规二范数正则化可能无法保留深层模型有利的稀疏特征学习能力。通过加权设计恢复这一偏置，可在适用的稀疏任务情境下显著改善中期学习效率与效果，但其优势伴随长期偏差，需要权衡任务跨度。

Abstract: We study logistic regression for continual learning with linear predictors in a high-dimensional statistical setting. Existing theoretical work has largely focused on continual linear regression with variants of L2 regularization, motivated by their analytical simplicity and connection to the kernel regime of neural networks. We first derive the optimal L2 regularization strength for continual linear classification and characterize its generalization performance, recovering behavior analogous to prior results for continual linear regression. Next, we examine the effect of L2 regularization in deeper models using a minimal two-layer diagonal linear network. In the single-task setting, L2 regularization on the weights of such models is known to promote sparsity in the resulting predictor, yielding a rich, sparse feature-learning regime beyond the kernel regime (a similar effect arises in two-layer ReLU networks). However, in continual learning, where L2 regularization is typically centered on previous-task weights, this sparsity bias is no longer preserved. We therefore propose a weighted variant of L2 regularization that extends this beneficial sparsity bias to continual learning. We show theoretically and empirically that, for a sufficiently sparse teacher, our variant converges substantially faster than directly learning a linear predictor with conventional continual L2 or L1 regularization, trading some long-horizon bias for markedly improved performance over moderate task horizons.

</details>


### [169] [Systolic Linear Attention: Co-Designing Tile-Grouped Gated Delta Attention for TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06828)
*Gagik Amirkhanyan,Nuojin Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向谷歌张量处理器优化的脉动线性注意力，通过调整注意力层结构并融合计算内核，提升门控差分注意力的运行速度；大规模预训练中，训练速度较基线提高百分之十一至百分之二十四，困惑度相近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门控差分线性注意力原本针对图形处理器设计，依赖较小的矩阵计算单元和快速向量运算。在谷歌张量处理器上直接移植时，宽大的脉动矩阵单元利用不足，且逐通道衰减重缩放等操作受较慢的向量单元限制，因此需要针对该硬件重新设计计算布局。

Method: 提出脉动线性注意力：将键维度扩展至二百五十六，并通过分组价值头共享计算；将衰减门分成四个、每个六十四通道的区块；再把区块内求解改写为块矩阵乘法，并在单个融合内核中完成。另测试了采用精确区块求逆的变体。

Result: 在第七代谷歌张量处理器上，完整注意力层相比开源基线内核快一点六至二点五倍；采用精确区块求逆时快一点四至二点四倍，主要收益来自头部共享和内核融合。在二千零四十八颗芯片参与的稀疏混合专家模型预训练中，模型规模为六十五亿至六百三十一亿参数，训练速度提升百分之十一至百分之二十四，困惑度总体相近；较小规模、随机种子匹配的实验中，困惑度略低于基线。

Conclusion: 针对硬件特性重构注意力计算，而非直接移植为其他加速器设计的实现，可以更充分利用谷歌张量处理器的矩阵单元。该方法在保持相近模型效果的同时提升了注意力层性能和大规模预训练效率。

Abstract: Gated-delta linear attention layers such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN), Kimi Delta Attention (KDA), and GDN-2 are tuned for GPUs, where small tensor-core tiles and fast vector registers make per-channel decay rescaling and sequential in-block solves cheap. On Google TPUs, which concentrate compute in wide $256 \times 256$ systolic matrix units backed by a slower vector unit, porting these layers directly leaves half of each matrix tile empty and bottlenecks on vector operations. We present \textbf{Systolic Linear Attention (SLA)}, which reshapes gated delta attention around the matrix unit by widening keys to $256$ with grouped value heads, grouping decay gates into four $64$-channel tiles, and computing the chunk solve entirely with block matrix products in a single fused kernel. On TPU v7x, a full \texttt{SLA-R4} layer runs $1.6\times$--$2.5\times$ faster than the open-source Tokamax KDA kernel ($1.4\times$--$2.4\times$ with exact chunk inversion), driven primarily by head sharing and kernel fusion. Across a $2{,}048$-chip TPU v7x pre-training study of sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models from $6.5\text{B}$ to $63.1\text{B}$ parameters, SLA trains $11\%$--$24\%$ faster than KDA at similar perplexity, and reaches slightly lower perplexity than KDA in smaller matched-seed runs.

</details>


### [170] [Navigating Disagreement Among Criteria for Evaluating Explanation Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06846)
*Grant Shanklin,Jiří Němeček,Asia Biega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一套分析框架，解释不同忠实性评估标准为何会对同一解释得出不同判断，并帮助审计者依据具体情境选择合适标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法解释应准确反映模型行为，但现有忠实性评估标准可能彼此冲突，使开发者和审计者难以判断应采用哪一种。论文旨在厘清分歧来源，并为评估标准的选择提供实践路径。

Method: 作者提出“忠实性函数”框架，从三个方面分析评估标准：解释对模型行为作出了什么主张、哪些比较能够检验该主张，以及如何汇总这些比较的结果。作者据此重构既有评估标准，并通过不利行动通知案例，分析一项行业建议程序隐含的评估方式及其选择依据。

Result: 分析表明，不同评估标准的分歧主要来自对上述三个问题的不同设定，而非忠实性要求本身互不相容。选择具体标准需要考虑解释目的、制度环境与规范要求。案例分析显示，具体应用场景能够为部分设定提供依据，但仍可能无法决定其他设定。

Conclusion: 忠实性是一项共同但尚未充分具体化的要求，因此评估标准出现多样性并不意外。审计者和实践者可以借助该框架识别标准之间的差异，并根据具体情境论证选择；不同指标虽有分歧，仍可支持情境化、合理的忠实性评估。

Abstract: Faithfulness requires that algorithmic explanations accurately reflect the model's behavior. Yet existing operationalizations of faithfulness can disagree about whether the same explanation is faithful, leaving developers and auditors to choose among conflicting evaluations. In this paper, we investigate disagreement among evaluation criteria and offer a practical path forward for evaluating explanation faithfulness. We first identify how operationalizations of faithfulness differ. We introduce a faithfulness functional that locates their disagreements in what an explanation claims about the model, which comparisons bear on that claim, and how agreement across those comparisons should be aggregated, and we reconstruct existing evaluation criteria within it. We argue these reconstructions support understanding faithfulness as a common though incompletely specified relation between the model and explanation, so the differences reflect choices left open by the general requirement. Justifying an operationalization then comes down to justifying these choices, which, on our view, draws on explanatory, institutional, and normative considerations. We support this argument with a case study of adverse action notices, reconstructing the evaluation implicit in an industry-proposed procedure and identifying which of its choices the setting justifies and which remain open. Our work gives auditors and practitioners a way to navigate disagreeing evaluations of faithfulness. It also explains why the literature should expect multiple operationalizations, while showing how concrete settings can narrow the choice among them. More broadly, our analysis shows how a shared requirement of faithfulness can support contextually justified evaluations despite disagreement among existing metrics.

</details>


### [171] [Adaptive Multi Mode: Budget-Aware Routing of Optimizers By Online Landscape Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06879)
*Benjamin Jacob Bodner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 自适应多模式优化器通过在线探测问题结构，为不同黑箱优化引擎分配预算；在多项留出测试中总体表现较强，但低预算优势有限，且对部分旋转后的可分问题优势减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续黑箱优化中，哪种经典优化器表现最好取决于预算和目标函数景观。现有方法常依据维度、预算、历史改进或特定基准上拟合的特征来选择优化器，较少在运行过程中检验各优化器所依赖的隐含假设。因此，研究旨在建立一种能够在线识别问题结构、并据此选择优化策略的路由方法。

Method: 自适应多模式方法将已知总预算分配给拟牛顿、信赖域、坐标搜索、多起点和进化策略等优化引擎。它通过在线探测曲率、平台区、可分性、折点、吸引盆地及分形结构来判断问题特征；每项探测都有默认设置、按维度与预算确定的适用范围，并将探测成本计入总预算。研究先在预注册的基准开发循环中检验预测，再于未参与开发的实例上进行一次留出评测，并与多种竞争方法及由自身优化引擎构造的控制方案比较。

Result: 在开发阶段，最近十六次确认中的三十二项预测有三十六项成立。留出测试覆盖维度二至四十、预算为每维十至一千次评估的情形；在六百个函数—维度—预算单元中，该方法在三百八十二个单元取得最高目标命中份额，其中二百三十六个为单独领先、其余为并列领先。按描述性后验规则判断，它在三十五个单元中可信地落后，且主要集中于每维十次和三十次评估的低预算场景。各预算下，它也略胜于自身引擎构成的多种控制方案以及一项调优进化策略基线。预注册测试显示，其相对优势在仿射变换、边界变换和箱约束基准上仍成立；但旋转可分函数后，优势大幅减弱。在另一多目标基准上，最高预算时其最终命中份额与进化策略基线持平，任意时刻综合表现更低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，依据在线结构探测来路由多种优化引擎，是提升连续黑箱优化整体表现的一种有前景的方案，且其优势并非仅来自对单一基准的拟合。不过，优势会随预算和问题变换而变化，尤其低预算下较不稳定，部分旋转后的可分问题及另一基准上的表现也限制了普遍性结论。作者已将该方法作为开源软件发布。

Abstract: The best classical optimizer for a continuous black-box problem depends on its budget and landscape. Existing routers decide from the dimension and budget, past improvement or features fitted to one benchmark, and few test an optimizer's implicit assumptions online. AMM (Adaptive Multi Mode) routes a known budget among quasi-Newton, trust-region, coordinate, multistart and evolution-strategy optimization engines with online probes of curvature, plateaus, separability, kinks, basins and fractal structure. Each probe has a default, a dimension-by-budget validity range and a cost charged to the budget. In its pre-registered development loop on bbob, 32 of 36 predictions held over the last 16 confirmations. AMM was then tested once on held-out bbob instances 101-110 (D=2-40, 10*D-1000*D) against up to seven competitors. It has the highest target share in 382 of 600 function-dimension-budget cells (236 outright, 146 tied) and, by a descriptive posterior rule, is credibly behind in 35, mostly at 10*D and 30*D. At every budget it also leads the controls built from its own engines (the best engine alone, the best engine per dimension and budget, and a greedy schedule), narrowly at 10*D and 30*D, and nevergrad's NgIohTuned. In pre-registered runs its lead over IPOP-CMA-ES holds under affine and box transformations, on box-constrained bbob and, pooled, on rotated f2-f4, but rotating the separable f3 and f4 removes most of the lead there. On held-out MA-BBOB its final share at 2000*D is level with IPOP-CMA-ES's and its anytime area lower. We published AMM as an open-source Python package (pip install amm-optimizer; https://github.com/BjBodner/amm-optimizer).

</details>


### [172] [Reinforcement Learning Enables Scaling Generalist Robot Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06905)
*Michal Korniak,Marco Bagatella,Ralf Römer,Michal Nauman,Yarden As,Pranav Vignesh,Manan Agarwal,Leander Diaz-Bone,Piotr Zalewski,Mikołaj Czarnecki,Lenart Treven,Shashwat Saxena,Sreyas Venkataraman,Sarvesh Patil,Zhiyuan Zhou,Daniel Marta,Oier Mees,Nur Muhammad Mahi Shafiullah,Carmelo Sferrazza,Bhavya Sukhija,Angela P. Schoellig,Andreas Krause,Max Simchowitz,Andrew Wagenmaker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，通用机器人策略能够通过在线强化学习，在多种任务上持续改进；合适的训练任务分布与基于价值的强化学习也能促使其泛化到未参与在线训练的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略虽能从大规模预训练中获得较强的零样本泛化能力，但部署时往往仍需适应。以往通过反复强化学习进行自我改进的研究主要聚焦单一任务，与基础策略的多任务能力不匹配。因此，论文探讨能否在部署中同时提升多种任务，以及实现这种通用改进所需的方法设计。

Method: 作者构建了一套结构化评测，用于检验通用策略的改进能力，并系统考察价值估计、策略提取和资源分配等算法与实践设计选择。研究在多种任务上开展在线训练，并分析训练任务分布及基于价值的强化学习对改进和泛化的影响。

Result: 实验表明，通用机器人策略可以在多种不同任务上通过在线学习提升表现。采用合适的方法后，策略还能在未纳入在线训练的任务上取得性能增益；这种泛化并非必然发生，而与训练任务分布以及是否采用基于价值的强化学习密切相关。

Conclusion: 论文提供了通用机器人基础模型能够在部署阶段进行多任务在线改进的初步证据，并指出任务分布和价值学习是实现有效改进与跨任务泛化的关键因素。

Abstract: The introduction of generalist robot policies has enabled a single, large model to learn to perform a variety of tasks from a vast pre-training corpus. While these models often exhibit powerful zero-shot generalization capabilities, they typically require an adaptation phase to achieve sufficient performance in deployment. Iterated self-improvement via reinforcement learning (RL) has demonstrated promise in enabling this adaptation, yet such approaches have been largely explored in single-task settings - where the goal is to simply improve on a narrow deployment task - which lies in stark contrast to the multi-task capabilities of the base policy. In this work we ask whether generalist robot policies are capable of multi-task, generalist improvement in deployment - targeting many diverse tasks simultaneously - and what algorithmic recipe enables this. To answer this question, we first develop a set of structured evaluations to test generalist improvement capabilities, and systematically evaluate algorithmic and practical design choices regarding value estimation, policy extraction and resource allocation. We show that not only can generalist policies be improved online across diverse tasks, but that the right recipe can enable generalization and increase performance on tasks not considered during online training. We characterize when this generalization occurs and show that both the training task distribution and the use of value-based RL are critical. To the best of our knowledge, this is among the first evidence for online generalist improvement for robot foundation models.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Item Representations Matter: Bridging Hard Semantic IDs and Soft Prototype Mixtures for Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05799)
*Vicente Balmaseda,Dongqi Fu,Jiarui Feng,Ren chen,Hanqing Zeng,Tianxin Wei,Yinglong Xia,Tianbao Yang,Hong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于共享原型软混合的连续生成式推荐方法，在保留连续表示的同时引入跨物品共享结构；实验显示其整体效果和推理吞吐量均优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐各有局限：语义标识符通过离散编码提供共享参数和语义结构，却会因硬量化而丢失原始内容嵌入的信息；连续方法避免量化，但通常采用彼此独立的物品查找表，缺少跨物品的显式参数共享。论文希望在不形成离散信息瓶颈的前提下，为连续推荐引入共享结构。

Method: 提出连续流式推荐方法，将每个物品表示为多个共享原型的软混合，再加上物品专属向量。模型联合训练原型、软分配和物品向量，并在推理时保留软表示。预训练信息是可选的：可用语义标识符提供的责任度作为分配先验，也可用完整内容嵌入初始化物品专属向量，且不改变初始平坦查找表。

Result: 在三个数据集的匹配比较中，该方法在两个稀疏亚马逊数据集上优于平坦查找表；不使用预训练信息时，在蒸汽平台数据集上也取得小幅提升。仅有离线语义标识符时，相比使用同一分词器构建的硬编码查找表，前五命中率提升百分之四点四五至百分之十四点九。在拥有完整内容嵌入时，该方法达到或超过强平坦查找表的表现，而硬分配在稀疏亚马逊数据集上效果退化。综合六项基准，相对于各数据集、各指标上表现最佳的已评估基线，前五命中率和前五归一化折损累计增益分别相对提升百分之六点九和百分之十三点六；实验中的推理吞吐量为离散基线的一点四至一百六十一倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，软原型混合能够在连续推荐中兼顾跨物品共享与细粒度信息保留，并可灵活利用或不依赖预训练语义信息。该方法在多项基准上提升推荐质量，同时保持较高推理效率；其优势在稀疏场景及避免硬量化信息损失方面尤为明显。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems rely on generative retrieval techniques to predict the next item either as discrete codes or as a continuous vector. On the one hand, Semantic IDs (SIDs) give discrete models shared parameters and pretrained semantic structure, but discretizing items into hard codes discards some information from the original content embeddings. On the other hand, continuous models avoid quantization, yet typically use flat item lookup tables with no explicit parameter tying across items. We ask whether a continuous recommender can introduce shared structure without making it a discrete bottleneck. We propose CAP-Mix, a flow recommender that represents each item as a soft mixture of shared prototypes plus an item-specific vector. The assignments, prototypes, and item vectors are trained jointly, and the soft representation is retained at inference. Pretrained information is optional: SID responsibilities can serve as a prior over assignments, while a full content embedding can initialize the item-specific vector without changing the initial flat table. In matched comparisons on three datasets, CAP-Mix improves over a flat table on the two sparse Amazon datasets and yields a small gain on Steam when trained without pretrained information. Given only an offline SID, it outperforms a hard table built from the same tokenizer by 4.45-14.90% in HR@5. Given the full content embedding, it matches or improves a strong flat table while hard assignments regress on the sparse Amazon datasets. Overall, averaged across six benchmarks, CAP-Mix improves HR@5 and NDCG@5 by 6.9% and 13.6% relative to the best evaluated baseline for each dataset and metric, while delivering 1.4-161x the inference throughput of the discrete baselines in our experiments.

</details>


### [174] [SciToT: Multimodal Tip-of-the-Tongue Retrieval for Scientific Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05829)
*Stan Fris,Sijie Tao,Makoto P. Kato,Maarten de Rijke*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向科学文献“舌尖现象”检索的多模态基准，支持用户凭内容或图表等模糊记忆重新寻找论文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者常记得论文的部分内容或视觉信息，却忘记标题、作者等可靠标识，因此难以重新找到目标论文。现有相关基准未覆盖科学文献，也未充分处理文本与图表并存的检索需求。

Method: 构建可扩展的查询生成流程，分别从科学文献中提取文本和视觉记忆线索，再借助大型语言模型生成模拟不完整人类回忆的查询。研究还分析公开的论文重寻请求，以刻画此类检索需求，并通过人工标注和自动质量控制评估生成查询。基准提供词汇匹配、稠密检索、后期交互和视觉关联检索等基线。

Result: 基准包含五个领域的108,512篇开放获取科学文献和11,499条合成查询。论文同时发布数据集、文档表示、记忆线索、评测划分，以及查询生成与检索流程；摘要未报告具体检索性能数值。

Conclusion: 该工作为科学论文的多模态重寻任务建立了专门的评测资源和基线，有助于推动利用文本与视觉记忆线索的检索研究。相关资源以宽松的开放许可证发布，便于后续研究复用。

Abstract: Researchers frequently encounter difficulties re-finding scientific papers when they remember aspects of the content but cannot recall reliable identifiers, such as titles or author names. This type of re-finding task, known as tip-of-the-tongue (ToT) retrieval, is particularly challenging for scientific documents, where recollections may involve textual information and visual elements, including figures, diagrams, and plots. Existing ToT retrieval benchmarks do not address scientific search.
  We introduce a multimodal benchmark for scientific ToT retrieval, comprising 108,512 open-access scientific documents across five domains and 11,499 synthetically generated queries. To construct the benchmark, we propose a scalable query elicitation pipeline that independently extracts textual and visual memory cues from source documents and uses large language models to generate queries resembling incomplete human recollections. We characterize scientific ToT information needs through an empirical analysis of publicly available re-finding requests and use the outcomes to assess the generated queries using human annotations and automated quality control. The benchmark includes lexical, dense, late-interaction, and visually grounded retrieval baselines.
