We release the dataset, extracted document representations, generated memory cues, evaluation splits, and accompanying generation and retrieval pipelines under an MIT license to facilitate research on multimodal scientific paper re-finding.

</details>


### [175] [Weaver: Weaving Preference Evidence through Incremental Reasoning for Personalized Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05900)
*Linyao Zheng,Zhifang Mao,Xuhang Shi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出织忆框架，通过依赖关系建模、逐步检索和结合情境的偏好推理，从长期对话中更完整、准确地提取用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化检索增强生成需要从长期交互历史中提取并应用用户偏好，但相关证据往往分散在多轮对话中，且受时间与情境限制。单独的片段通常不足以支撑回答，而不同条件下表达的偏好也容易被混为一谈。

Method: 织忆框架采用双层检索与推理图：上层意图依赖图追踪回答当前请求所需的信息及其前置依赖；下层记忆条件检索轨迹图记录基于既有前提和线索寻找缺失证据的过程。框架进一步结合情境范围感知的组合推理，识别互补或替代证据，并区分同一时间与情境下的冲突主张。推理结论会迭代更新任务前提和剩余信息缺口，以指导后续检索。

Result: 在个性化检索增强生成基准测试中，尤其是在人物记忆基准上，织忆优于领先基线，在证据完整性和回答质量方面均取得更好表现。摘要未提供具体数值或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将检索过程显式建模为依赖关系与记忆检索轨迹，并以时间和情境为范围进行组合推理，有助于缓解长期对话中偏好证据分散及情境混淆的问题，从而提升个性化回答的依据完整性与质量。

Abstract: Personalized retrieval-augmented generation requires extracting and applying user preferences from extended interaction histories. This task is challenging because preference evidence is scattered across past conversations and remains bound to specific temporal and situational contexts. Consequently, isolated fragments rarely provide complete support for a response, while preferences expressed under different conditions are easily conflated. To address these limitations, we propose Weaver, a framework that incrementally weaves memory-conditioned retrieval trajectories with compositional preference reasoning for personalized responses. Weaver operates on a Dual-Layer Retrieval and Reasoning Graph that couples an upper Intent Dependency Graph with a lower Memory-Conditioned Retrieval Trajectory Graph. Dependency Information Gap Tracking represents the information needed to answer the current request and its prerequisite dependencies in the upper graph. Memory-Conditioned Incremental Retrieval uses established premises and clues from retrieved conversations to locate missing evidence, recording the resulting retrieval trajectories in the lower graph. Scope-Aware Compositional Reasoning jointly interprets the accumulated evidence to determine which preferences apply to the request. It identifies complementary and alternative support and distinguishes conflicting claims within the same temporal and situational context. These conclusions iteratively update task premises and remaining information gaps, guiding subsequent retrieval to establish a coherent contextual basis for the final response. Experiments on personalized RAG benchmarks, particularly PersonaMem, demonstrate that Weaver outperforms leading baselines in both evidence completeness and answer quality.

</details>


### [176] [Optimal Re-Ranking Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05908)
*Siqing Huo,Andrew Parry,Debasis Ganguly,Charles L. A. Clarke*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究如何按查询动态确定二阶段神经重排深度。实验发现，许多查询存在明确的最优深度；利用大语言模型评估首阶段排序质量，可在基本保持效果的同时，将平均重排深度最多降低至原来的三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二阶段神经排序通常采用固定重排深度，但不同查询的最优深度可能差异显著，继续扩大深度也未必带来收益，甚至可能损害效果。过去，逐查询研究这一问题需要大量人工相关性标注，成本过高；大语言模型生成的相关性判断使大规模分析成为可能。

Method: 研究者在典型的检索与重排流程中，使用大语言模型生成密集相关性判断，逐查询确定最优重排深度，并考察能否依据首阶段检索特征提前预测该深度。他们比较了多种标准查询效果预测方法，并提出一种基于大语言模型评估首阶段前十位排序质量的指标，用于预测重排深度。

Result: 许多查询在超过某一深度后，检索效果趋于停滞或下降。多数标准查询效果预测方法无法有效预测最优深度；新提出的首阶段质量指标则可依所用排序器配置，在基本保持整体效果的同时，将平均重排深度最多降低约三倍。若采用已知最优深度的理想选择，在微软问答检索开发集上，效果可提升超过百分之七，平均重排深度可降低约五倍。

Conclusion: 重排深度应被视为查询相关属性，而非固定的系统参数。大语言模型生成的相关性判断有助于开展过去难以进行的大规模研究，也能支持更高效的自适应重排。作者同时公开相关性判断、代码和实验材料，以促进结果复现与后续研究。

Abstract: Second-stage neural rankers are commonly applied with a fixed re-ranking depth, assuming that retrieval effectiveness saturates as depth increases. Prior research has questioned the assumption that increasing the re-ranking depth yields linear performance gains, further suggesting that optimal re-ranking depth varies considerably from query to query. In the past, studying such phenomena was methodologically difficult given the scale of manual annotation required. With the advent of LLM-based relevance judgments we can now more easily undertake such studies, in this case to pinpoint the optimal re-ranking depth on a per-query basis. Using dense LLM-based relevance judgments over a typical re-ranking pipeline, we show that many queries exhibit a well-defined optimal re-ranking depth, beyond which effectiveness stagnates or degrades. We formulate re-ranking depth as a query-specific property and study whether it can be predicted a priori from first-stage retrieval characteristics. Through a large-scale analysis, we find that most standard query performance prediction (QPP) methods are ineffective for this task. In contrast, a predictor derived from LLM-assessed first-stage ranking quality, which we term IR-DCG@10, can reduce the average re-ranking depth by up to a factor of 3 while preserving overall effectiveness, depending on the specific first- and second-stage rankers used. Under oracle selection of optimal depths, we further show that retrieval effectiveness can improve by more than 7% while reducing the average re-ranking depth by a factor of 5 on the MSMARCO DEV collection. Given our promising preliminary findings, we would encourage the use of automatic judgments to facilitate research otherwise infeasible under manual annotation. Towards this point, we release relevance judgments, our codebase, and experimental artefacts to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [177] [One Basket, Many Experts: Decorrelated Re-ranking for Financial Asset Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05952)
*Ryuki Unno,Keigo Sakurai,Koshi Watanabe,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该方法保留最相关的部分推荐，并将其余名额替换为收益相关性较低的资产，以可调参数明确控制相关性与组合表现之间的取舍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售金融资产推荐通常优先匹配用户购买偏好，但最受欢迎的资产往往集中且收益高度相关，限制了推荐组合的风险调整后收益。论文旨在兼顾用户相关性与服务提供方关注的组合表现，并使两者的权衡透明、可控。

Method: 提出去相关卫星专家重排序方法：从专家推荐列表中保留最相关的资产，将其余推荐名额替换为收益相关性较低的备选资产。替换名额数由单一整数参数控制，服务提供方可据此选择不同的相关性—表现权衡点。

Result: 在公开的零售券商数据集上，主要实验方案显示，替换少量推荐名额即可提升风险调整后收益，同时只造成较小的相关性损失；在典型零售投资组合规模下，收益提升在计入实际交易成本后仍然存在。收益改善并非来自波动率下降：数据中相关性最低的资产波动率更高，但其较高收益推动了组合收益表现。相关性成本在不同市场阶段较为一致，而收益提升则随市场阶段变化。

Conclusion: 该方法将推荐相关性与组合表现之间的权衡转化为服务提供方可审计、可调节的显式选择。实践中，提供方可通过设定替换名额数，直接了解不同运营方案需要付出的相关性成本；不过，最终收益提升具有市场阶段依赖性。

Abstract: Financial asset recommendation for retail investors is typically optimized for relevance, that is, how well a recommendation matches what each user will buy. From the service provider's point of view, the value of a recommendation also depends on portfolio performance: the risk-adjusted return the recommended assets (the basket) deliver, which drives user engagement and fee revenue. The two goals are rarely achieved jointly, because the recommendations that best match user behavior concentrate on a few popular assets whose returns are highly correlated, which caps the risk-adjusted return a provider can offer. We propose Decorrelated-Satellite Expert Re-ranking (\textbf{DSER}), which retains the most relevant recommendations from an expert-following list and replaces the rest with return-decorrelated alternatives. The number of replaced slots is a single integer~$m$ (a knob) that the provider sets, so the relevance--performance trade-off becomes an explicit, auditable choice rather than a property hidden inside a learned model. In experiments on the public FAR-Trans retail-brokerage dataset, under the primary protocol, replacing a few slots improves risk-adjusted return at a small relevance cost, and the gain survives realistic trading costs at typical retail portfolio sizes. Minimizing correlation would lower basket volatility, but in our data the least-correlated assets are more volatile and higher-return, so their volatility offsets that reduction while their return drives the gain, not lower volatility. The resulting trade-off is asymmetric, with the relevance cost consistent across market phases while the gain is phase-dependent. The provider sets $m$ to read off exactly how much relevance each operating point costs.

</details>


### [178] [Do Search-Augmented Recommenders Use the Latest Search Episode?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06044)
*Jiandong Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种反事实审计方法，比较中性掩码与匹配的真实搜索替换，检验搜索信息对推荐的贡献。结果显示，估计值会随对照方式变化；不能仅凭掩码实验断定搜索状态或最近一次搜索带来收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索增强推荐系统可利用最近一次搜索以及更早的搜索，但这些信息究竟如何影响下一条推荐，尤其是掩码后排名变化是否足以证明搜索信息有益，仍需更可靠的评估。论文旨在区分真实搜索信息的贡献与中性掩码造成的分布偏移效应。

Method: 提出“焦点交换”审计：针对每条推荐，在固定目标、候选集、搜索前历史和评分器的条件下，替换最近一次搜索，或替换全部搜索输入。方法将中性掩码与按类别、用户画像匹配的真实搜索序列替换进行对照，记录目标排名变化、相对各自对照的倒数排名增益，以及模型对真实焦点搜索的偏好。研究先通过植入信号和现有模型的当前查询任务验证审计能力，再在两个数据集及多个已发布实现训练出的模型检查点上评估行为和词汇证据，并测试不同捐赠样本分配的稳健性。

Result: 在三个独立训练的模型检查点、同一组六万六千余条样本上，将所有搜索输入替换为中性标识符时，估计得到正向贡献；改用类别及画像匹配的真实搜索序列后，估计值更小，其中一个检查点的区间低于零。两种对照间的配对倒数排名差约为千分之二点七七至千分之六点六零四，调整后的区间均高于零。中性掩码改变约百分之五十九点七至六十一点七的目标排名，而匹配替换改变约百分之四十四点一至四十四点八；换用五种捐赠样本分配后，对照差异仍存在。在主要的中性掩码和类别匹配对照下，无论是另一种推荐模型还是上述模型，均未能确立最近一次搜索具有正向贡献。

Conclusion: 搜索输入贡献的估计强烈依赖所选对照。中性掩码可能引入较大的分布变化，因此不能单独据此将排名变化归因于搜索信息的实际价值。评估整体搜索状态或最近一次搜索的作用时，应将掩码结果与匹配的真实搜索替换结果对照，并谨慎解释正向收益。

Abstract: Search-augmented recommenders can use the latest search episode and earlier searches. We introduce FocalSwap to test how these inputs contribute to the next recommendation. For each recommendation, it changes the latest search slot or all search inputs while holding the target, candidates, pre-search history, and scorer fixed. It compares neutral masks with matched observed-search replacements, recording rank changes, RR gains relative to each control, and preference for the true focal search. A planted signal and GenSAR's current-query task confirm that the audit detects known input dependence. We evaluate behavioral and lexical evidence on KuaiSAR and Qilin, and recommendation checkpoints trained using the released SESRec and UniSAR implementations.
  Across three independently trained UniSAR checkpoints, the estimated all-source benefit depends on the control. On the same 66,139 rows, replacing all search inputs with a neutral ID yields a positive signed contribution, whereas replacing them with category/profile-matched observed search sequences gives smaller estimates, with an interval below zero for one checkpoint. The paired RR gaps range from $+2.770 \times 10^{-3}$ to $+6.604 \times 10^{-3}$, and every adjusted interval is above zero. Neutral masks change 59.7-61.7% of target ranks, while matched replacements change 44.1-44.8%. The control contrast persists across five alternative donor assignments per checkpoint. Under the primary neutral-mask and category-matched controls, positive latest-slot contribution is not established in SESRec or UniSAR. These results show why masking evidence should be compared with observed-search replacements when attributing a benefit to broader search state or the latest episode.

</details>


### [179] [Generative Retrieval under Real-Time Supply Constraints in Local Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06323)
*Shaopeng Liang,Biao Zhang,Hongzhen Ye,Zeyang Hou,Ma Jiang,Jintao Tu,yunxi. hz,Zhijia Fang,Jianqiang Cao,Junxue Rao,Jia Jia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向请求时供给快照的生成式检索实现：预先为当前可供服务的候选项建立压缩索引，并在生成时约束标识符扩展。初步结果显示过滤效果与较低的服务端延迟增量，但尚未证明在检索质量相同的条件下能节省计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索可能生成目录中有效、但因地点、天气、交通或时段而无法实际服务的商家与商品。若等生成完成后再过滤，不仅会浪费推理计算，也会占用有限的候选束容量。针对请求时供给变化，如何在短输出序列下高效准备并实施约束，是本文关注的问题。

Method: 系统将当前可用候选项的残差量化标识符编译成压缩稀疏行前缀索引；在模型预填充阶段并行进行索引编译，并让多个候选束共享已上传的索引。解码时只允许合法扩展，同时保留原模型的对数概率，并依据候选束父节点更新约束状态。论文还形式化定义了相对于供给快照的标识符有效性，并将其与实体层面的可用资格及新鲜度区分。实现生成由三个标记组成的商家标识符。

Result: 在一个束宽匹配的每日约束案例中，候选商家中被过滤的数量从二百九十九个中的一百七十个降至二十九个。残差距离过滤结果促使作者进一步采用请求特定约束。另一项使用三万个输入条目的异主机对比显示，服务端延迟增加零点六毫秒；客户端测量则揭示了额外的请求开销。来自三个地理环境的请求记录还展示了不同供给规模和输出宽度下的系统表现。由于测量跨越不同开发阶段和边界，这些结果并非统一条件下的完整对比。

Conclusion: 本文展示了在生成过程中直接施加请求时供给约束的一种可行工程方案，并指出索引准备、请求开销及测量口径仍是关键问题。现有证据能够说明实现可运行并具有一定过滤效果，但不足以证明在检索质量相同的前提下实现了计算节省。

Abstract: Generative retrieval predicts semantic identifiers for candidate entities. In local retail and food delivery, candidates must also be serviceable for a particular user at a particular time. Location, weather, and traffic reshape merchant and product supply, so a catalog-valid identifier may be infeasible for the current request. Post-generation filtering removes such candidates only after beam capacity and inference computation have been spent. We study retrieval over an explicitly materialized, request-time supply snapshot, where short output sequences leave little opportunity to amortize online constraint preparation. Our RTP-LLM pipeline compiles eligible residual-quantization identifiers into a compressed sparse row prefix index, overlaps CPU compilation with model prefill, and shares the uploaded index across beams. Legal expansions retain original-model log probabilities, and constraint states follow selected beam parents. We formalize snapshot-relative identifier validity and distinguish it from entity-level eligibility and freshness. The implementation generates three-token merchant identifiers. Historical evidence covers daily catalog constraints and subsequent request-scoped serving evaluations. In one matched-beam daily-constraint case, filtered merchants decrease from 170 of 299 candidates to 29 of 299; residual distance filtering motivates request-specific constraints. A different-host comparison with 30,000 supplied entries reports a 0.6 ms service-side latency increment, while client measurements expose additional request costs. Captured requests from three geographic settings characterize behavior across supply sizes and output widths. These observations span development stages and measurement boundaries. They establish a practical implementation and identify bottlenecks, but do not establish compute savings at matched retrieval quality.

</details>


### [180] [PMRecBench: A Benchmark for Personalization in Recommendation Agents under Multimodal Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06349)
*Xiaoyu Zhang,Yougang Lyu,Bin Wu,Sahan Bulathwela,Emine Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出个性化多模态推荐基准，用真实用户历史和图文查询评估推荐系统。实验发现，多模态信息虽能提升整体排序表现，却可能削弱系统对用户长期偏好的遵循。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化推荐基准主要采用纯文本查询，无法评估系统面对图像与文本组合查询时是否仍能结合真实用户历史进行个性化推荐；已有多模态基准则侧重匹配查询要求，未检验个性化能力。因此，需要能够同时考察需求理解与偏好遵循的评测基准。

Method: 构建个性化多模态推荐基准，收录服装和电影领域的五百一十组经质量控制的图文查询，并关联对应用户的真实历史记录。查询依据用户记录中稳定的行为模式构造，参考图片和目标商品均来自真实交互。基准还设计诊断候选集：一类商品符合用户偏好但违反查询要求，另一类符合查询要求但未获用户高评价，以分别检验需求理解和偏好遵循。

Result: 诊断实验显示，加入多模态查询后，系统的整体排序表现有所提升，但相较于仅依据个性化信号的情形，系统遵循用户偏好的能力会减弱。进一步分析表明，图像解读可能使系统过度重视候选商品与参考图片的相似性，而忽视用户历史；单纯采用更强的商业模型也无法消除这一失衡。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了多模态推荐中的个性化失衡：系统在更好理解当前查询要求的同时，可能偏离用户长期偏好。未来推荐系统需要兼顾排序效果、需求匹配与偏好遵循，避免图像线索压过用户历史证据。

Abstract: On Web platforms for shopping and entertainment, recommendation agents under multimodal queries aim to deliver personalized recommendations that satisfy users' visually specified requirements and reflect preferences inferred from user histories. However, existing personalized recommendation benchmarks use text-only queries, while multimodal ones assess query matching without evaluating personalization based on real user histories. We introduce the Personalized Multimodal Recommendation Benchmark (PMRecBench), the first benchmark to evaluate the personalization of recommendation agents under multimodal queries. It pairs 510 quality-controlled image-text queries across clothing and movies with the corresponding users' real histories. PMRecBench features two key designs: (1) queries derived from consistent behavioral patterns in real user records, with reference images and targets linked to actual interactions; and (2) challenging diagnostic pools with candidate items rated highly by the user but violating requirements, and items satisfying requirements but never rated highly, testing requirement understanding and preference following, respectively. Extensive experiments on diagnostic pools reveal a personalization imbalance in recommendation agents: relative to personalization signals alone, adding multimodal queries weakens preference following, even though the additional information about query requirements improves overall ranking performance. Further analysis links this decline in preference following to image interpretation, where agents can prioritize resemblance to reference images over evidence from user histories, and stronger commercial backbones alone fail to resolve this imbalance. This personalization imbalance highlights a central challenge for recommendation agents: improving ranking performance while preserving preference following when responding to multimodal user queries

</details>


### [181] [Deliberate Latent Reasoning for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06521)
*Haobo Zhang,Qiannan Zhu,Yutao Zhu,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向序列推荐的个性化潜在推理框架，可根据用户历史动态决定推理深度，并提取随时间演变的偏好，以提升用户表示和物品排序效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于变换器的序列推荐方法通常为所有用户设置相同的推理步数。然而，用户历史的复杂程度各不相同：推理过多可能造成冗余，推理不足则难以捕捉复杂偏好。因此，固定推理深度容易导致用户表示学习不充分。

Method: 该框架包含两个关键机制：一是推理步数选择模块，在训练阶段为每条用户序列识别信息量最丰富的推理状态，再通过自蒸馏将该状态压缩到最终推理步骤，使其在推理阶段作为用户表示用于物品排序；二是渐进式偏好提取机制，根据用户历史动态划分数量可变的时间阶段，并将各阶段概括为表示阶段性偏好的潜在里程碑，作为后续推理的输入。

Result: 在六个公开数据集和四种基础推荐模型上的大量实验表明，该方法整体优于八种基线模型。摘要未提供具体指标或各数据集上的提升幅度。

Conclusion: 研究表明，为不同用户适配推理深度，并显式刻画用户偏好的阶段性变化，有助于改善序列推荐中的用户建模。该框架兼顾了训练阶段的信息筛选与推理阶段的表示效率。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders are widely applied in real-world scenarios to model user preferences and behavior patterns based on user interactions. Recent advances in sequential recommendation enhance transformer-based models with latent reasoning to refine user representations by autoregressively generating intermediate reasoning vectors. However, exploring the optimal reasoning depth for diverse user histories remains challenging: existing methods typically employ a fixed number of reasoning steps for all users. This ``one-size-fits-all'' strategy often leads to suboptimal representation learning due to over-thinking in simple histories or under-thinking in complex ones. To address this issue, we propose DESRec, a deliberate latent reasoning framework that performs personalized latent reasoning to learn more informative reasoning states and enhance user modeling for sequential recommendation. Specifically, it designs a reasoning step selection module that identifies the optimal reasoning step for each user sequence during the training phase. Through a self-distillation mechanism, it compresses this most informative state into the final reasoning step, which serves as the final user representation for item ranking during inference. Moreover, it introduces a progressive preference extraction mechanism for personalized reasoning, which dynamically segments a user's history into a variable number of periods and summarizes each into a latent milestone representing the user's preference for that period. It will be used as the input of the subsequent reasoning. The number of milestones varies with the users' history, enabling a personalized reasoning over users' evolving interests. Extensive experimental results on six public datasets and four backbone recommenders demonstrate the superiority of DESRec over eight baseline models.

</details>


### [182] [WISE: An Agentic Framework for Whole-page Multimodal Search Experience Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06777)
*Wenyi Xu,Renzhe Zhou,Songyang Li,Feiran Zhu,Ruoqi Zhao,Chenglei Dai,Xuanping Li,Kaiqiao Zhan,Jingwei Zhuo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向算法驱动服务的系统质量诊断任务，并以工业搜索为例构建智能体框架、训练方法与真实场景基准，实现问题发现及其系统组件归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索、推荐和广告等服务由多阶段模型流水线构成。现有研究主要关注模型优化，部署后监控则偏重运行稳定性；因此，即使系统运行正常，也可能持续产出低质量结果。针对这一评估与监控缺口，论文提出系统化诊断问题，并指出既有工作缺少明确任务定义和基准。

Method: 论文将系统质量诊断定义为不仅识别用户可感知的质量问题，还要追溯其在系统组件中的成因。针对工业搜索，提出端到端智能体框架 WISE，用于发现问题并定位底层算法组件。为提高诊断可靠性，提出 ReTrace，构造证据一致的轨迹监督；并提出自适应框架—策略优化方法，通过失败案例指导智能体框架和模型策略的优化。此外，构建真实场景多模态基准 WISE-BENCH，整合用户侧证据、内部流水线轨迹和专家诊断标注。

Result: 实验显示，WISE 在不同规模模型上均取得稳定提升；在 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 上，端到端准确率达到 75.20%。生产应用表明，WISE 可支持质量审计、在线质量保障和模型优化，降低诊断成本，并改善下游相关性模型。

Conclusion: 论文为算法驱动服务提出了从质量问题识别到系统级原因归因的诊断范式，并通过框架、训练方法和基准推动其系统化评估。结果表明，智能体诊断不仅可用于发现搜索质量问题，也具有生产应用价值；不过摘要未提供基准规模、对比方法及各模块的独立消融结果，相关结论仍需结合正文进一步评估。

Abstract: Algorithm-driven services, including search, recommendation, and advertising, rely on multi-stage model pipelines. While research prioritizes model optimization, post-deployment monitoring focuses on operational reliability. Yet operationally healthy systems may still produce poor-quality results, exposing a gap in existing evaluation and monitoring practices. However, a systematic problem formulation and benchmark for this task remain lacking. To bridge this gap, we formulate System Quality Diagnosis (SQD) for algorithm-driven services, a new task that extends quality evaluation beyond identifying poor outcomes to attributing their causes across system components. Focusing on industrial search, we propose WISE, an agentic framework for end-to-end quality diagnosis that discovers user-facing issues and traces their origins to underlying algorithmic components. To improve diagnostic reliability, we introduce ReTrace for constructing evidence-consistent trajectory supervision and Adaptive Harness-Policy Optimization (AHPO) for failure-guided optimization of the agent harness and model policy. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct WISE-BENCH, a real-world multimodal search benchmark that combines user-facing evidence, internal pipeline traces, and expert diagnosis annotations. Extensive experiments show consistent gains across model scales, with WISE achieving 75.20% end-to-end accuracy on Qwen3.5-35B-A3B. In production, WISE supports quality auditing, online quality assurance, and model optimization, reducing diagnosis costs and improving downstream relevance models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Who May Overrule the Agent? Evidence-Gated Authority in LLM Review Institutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05390)
*Fuad Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，审查者是否能纠正错误、又不破坏正确决策，取决于其能力、操作界面与权限规则的共同作用；证据门槛可减少误改，但不是普遍有效的保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体语言模型系统常加入审查者来改进上游决策，但审查者既可能修正错误，也可能推翻原本正确的方案。研究关注的核心问题是：在提案、任务信息、模型和审查意见相同的情况下，何时应赋予审查者让建议生效的权限？

Method: 研究比较两种审查权限：一种允许审查者广泛推翻上游提案；另一种设置证据门槛，只有审查者提交机械可验证的记录，证明提案违反了具有约束力的规则，并给出符合规则的修正方案，才能替换提案。实验使用两种本地运行的开放权重模型，在九十六项空间政策任务及一项包含九十六项任务的组合迁移基准上进行测试。后续验证还让审查者明确选择保留、替换或升级处理。

Result: 证据门槛显著减少了对正确提案的破坏，同时保留了对违规提案的大部分修正能力。不过，权衡幅度取决于审查者的决策转变及上游提案状态。明确提供保留、替换或升级选项后，千问模型不再破坏正确提案，但也拒绝了所有仅以福利改善为依据的修正；米斯特拉尔模型则仍替换了全部四十八项正确提案。由此可见，界面设计会改变审查行为，却不能构成适用于所有模型的保障。

Conclusion: 限制审查权限并非在所有情形下都更优。纠错范围与误改风险由审查者能力、行动界面和具有约束力的权限规则共同决定；因此，设计可靠的多智能体审查机制时，必须同时评估这三者，而不能仅依赖权限限制或界面提示。

Abstract: Multi-agent language-model systems often add a reviewer to improve an upstream agent's decision. Yet a reviewer can both repair errors and replace decisions that were already correct. We study this as a problem of institutional jurisdiction: conditional on the same proposal, task information, model, and review output, when is the reviewer authorized to make its recommendation binding? We compare broad override authority with an evidence gate that permits replacement only after the reviewer produces a mechanically valid record of a binding-constraint violation and a compliant repair. Across two locally run open-weight models, 96 spatial-policy tasks, and a 96-task portfolio-transfer benchmark, the gate sharply reduces corruption of correct proposals while retaining most repairs of violations. The directions of this tradeoff are partly implied by the rule; its magnitude depends on reviewer transitions and upstream proposal states. A prospective validation then gives the reviewer an explicit choice to retain, replace, or escalate. This eliminates correct-proposal corruption for Qwen, but Mistral still replaces all 48 correct proposals; Qwen also declines every welfare-only repair that Mistral frequently attempts. Thus interface design changes reviewer behavior but is not a model-independent safeguard. The result is not that restricted review is universally better. It is that reviewer competence, action interface, and binding jurisdiction jointly determine both the correction domain and the corruption surface.

</details>


### [184] [Agent Harness Awareness: Turning Verified Telemetry into Operational Control for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05391)
*Manu Agrawal,Shrey Nagpal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“智能体运行环境感知”：由宿主在关键操作前向模型提供少量、最新且经过验证的状态信息，同时由宿主保留监测、执行约束、评估和最终派发权。实验显示，工具健康状态信息显著降低操作失误成本，并提升任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具说明只能描述操作的功能，无法说明工具在当前时刻是否健康或适用。运行环境往往掌握监控数据、调用轨迹和近期故障信息，但决策模型看不到这些信息，形成“运行状态盲区”，可能导致不必要的延迟和操作失败。

Method: 提出一种信任边界清晰的设计：宿主在可能产生重要后果的操作前，提供小规模、及时且经验证的状态快照，但不将测量、执行约束、评估及最终派发权交给模型。研究通过受控隔离基准固定任务和候选操作的语义，仅改变模型可获得的运行信息；另以本地网络执行、遥测调用跨度和故障注入进行端到端复现。

Result: 在三种模型的二百一十六次决策消融实验中，与格式相同但不含有效状态信息的对照相比，经过验证的工具健康状态使平均可观测策略遗憾降低百分之九十六点九，从四千二百五十二毫秒降至一百三十一毫秒；单次派发完成率由百分之六十九点四升至百分之九十五点八。另一个包含二十七次决策的端到端实验中，平均遗憾从八千九百五十六毫秒降至零，九种模型与故障组合均完成实时操作，而对照组为九种中的五种。

Conclusion: 结果支持在派发前向模型提供经过验证的宿主遥测信息，能够实质改善工具与网络场景中的操作选择。本文验证的是该架构在工具健康状态方面的实例；运行时状态、硬件压力、经济因素、考虑重试的恢复策略以及异构工具选择仍需进一步实证研究。

Abstract: Tool descriptions tell an agent what an action can do, but they do not necessarily reveal whether that action is healthy or suitable at the moment of use. Execution harnesses often possess this information through monitors, traces, and recent failures while the model choosing the next action does not. We call this gap operational blindness. We introduce Agent Harness Awareness, a trust-separated design in which the host supplies a small, fresh, verified state snapshot before a consequential action while retaining measurement, enforcement, evaluation, and final dispatch authority. We test its tools-and-network instance with ToolRoute, a controlled isolation benchmark that holds the task and candidate semantics constant and varies the operational information available to the model. In a 216-decision ablation across GPT-5.6 Sol, Claude Sonnet 5, and Gemini 3.7 Flash, verified tool-health state reduced mean observable policy regret by 96.9% against a same-shape neutral control (4,252 ms to 131 ms) and raised single-dispatch completion from 69.4% to 95.8%. In a separate 27-decision end-to-end replication with live local TCP/HTTP execution, OpenTelemetry client spans, and Toxiproxy-injected faults, verified state reduced mean regret from 8,956 ms to 0 ms (zero excess observable operational cost) and completed the live action in 9/9 model-by-fault cases versus 5/9 for the neutral control. These studies establish the tools-and-network instance of Agent Harness Awareness: selected host telemetry materially improves pre-dispatch operational choice. Runtime state, hardware pressure, economics, retry-aware recovery, and heterogeneous selection remain open empirical extensions of the same architecture.

</details>


### [185] [Uncertainty-Aware Resource Orchestration for Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05392)
*Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于保形预测的在线资源预留方法，可在执行需求未知且波动显著的智能体流水线启动时，按指定概率保障资源覆盖和期限达成，并减少资源浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由大语言模型驱动的智能体会在运行中动态调用工具、更新记忆并反复执行，因而其计算量、带宽需求和总时延无法在派发时准确获知，且可能呈现重尾、多峰和非平稳特征。预留不足会导致限流或错过期限，预留过多则浪费边缘或云端资源；现有依赖已知流程图或固定需求模型的方法难以适用。

Method: 论文将问题形式化为不确定性感知的智能体编排：在派发时预留计算与带宽资源，并以预先设定的概率保障实际需求得到覆盖，同时检验任务能否按期完成。提出一种面向编排的保形预留在线算法，根据近期执行轨迹的滑动窗口，利用保形预测生成资源分配；该方法在有限样本条件下提供不依赖具体分布假设的覆盖保证。

Result: 在包含异质任务以及多峰、重尾需求的仿真中，所提方法是受评估方法中唯一能在所有风险水平下满足目标概率覆盖的方法。在满足覆盖要求的方法中，它的过度预留最少，资源浪费约为切比雪夫界基线的一半。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于历史执行轨迹进行保形预测，可为需求未知、动态变化的智能体流水线提供具有统计保证的派发时资源预留，并兼顾期限保障与资源利用效率。结论目前基于仿真评估，实际部署效果仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems powered by large language models (LLMs) execute as autonomous multi-step pipelines that dynamically invoke tools, integrate observations into memory, and iterate until a task is resolved or a resource budget is exhausted. Unlike classical workflows with fixed graphs, the aggregate compute, bandwidth, and end-to-end latency demands of such pipelines are unknown at dispatch time: they emerge at runtime from intermediate observations and stochastic token sampling, yielding heavy-tailed, multi-modal random variables. This unpredictability forces infrastructure orchestrators into a dilemma: under-provisioning risks mid-execution throttling and deadline violations, while over-provisioning wastes scarce edge or cloud capacity. Existing schedulers require a fully known pipeline graph, while edge resource-management methods rely on parametric or stationary demand models, and neither fits the unknown, non-stationary profiles of agentic pipelines. We formalize this setting as the uncertainty-aware agentic orchestration problem, where the goal is to reserve, at dispatch time, compute and bandwidth allocations that cover actual pipeline demands, and to verify that the pipeline can meet its deadline, each with a prescribed probability. We then propose Conformal Reservation for Orchestration (CRO), an online algorithm that translates a sliding window of past execution traces into dispatch-time resource allocations with finite-sample, distribution-free coverage guarantees via conformal prediction. Simulations over heterogeneous task mixes with multi-modal and heavy-tailed profiles show that CRO is the only evaluated method that simultaneously satisfies target probabilistic coverage across all risk levels while, among coverage-feasible methods, attaining the lowest over-reservation, roughly halving the resource waste of the Chebyshev-bound baseline.

</details>


### [186] [Challenge-Aware Knowledge Recruitment with Meta-Agent Supervision for Medical Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05393)
*Riccardo Riccio,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出依据推理过程中暴露的知识缺口，动态检索知识或招募专科模型；实验表明，这种按需干预优于仅依据问题预先配置资源，但检索与招募不可随意叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在常规医学问答中表现较强，但面对复杂推理时能力下降；多智能体辩论也常无法超越强力的单模型提示。作者认为，现有方法通常只根据问题选择专家或检索知识，忽视了推理过程中逐步显现的具体事实与专业能力缺口。

Method: 提出“挑战感知自适应招募”机制：由外部挑战者持续检查不断演进的推理过程，识别具体知识或专业能力缺口，并据此触发定向知识检索，或在讨论中途招募专科模型。论文还比较了预先检索与推理中途检索、自主招募与挑战者触发招募，并进行了消融实验及不同检索方式的验证。

Result: 在涵盖九个生物医学数据集、共862道难题的基准测试中，预先检索收益有限，而推理中途、针对缺口的检索效果更好；在多专科模型系统中，该方法在九个数据集中的八个提升准确率，相比仅使用挑战者提高6个百分点。消融结果支持检索时机和缺口特定查询信号均有贡献，实时网页搜索实验也显示时机优势可跨检索后端保持。专科招募方面，自主触发不稳定；挑战者触发使某医学问答测试上的准确率从46%升至54%，且在所有测试的泛化数据集上均呈正向变化。简单地同时采用检索和专科招募反而会降低表现。

Conclusion: 医学多智能体推理的关键不仅在于是否使用外部知识或专家，更在于何时、为何以及如何协调这些资源。依据推理中实际出现的缺口实施适时干预，比仅凭问题预先配置资源更有潜力；不同干预渠道的组合则需要经过协调设计。

Abstract: Large language models perform strongly on standard medical QA but degrade on harder reasoning tasks, where multi-agent debate often fails to beat strong single-model prompting. We argue that a key limitation is that existing systems select experts and retrieve knowledge from the question alone, rather than from gaps that emerge during reasoning. We propose challenge-aware adaptive recruitment: an external CHALLENGER inspects the evolving reasoning trace and identifies specific factual or expertise gaps. These signals trigger targeted knowledge retrieval or mid-discussion specialist recruitment. On the MEDAGENTSBENCH hard subset (862 questions across nine biomedical datasets), upfront retrieval provides little benefit, while challenge-aware in-between retrieval performs better; on a multi-specialist backbone it improves accuracy on 8 of 9 datasets and adds 6 percentage points over Challenger Only. Ablations indicate benefits from both retrieval timing and gap-specific query signals, and a live web-search experiment shows that the timing advantage persists across retrieval backends. For specialist recruitment, self-triggered adaptation is unreliable, whereas Challenger-triggered recruitment raises GPT-4.1-mini MedQA accuracy from 46% to 54%, with positive direction on all tested generalization datasets. Naively combining retrieval and specialist recruitment degrades performance, highlighting the need to coordinate adaptive intervention channels. These results show that when and why external resources are recruited is a key design choice in multi-agent medical reasoning.

</details>


### [187] [A Feasibility Condition for Confidence-Gated Tiered LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05394)
*Sumit P. Ahuja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文推导了分层推理级联是否优于所有请求都交给后备模型的闭式可行条件，并指出其关键取决于本地计算成本如何计费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层推理常被视为降低大语言模型服务成本、延迟并增强隐私的途径，但现有系统通常使用冻结模型，并依据前置模型的置信信号决定本地处理或升级请求。论文关注一个基础问题：是否存在任何置信阈值，能让级联系统优于直接将所有请求交给后备模型？

Method: 将路由决策建模为二元检验，并用架构成本比区分本地处理与升级的成本。作者推导出级联可行性的闭式条件，并以两个界限刻画：与信号质量无关的可行性下界，以及理想信号下仍无法实现可行性的上界。进一步沿架构成本比分析条件如何变化，并据此提出三项设计要求；现有训练后组件虽分别满足其中部分要求，但尚未共同满足全部要求。作者将协同设计架构及其测试协议作为未来研究方向。

Result: 推导表明，级联是否可行受信号质量与成本结构共同约束：低于信号无关下界时，任何信号质量下都可行；高于完美信号上界时，即使信号完美也不可行。成本比变化会使结论发生反转：本地计算需要计费时，成本可能成为主要限制，这与近期实证发现相符；本地计算成本已沉没、例如由分散式设备承担时，可行性变为无条件成立，而信号质量决定级联能够达到的效果。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是给出并解释分层推理级联的可行性条件及其随成本结构反转的规律，而不是提出一个已经验证的完整架构。该条件独立于未来拟议的协同设计架构，为判断级联系统是否值得采用及其设计方向提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Tiered inference distributes large language model computation across a hierarchy of devices, from on-device and edge hardware to the datacenter, and is increasingly offered as the route to cheaper, lower-latency, and more private serving. Most such systems orchestrate frozen model checkpoints, and most decide what to resolve locally and what to escalate using a confidence signal produced by a forward-deployed model. We give a closed-form condition for when no threshold on that signal makes a tiered cascade worthwhile relative to sending every query to the backstop. Modeling the routing decision as a binary test and separating local from escalation cost through an architecture cost ratio $κ$, we derive a feasibility condition bracketed by a signal-free floor, below which a cascade is feasible at any signal quality, and a perfect-signal ceiling, above which none is. Read along the $κ$ axis the condition inverts: where local compute is billed, cost binds, matching a recent empirical finding; where it is sunk, on decentralized hardware, feasibility becomes unconditional and signal quality instead sets what the cascade achieves. We turn the condition into three design requirements that existing trained-in primitives satisfy only in isolation, and we propose, as future work, a co-designed architecture and a protocol to test it. The condition and the inversion hold independently of that architecture; they are the paper's contribution.

</details>


### [188] [Towards Robust Long-Lived Agents: Benchmarking Experience Lifecycles in Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05450)
*Shijue Huang,Hangyu Guo,Hengheng Zhang,Guo An,Zhitao He,Qi Tian,Yiren Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个评测智能体长期积累、复用和更新经验的基准，发现经验有助于完成任务，但智能体难以在环境变化后及时修正经验，也未必能有效运用已记录的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体正从执行单次任务转向跨多个用户目标提供持续协助，因此需要理解陌生环境、跨任务复用经验，并在环境变化时更新经验。现有评测较少揭示经验具体哪些部分被修改、保留或遗失，难以深入分析长期经验管理能力。

Method: 研究提出覆盖十八种交互环境的经验生命周期基准，并将经验区分为两类：描述环境结构及访问方式的结构经验，以及指导跨任务决策的规则经验。基准采用以经验为核心的任务流构造方式，使多个任务共享经验，并在过程中引入不作提示的环境变化；评测任务表现，同时追踪任务流中保存经验的变化。

Result: 对二十种模型的评测显示，保留下来的经验能够提升任务表现，但环境变化后，智能体难以使经验保持准确和及时。即使所需经验已被正确记录，智能体也不总能成功完成任务，说明记录经验与有效运用经验并非一回事。

Conclusion: 长期运行的智能体面临两项相互补充的挑战：有选择地维护经验，以及在任务中有效使用经验。该基准为分析经验的获取、复用、修订与遗失提供了系统化评测框架。

Abstract: As agents become more capable, they are moving beyond one-off tasks toward sustained assistance across successive user goals. Such assistance requires agents to discover how unfamiliar environments work, reuse what they learn across tasks, and keep that experience current as environments evolve. Existing benchmarks increasingly study experience reuse and adaptation, but provide limited insight into which parts of accumulated experience agents revise, retain, or lose. To address this gap, we introduce ReScopeBench, a benchmark for the experience lifecycle across 18 interactive environments. It distinguishes structural experience about how an environment is organized and accessed from rule experience about reusable principles and constraints that guide decisions across tasks. To study how experience is acquired, reused, and revised, we propose an experience-first construction approach. It builds task streams around shared experience and introduces silent environmental changes. We evaluate task performance and track changes in saved experience throughout each stream. Across 20 models, we find that retained experience improves task performance, but agents struggle to keep it current as environments change. Even when the required experience is correctly recorded, agents do not always complete tasks successfully. These findings identify selective maintenance and effective use as complementary challenges for long-lived agents.

</details>


### [189] [Can Large Language Models Review the Scientific Literature? A Test Against a 102-Article Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05459)
*Abdullah Al Kalbani,David M. Schultz,Alexandra K. Anderson-Frey,Zhonghua Zheng,Catherine McGuire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究以西班牙羽流天气型综述及其一百零二篇文献为基准，评估六种人工智能工具在文献综述四个阶段的表现。工具可辅助检索和解读，但在筛选证据、综合研究和判断研究共识方面仍需专家把关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多聚焦于文献综述中的单项任务，未能反映完整综述流程。研究旨在系统检验人工智能工具从寻找文献到提出未来研究方向的能力，并明确其适用边界。

Method: 研究围绕四个阶段开展评估：检索相关论文、解读论文、跨文献综合，以及提出未来研究方向。以一篇已发表的西班牙羽流天气型综述及其一百零二篇文献为基准；比较六种工具的检索表现，并让其中三种工具在完整论文集上识别相关研究、撰写综合内容和提出研究方向。两位领域专家对综合内容进行盲评。

Result: 初始检索中，六种工具合计找到五十七篇文献，表现最佳的单一工具找到四十二篇。机构图书馆访问权限使两种工具找到的文献数分别从四十二篇增至八十二篇、从十五篇增至七十九篇。在完整论文集中，三种工具均找出了全部十八篇实质讨论该天气型的文章，但将百分之四十至百分之七十九的仅顺带提及文章误判为实质相关。工具生成的综述总体较准确，但洞见有限，且出现无依据论断、引文错误和夸大共识等问题。三种工具提出的研究方向均超出了该领域文献范围，其中两种提出的问题与基准综述中的问题较为接近。没有任何一种工具在所有任务中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 人工智能工具能够辅助科学文献检索与解读，机构资源访问可显著改善文献发现能力；但其相关性判断和跨文献综合仍存在误判与证据表述风险，不能取代专家对证据质量、研究共识和未来方向的判断。研究据此提出八项使用建议，但摘要未具体列出。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support scientific literature reviews, yet evaluations often examine individual tasks rather than the review process as a whole. We assess six AI tools across four stages: finding relevant articles, interpreting them, synthesizing across them, and proposing future research. The benchmark is a published review of the Spanish plume weather pattern and its 102-article corpus.
  In the initial searches, five tools collectively identified 57 articles, with the best individual tool finding 42. Institutional library access increased discovery from 42 to 82 articles for Codex and from 15 to 79 for Claude Code. Given the complete corpus of 102 PDFs, three tools correctly identified all 18 articles discussing the Spanish plume substantially, but incorrectly classified 40% to 79% of the 84 passing mentions as substantial. The same three tools produced syntheses evaluated blindly by two domain experts. Although largely accurate, the syntheses provided limited insight and sometimes contained unsupported claims, quotation errors, and exaggerated statements of consensus. Finally, all three tools proposed research directions broader than the Spanish plume literature, although two approached questions identified in the benchmark review.
  No single tool performed best across all tasks. We provide eight recommendations for researchers using AI tools in literature reviews. Our findings suggest that LLMs can assist with discovering and interpreting scientific literature, particularly when institutional access is available, but cannot replace expert judgment in evaluating evidence or synthesizing research.

</details>


### [190] [SlackBench: Benchmarking Agents on Collaborative Projects Grounded in Real Code Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05512)
*Mingxuan Li,Huanzhi Mao,Ruitao Zheng,Jarod Stanbury James,Yunze Li,Natanya Anderson,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Alex Dimakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向协作式研究与工程工作的智能体基准，要求系统整合工作沟通、代码仓库和文档中的信息；现有系统表现仍有明显提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测常依赖合成数据或孤立任务，难以体现真实知识工作中的多方协作、长对话、决策演变及信息分散等特点。即使是简单问题，也可能需要跨渠道检索、恢复讨论上下文、检查代码状态，并处理过时或相互矛盾的证据。

Method: 构建基于真实学术研究项目隐私保护式重现的评测基准，提供可复现环境，涵盖多方对话、决策变化及与代码仓库和在线文档关联的内容。基准包含一百四十个问题，覆盖异构来源检索、对无法回答或基于错误前提的问题拒答、目标导向总结，以及长对话中的情境推断。

Result: 在三十三种前沿模型及智能体执行框架的评测中，表现最佳的系统得分约为百分之八十，多数系统低于百分之六十六。结果表明，当前智能体在跨对话与代码证据进行检索、推理和综合方面仍存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 该基准为评估智能体在协作工作环境中的实际能力提供了可扩展框架。其任务强调跨来源、随时间变化的证据综合，而非静态语料问答，可用于揭示并推动智能体在真实研究与工程工作流中的能力改进。

Abstract: AI Agents are becoming digital coworkers that can browse the web, write code, run experiments, and assist humans over extended horizons. Yet existing benchmarks fall short of real knowledge work: many rely on synthetic data or isolated tasks, missing the complex, multi-party dynamics of collaborative projects, where critical information is scattered across conversations, code, and evolving decisions. Even simple workplace questions may require locating relevant channels and direct messages, recovering context from long discussions, inspecting repository state, reconciling stale or conflicting evidence, and synthesizing across sources.
  We introduce SlackBench, a benchmark for evaluating agents on realistic research and engineering workflows that require joint reasoning over workplace communication and evolving repository state. Grounded in privacy-preserving reenactments of real academic research projects, SlackBench provides reproducible environments with realistic multi-party dialogue, evolving decisions, and corresponding code changes grounded in GitHub repositories and Google Docs. It contains 140 queries spanning heterogeneous-source agentic search, abstention on unanswerable or false-premise questions, goal-directed summarization, and circumstance inference over long conversations. These tasks are not reducible to static corpus question answering: agents must determine which conversations matter, traverse threads, inspect repository state, and synthesize evidence across communication and code artifacts. Across 33 frontier models and agent harnesses, the best system scores around 80% and most agents score below 66%, highlighting substantial headroom for improvement. SlackBench thus provides an extensible framework for evaluating agents in collaborative work environments. The SlackBench dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/HuanzhiMao/SlackBench .

</details>


### [191] [MA-SafeDiffuser: Safe Multi-Agent Planning with Diffusion Probabilistic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05524)
*Kiran Ravish,Ankita Kushwaha,Preeti,Pawan Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多智能体轨迹规划的安全扩散方法，在保证联合安全约束的同时尽量保留规划质量，并通过实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散模型的轨迹规划方法虽具备较强的规划能力，但在多智能体场景中，单个智能体的安全约束不足以保证整体安全，智能体之间还需避免相互冲突。此外，安全约束可能造成局部困陷或死锁，因此需要兼顾安全性与持续行动能力的多智能体规划框架。

Method: 该方法将单智能体安全扩散框架扩展至多智能体场景，在反向扩散更新中嵌入控制屏障函数约束。它将联合安全集合表示为各智能体自身安全集合与智能体两两安全集合的交集，并设计集中式及考虑通信条件的分布式受约束去噪程序，在一定假设下证明安全集合的不变性。为缓解局部困陷和死锁，方法还引入随时间变化的安全规范与活性控制屏障函数，并提供包含多智能体二维迷宫环境的基准测试套件。

Result: 实验显示，与不受约束的扩散规划基线相比，该方法减少了安全违规次数，同时保持了规划质量。摘要未提供具体数值或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 该研究为扩散式多智能体轨迹规划提供了具有有限时间扩散不变性保证的安全约束机制，并通过理论分析、算法和基准测试加以支持。其结果表明，在多智能体规划中结合安全约束与活性机制具有应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose MA-SafeDiffuser, a multi-agent extension of SafeDiffuser that equips diffusion-based trajectory planners with finite-time diffusion invariance guarantees for joint-agent safety specifications. Building on the single-agent construction that embeds control barrier function (CBF) constraints into reverse-diffusion updates, we: (i) formalize joint safe sets as intersections of per-agent and pairwise barrier sets; (ii) derive centralized and decentralized (communication-aware) constrained denoising procedures with provable invariance under mild assumptions; (iii) address local-trap and deadlock phenomena via time-varying specifications and liveness CBFs; and (iv) develop a lightweight benchmarking suite including a multi-agent Maze2D domain. Empirically, MA-SafeDiffuser reduces violation counts relative to unconstrained diffusion baselines, while retaining planning quality. We provide algorithms, proofs, and reference implementations.

</details>


### [192] [Towards Efficient Whole Page Ranking with One-Pass Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05542)
*Qi Xu,Zhigang Hua,Zijie Lei,Yan Xie,Jizhe Zhang,Lynn Lu,Delun Du,Sha Hua,Ren Mao,Ke Pan,Haomin Yu,Sandeep Pandey,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种兼顾排序质量与推理效率的整页排序方法，在接近自回归方法生成质量的同时，显著提升解码速度并降低线上计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整页排序需要同时安排广告与自然内容，并平衡商业目标、用户参与度及复杂布局约束。非自回归方法推理高效，却通常独立评估候选内容，难以建模内容顺序带来的交互；自回归方法能利用已选内容作为上下文，但逐步推理开销较大，不利于大规模部署。因此，研究希望缩小两类方法在质量与效率上的差距。

Method: 提出多项目评分框架，将前缀感知能力融入非自回归式排序。该框架在共享表示架构中结合全局候选项交互、局部前缀条件信息与位置感知评分；每次请求只需计算一次可复用表示，再在受约束的束搜索中进行轻量级前缀条件评分，从而捕捉顺序依赖，避免反复执行完整模型。

Result: 在快手随机数据集和强化学习推荐数据集上，该方法的生成质量接近自回归基线，同时中央处理器解码速度分别提升至其的四点零七倍和三点五四倍。某大型社交视频平台的随机线上评估显示，实现指标提升百分之零点一零四、观看时长提升百分之零点零五八，排序服务中央处理器消耗降低百分之九点七三。

Conclusion: 前缀感知的多项目评分能够在高效非自回归生成中融入关键顺序依赖，有效缩小其与自回归方法的质量差距，并具备满足工业级规模部署的效率。

Abstract: Whole-page ranking jointly optimizes advertisements and organic content to balance business outcomes and user engagement under complex layout and business constraints. Existing non-autoregressive (NAR) approaches enable efficient page generation but typically score candidate items independently of previously selected items, limiting their ability to capture order-dependent interactions. Autoregressive (AR) methods address this limitation through sequential conditioning, but incur substantial inference overhead that challenges large-scale deployment. Bridging this quality-efficiency gap remains a challenge in industrial whole-page ranking.
  We propose Multi-Item Scoring (MIS), a prefix-aware NAR-style framework that incorporates sequential context into efficient multi-item scoring. MIS integrates global candidate interactions, localized prefix conditioning, and position-aware scoring within a shared representation architecture. By computing reusable representations once per request and performing lightweight prefix-conditioned scoring during constrained beam search, MIS captures important sequential dependencies without repeated full-model inference.
  Experiments on KuaiRand-1K and RL4RS demonstrate that MIS approaches the generation quality of an autoregressive CGA baseline while achieving 4.07x and 3.54x faster CPU decoding, respectively. In a randomized evaluation on a large-scale social-video platform, MIS improves the realization metric by 0.104% and watch time by 0.058%, while reducing ranking-service CPU consumption by 9.73%. These results demonstrate that prefix-aware multi-item scoring can narrow the quality gap between NAR and AR generation while retaining the efficiency required for industrial scalability.

</details>


### [193] [Iterated creation, grading, and revision of data science projects by language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05548)
*Rohan Alexander*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究显示，智能体模型可借助反馈改善端到端数据科学项目表现，但提升幅度因模型及其担任的角色而异；评分噪声、自评偏高以及单纯增加修订机会也会影响结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估智能体模型能否独立完成完整的数据科学项目，并检验反馈迭代是否能提升成果质量。作者也关注提升究竟来自具体反馈，还是来自额外的修订时间与工具调用机会，以及不同模型担任创作者和评分者时的差异。

Method: 研究选取来自五家开发者的七个模型。创作者模型使用指定工具完成数据集下载、清理、分析，并撰写简短论文；评分者模型负责评估成果，之后创作者可根据反馈修改，共进行三轮。每个模型分别担任创作者和评分者，共组成四十九种配对，每种配对运行三次，初始提交共一百四十七份。研究还比较自评与他评，并考察评分噪声及无反馈修订的作用。

Result: 一百四十七份初始提交中，一百零七份能够运行并生成结果。平均得分从初始的百分之五十九升至最终的百分之七十六。提升幅度因创作者而异，约为三至四十六个百分点；因评分者而异，约为十三至三十二个百分点。模型为自己的成果评分时，得分平均比其他模型给出的平均分高约九个百分点；轮次间观察到的差异约有一半可归因于评分者自身的波动。反馈通常带来最高评分，但仅提供修订机会或增加时间、工具调用预算，也能使部分模型明显进步，另一些模型则改善有限。

Conclusion: 反馈迭代能够提升模型完成数据科学项目的表现，但效果并非普遍一致，而取决于创作者与评分者的组合。由于评分噪声和自评偏高，分数变化不应全部视为真实进步；同时，较弱模型通常难以推动较强模型进一步改善。评估此类系统时，应区分反馈内容、修订机会与资源增加各自的贡献。

Abstract: We evaluate the extent to which agentic AI models can complete an end-to-end data science project, and how this improves with feedback. A 'writer' model with specific tools creates a complete data science project including downloading, cleaning, and analyzing a dataset, and writing a short paper about it. The project is executed and a 'grader' model evaluates it. The writer can then revise its submission, and this repeats for a total of three rounds. We consider seven models from five developers: Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, DeepSeek, and Z.AI. Each model is both a writer and a grader, so there are 49 writer-grader pairs and we run each pair three times. Of the 147 initial submissions, 107 run and render, and the average score increases from 59 per cent to 76 per cent after the final round of feedback. By writer the extent of the increase ranges from 3 percentage points for Anthropic's Opus 5.5 to 46 percentage points for Anthropic's Haiku 4.5, and by grader it ranges from 13 percentage points for Google's Gemini 3.8 Flash to 32 percentage points for Anthropic's Haiku 4.5. When a model grades its own writing the score tends to be about 9 percentage points higher than the average of what the other models give it. About half of the difference that we see from round to round is within the grader's own noise. Feedback generally results in the highest marks, but even just having the chance to revise, or having the additional budget, in terms of time or tool calls, without any feedback, results in considerable increases for some of the models but not for others. We find that how much such a loop helps depends on which model plays which role, but weaker models generally do not improve stronger models.

</details>


### [194] [Whose Transparency is it Anyway? Understanding Generative AI Transparency Needs and Gaps Across Stakeholders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05549)
*Genevieve Smith,Sahiba Chopra,Natalia Luka,Min Kyung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，生成式人工智能的透明度需求虽有共同基础，却会随使用者角色、任务和情境变化；现行做法更贴合建设者与政策制定者，未充分回应终端用户，因此需要多元、情境化的透明度机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能治理日益重视透明度，但各方对透明度的含义、适用对象和适用情境缺乏共识。以人工智能披露和静态文档为主的通行做法，可能忽视不同利益相关者的实际需求，尤其是终端用户的需求。

Method: 研究借鉴社会技术建构理论中的解释灵活性视角，调查了来自全球的四百名终端用户、人工智能建设者和政策制定者，并组织终端用户参与结构化焦点小组，以定量调查和定性讨论相结合的方式考察各方如何理解和体验生成式人工智能透明度。

Result: 三类群体对透明度的基本理解及其目标相近，主要关注准确性与隐私保护。但终端用户对透明度功能重要性的评价与其感知到的实际可用性之间差距更大；仅有百分之二十八的终端用户认为现行做法非常符合其需求，而建设者和政策制定者的比例分别为百分之五十三和百分之四十九。此外，透明度优先事项会随情境和任务结构变化，各群体特有的需求框架会在相关情境中凸显，在其他情境中减弱。

Conclusion: 有意义的人工智能透明度不应依赖适用于所有人的单一方案，也不能局限于披露人工智能身份和提供静态文档。治理实践应建立多元且能适应具体情境的透明度机制，反映不同用户和利益相关者的需求，并正视权力关系对透明度设计及其服务对象的影响。

Abstract: Transparency has become a central principle in AI governance, yet there is little agreement about what transparency means, for whom, and in what contexts. Drawing on interpretive flexibility within the Social Construction of Technology, this paper examines how different stakeholders understand and experience transparency in generative AI. This study draws from a global survey of 400 end-users, AI builders, and policymakers, complemented by a structured focus group with end-users, grounding the quantitative findings in qualitative insights from the group most underserved by current transparency practice. We present three key findings. First, all three groups share similar understandings of what transparency means and what it should afford, centered on ensuring accuracy and privacy. Second, gaps between the importance of transparency features and their perceived availability are substantially larger for end-users than builders, revealing how power shapes what gets built and who it serves. Only 28% of end-users report that current transparency practices reflect their needs very well, compared to 53% of builders and 49% of policymakers. Third, transparency is situationally constructed, priorities shift with context and task structure, with role-specific frames activating when context demands them and receding when it does not. We argue that meaningful AI transparency requires moving beyond a one-size-fits-all solution, including current emphases on AI disclosure and static documentation, toward pluralistic transparency infrastructures that reflect the needs of diverse users and stakeholders.

</details>


### [195] [VirtualTrialHarness: Coordinating Agentic Operationalization of Virtual Trial Protocols Across Complex Clinical Schemas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05551)
*Pengze Li,Jingna Feng,Yue Yu,Yining Wu,Xinyue Hu,Jie Lin,Avanti Bhandarkar,Cui Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出虚拟试验操作化框架，通过可复用流程技能和跨阶段决策约束，将研究方案更一致地转化为临床数据分析；在合成环境、梅奥诊所案例及医疗信息市场数据库任务中均显示出应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟试验利用常规收集的临床数据评估治疗策略，但研究方案难以直接转化为可执行分析。研究者必须根据本地数据结构和操作规范，判断数据源、患者与就诊身份、数据关联及时间解释等问题；若各分析阶段的决策不一致，可能导致队列构建和后续分析偏离研究要求。

Method: 研究提出虚拟试验操作化智能体框架，按工作流程各阶段选择可复用的程序技能，并通过明确的阶段间约定保持研究决策一致。同时构建虚拟试验基准，用于评估数据源选择和队列构建。框架在三个复杂合成环境中进行测试，并在梅奥诊所案例、医疗信息市场数据库任务及临床研究项目中评估其表现与实际应用。

Result: 在需求澄清后，该框架在合成环境中有百分之九十五点一的工作流生成了全部符合参考结果的队列输出；使用同一程序语料进行检索的对照方法为百分之六十点四。程序技能在三种语言模型中均改善了数据源恢复效果，并在梅奥诊所的十三个案例中提高了与分析人员代码的数据源重合度。针对具体机构进行适配后，框架在八项医疗信息市场数据库任务中实现了与参考结果匹配的队列执行。该框架还支持梅奥诊所虚拟试验项目中的研究者审查队列构建和终点分析。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，将程序性指导与工作流程决策协调起来，有助于提升复杂临床数据结构下虚拟试验实施的一致性与可复现性。该框架展现了支持队列构建和研究者审核的潜力，但摘要未提供临床疗效结论，也不足以说明其在更广泛真实机构中的普遍表现。

Abstract: Virtual trials use routinely collected clinical data to evaluate treatment strategies, but translating a study protocol into an executable analysis requires determining how clinical concepts and study requirements should be represented in the available data and applied consistently across the workflow. These decisions depend on local data representations and operational conventions and can involve source selection, patient and encounter identity, linkage, and temporal interpretation. Here we introduce VirtualTrialHarness (VTHarness), an agent framework that coordinates virtual-trial operationalization by selecting reusable procedural skills for each workflow stage and uses explicit contracts to maintain consistent study decisions across stages. We also introduce VTBenchmark for evaluating source selection and cohort construction. Across three complex synthetic environments, VTHarness successfully produced all required cohort outputs matching the references in 95.1% of workflows after requirement clarification, compared with 60.4% using retrieval from the same procedural corpus. Procedural skills improved source recovery across three language models and increased source overlap with analyst code in 13 Mayo Clinic cases. Site-specific adaptation enabled reference-matched cohort execution across eight MIMIC-IV tasks. Within the Mayo Clinic Virtual Trials Program, the framework supported investigator review of cohort construction and endpoint analysis. These findings support coordinating procedural guidance with workflow decisions to improve virtual-trial implementation across complex clinical schemas.

</details>


### [196] [AGORA: Reliability-Aware Aggregation of LLM Judges via Item-Response Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05556)
*Junhyung Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需人工标签、基于项目反应理论的评审意见聚合方法；在评测样本较少或评审者可靠性差异较大时，表现优于或不逊于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有做法常对多个大语言模型评审的意见简单求平均，导致不可靠评审与可靠评审权重相同。另一方面，基于混淆矩阵的方法需要从少量人工标注数据中估计每位评审的错误率，数据不足时估计容易不稳定。因此，论文旨在提升小规模评测中的聚合可靠性，并减少对人工标注的依赖。

Method: 提出可靠性加权评估聚合方法，将每个待评项目映射到统一的潜在质量尺度，并用每位评审的敏感度参数和有序评分阈值刻画其判断特性。模型联合利用所有项目估计评审可靠性，不需要人工标签，再据此对评审意见进行加权聚合。

Result: 在三个基准测试中，该方法在较大评测集上达到或超过达维德—斯凯恩方法；当评测集缩小至数百个项目时，优势进一步扩大。在评审者可靠性差异更大的情况下，该方法仍保持准确。研究还发现，无需标签的离散度统计量可预先提示加权是否有益，模型学得的评审权重也与各评审相对人工判断的有效性相符。

Conclusion: 该方法通过从全部评审意见中共同学习评审可靠性，缓解了简单平均和小样本错误率估计的局限，尤其适用于人工标注有限、评审质量不均的评测场景。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge is a common substitute for costly human evaluation, and aggregating several judges is the standard remedy for any single judge's bias. Judge pools mix judges of very different reliability, while the human-labeled sets that validate them hold a few hundred items or fewer. In this regime the per-item mean weights weak judges as heavily as strong ones, and confusion-table models such as Dawid-Skene must estimate a per-judge error table from too few items. We propose AGORA (Aggregation Of Reliability-weighted Assessments), an item-response-theory aggregator that places every item on one latent quality scale and describes each judge by a sensitivity parameter and ordered rating thresholds, so judge reliability is learned from all items jointly and without human labels. Across three benchmarks, AGORA matches or exceeds Dawid-Skene on large evaluation sets, and its advantage widens as the set shrinks toward a few hundred items. It remains accurate as pools grow more uneven, a label-free dispersion statistic indicates in advance when trust weighting helps, and its learned trust weights track per-judge human validity.

</details>


### [197] [BrainBind: Bridging Synchronized EEG and fMRI through Long-Form Naturalistic Video Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05581)
*Shixiao Wang,Rining Wu,Jian K. Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出脑信绑定框架，将同步采集的脑电与功能磁共振信号映射到预训练的视频嵌入空间，用于自然长视频观看场景下的视觉语义检索；结果显示，不同脑信号、视频参照与系统配置各有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实视觉体验具有动态性与丰富场景信息，而脑电和功能磁共振分别提供较高的时间与空间分辨率。既有神经解码研究多单独处理一种信号，且常以静态图像或短视频为对象，较少考察两种信号在同一段长篇自然视频观看过程中的互补性，以及重复刺激和叙事背景对检索的影响。

Method: 提出脑信绑定框架，将同步采集的脑电和功能磁共振信号映射至冻结的长视频嵌入空间，并将这些空间作为共同的视觉语义参照，而非声称其代表神经活动的字面编码。在同一数据集上，以五个随机种子比较两种视频参照和四种系统配置，并通过按剧集划分、避免剧集重叠的评估，分析随机划分中重复刺激身份与叙事背景的作用。

Result: 采用全量候选排序指标时，基于全向多模态参照的功能磁共振配置表现最强，平均倒数排名为23.82，前十名归一化折损累积增益为25.31。采用考虑标签的首位召回指标时，双路联合配置的平均首位召回率最高，为13.52%。不同配置呈现不同的首位及全排序检索特征；剧集互斥评估则用于衡量随机划分中重复刺激与叙事背景带来的影响。摘要未给出该互斥评估的具体数值。

Conclusion: 在同步自然视频观看数据中，脑电与功能磁共振映射到长视频语义空间后，可形成具有差异化表现的检索方案；最佳配置取决于评价指标及排序范围。研究也强调，应区分刺激身份和叙事背景可能带来的帮助，且这些发现目前限于所研究的数据集，不能据此将视频嵌入视为神经编码本身。

Abstract: Real-world vision is dynamic and scene-rich, while EEG and fMRI resolve it at complementary temporal and spatial scales. Most neural decoding systems, however, align one modality in isolation and mostly against static images or short clips. We study the synchronized case: EEG and fMRI are recorded during the same long-form naturalistic viewing, giving a shared stimulus but also recurring narrative context and many semantically similar candidates. We present BrainBind, a framework that maps synchronized EEG and fMRI into frozen long-form video embedding spaces and treats those spaces as common visual-semantic references, not literal neural codes. On CineBrain, a five-seed Recall@K 2 x 4 matrix across two video anchors and four system configurations shows distinct retrieval profiles. With Omni, fMRI organizes the full candidate list most strongly (MRR = 23.82, nDCG@10 = 25.31), while Joint-Dual gives the highest mean label-aware R@1 (13.52%) with a distinct rank-wide profile. The complementary episode-disjoint evaluation quantifies the contribution of repeated stimulus identity and narrative context to the random split. Across these experiments, proxy choice and system configuration produce distinct top-rank and rank-wide retrieval profiles within the present synchronized dataset. The code is available at https://github.com/Forainest789/Brainbind.

</details>


### [198] [Combining Relevant Expert Knowledge Using Mixtures of Feedback Fuzzy Cognitive Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05664)
*Olaoluwa Adigun,Akash Kumar Panda,Bart Kosko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向具体查询的相关性加权方法，用来筛选并融合多个专家的模糊认知图，使最终知识网络侧重于对当前问题有用的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着知识网络连接的知识源不断增加，若不区分相关程度而直接融合专家答案，可能降低准确性并影响扩展性。论文借鉴证据法中的相关性定义：答案既要对判断具有证明力，也要与当前问题有关。

Method: 先将多个专家的反馈模糊认知图合并为共同的因果知识网络，再比较各专家图与混合图在均衡状态或极限环上的预测差异，以估计答案的证明力。随后提出类似贝叶斯定理的分解方法，将混合知识网络拆解为对应的后验式认知图，并据此比较相关性，计算最终融合权重。权重随查询变化，因此不同查询可生成不同的相关性加权网络。

Result: 论文通过一个基于真实十三节点缺血性卒中风险专家模型生成的合成认知图，展示了该方法的应用。摘要报告该方法能够依据查询调整专家知识的融合权重，但未提供定量性能指标或与其他方法的比较结果。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了将证明力与问题关联性结合起来的专家知识融合框架，使神经符号系统能够按查询选择并整合知识来源。其主要价值在于为规模不断扩大的知识网络提供查询依赖的融合机制；实际效果仍需通过更多真实任务和定量评估验证。

Abstract: A neuro-symbolic system should combine only relevant answers from expert agents or other knowledge sources. Using only relevant experts for a given input query becomes ever more important for accuracy and scalability as the knowledge network grows and connects ever more knowledge sources. We extend the standard definition of relevance from the legal theory of evidence: An answer is relevant if and only if it is both probative and material for the matter at hand given the knowledge available. We apply this relevancy scheme to mixing the feedback fuzzy cognitive maps (FCMs) of real or artificial experts. The expert FCM directed causal graphs mix or combine into a common knowledge network. We compare the equilibrium limit-cycle predictions or answers of the experts to the answers of the mixture FCM and then estimate the probative status of answers by their difference or variation. A new Bayes-like theorem de-mixes the knowledge mixture into corresponding posterior-like FCMs that also allow relevancy-based comparisons. The result is a final set of relevancy mixing weights that produces a relevancy-mixed FCM knowledge network for the given query. So different queries produce different relevancy-mixed FCMs. We demonstrate this relevancy approach using generated synthetic FCMs from an actual 13-node expert FCM that models the causes and feedbacks of ischemic-stroke risk.

</details>


### [199] [Anduin: A sybil-resistant agentic marketplace reputation system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05586)
*Reza Ramji,Preston Vander Vos,Ziming Zeng,Duc V. Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究利用链上支付关系为服务提供者评分；在大规模交易数据和模拟攻击中，所提方法显著提高了评分操纵成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体进入开放式链上市场后，选择可靠服务提供者十分困难。现有声誉机制依赖人工评价、身份凭证或自愿评论，覆盖有限；简单按交易量排名又容易受到女巫攻击，即攻击者通过多个虚假身份操纵排名。因此，论文探讨能否从已有链上支付记录中构建更可靠的提供者评分。

Method: 作者将买方与服务提供者之间的支付记录建模为二分图，并依据买方既有可信网络中可连接到提供者的图路径计算评分。论文提出三种抗女巫攻击的评分算法：一种基于最大流，另两种为放宽约束、以提升评分效果为目标的算法。研究使用覆盖十六条区块链、持续一百五十七天的数据集，包含六千六百万笔、总额六百一十万美元的智能体交易，并在八百一十三名买方上模拟攻击者进行评估。

Result: 模拟结果显示，操纵评分的成本相当于相关买方总支出的百分之二十；与标准相似度规则（协同过滤）相比，所提方法约难以操纵二十三倍。

Conclusion: 链上支付关系可作为无需依赖人工评论或稀缺身份资源的声誉信号。利用买方可信网络中的支付路径进行评分，有望提升开放式市场中服务提供者评价的抗操纵能力；不过，结论主要基于历史交易数据和模拟对手，实际部署效果仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: As autonomous AI agents enter permissionless onchain markets, selecting new service providers remains a core challenge. Existing reputation systems depend on scarce resources like human curation, identity credentials, or voluntary reviews, while naive volume rankings are vulnerable to Sybil attacks.
  We evaluate whether existing onchain payment histories can yield provider ratings that are resilient against Sybil attacks. Using a 157-day dataset comprising 66M agentic transactions worth $6.1M across 16 blockchains, we model payment flows as a bipartite graph between buyers and providers. A provider's rating is derived from graph paths linked to a buyer's existing trusted network. We propose three Sybil-resistant rating algorithms: a max-flow rating and two relaxed algorithms optimized for stronger rating performance.
  Evaluating these algorithms against simulated adversaries across 813 buyers, we show that rating manipulation costs 20% of those buyers' total spend and is around 23x harder to manipulate than the standard similarity rule (collaborative filtering).

</details>


### [200] [Toward a Compositional Knowledge Architecture for a Shared Organizational Cortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05753)
*Andrew Miracle,Phyl Georgiou,Christine Okoth,Ese Monday*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种组织记忆架构，使组织判断及其依据能够被保存、修订并用于检索；实验显示，加入维度信息及完整架构可提升检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织会在对话、文件、决策和关系中积累证据，但实际行动依赖的是基于多项观察、包含假设且会随情境变化而调整的判断。现有检索系统、知识图谱和智能体记忆通常保存记录，却难以保留这些判断及其解释过程。

Method: 提出由信号、领域、主张和维度构成的四层架构。该架构保存判断及其依据和当前状态，并通过真值维护网络在证据变化时修订判断状态；经过验证且带有版本信息的维度，则将判断定位到明确命名的坐标轴上。

Result: 注册检索实验中，加入维度术语使十位截断归一化折损累积增益提高0.028，统计检验结果为显著（p＝0.002）。系统级比较中，完整检索配置相较于仅使用信号的检索提高0.081，相较于加入图结构的信号检索提高0.053；两项结果均报告为高度显著（p＜0.001）。

Conclusion: 该架构旨在构建组织共享的认知基础，使判断及其证据能够持续保存，并在新证据出现时更新。摘要报告的实验结果表明，维度信息和完整架构有助于提升检索效果。

Abstract: Organizations accumulate evidence in conversations, documents, decisions and relationships, but they act on interpretations: judgements assembled from several observations, resting on assumptions, and revised as circumstances change. Existing retrieval, knowledge graphs and agent memory keep the records and lose the interpretation. We propose a four-layer architecture, Signals, Domains, Claims and Dimensions, in which interpretations persist with their justifications and standing, a truth-maintenance network revises that standing as evidence changes, and validated, versioned Dimensions position interpretations on named axes. We call the result a shared organizational cortex. In a registered retrieval experiment, the Dimension term gains 0.028 nDCG@10 (p = 0.002). In system-level comparisons, the full retrieval configuration improves nDCG@10 by 0.081 over Signals-only and 0.053 over graph-augmented Signals-only retrieval (both reported p < 0.001). The architecture gives organizations a persistent, revisable basis for shared judgement.

</details>


### [201] [Verified but Vacuous: Three Failure Surfaces of LLM-Driven Verified Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05590)
*Aditya Vempaty,Himaneesh Sompalle,Manan Tayal,Mowafak Allaham,Prasenjit Dey,Ashish Jagmohan,Ravi Kokku*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，代码通过验证器、证明义务确实绑定规格、规格准确表达需求，是三个不同层次；单一验证率会掩盖它们之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常用一个数字概括模型生成代码是否“经过验证”，但这个数字无法说明验证究竟保证了什么。论文将验证拆分为三项：验证器是否接受、证明是否绑定到规格，以及规格是否忠实表达非形式化需求，并检验三者是否可能彼此脱节。

Method: 论文通过两组实证分析检验这三项性质。第一组在三个 Python 基准上，以克劳德奥普斯 4.7 重现达夫尼中间表示流程，比较验证器接受率与证明对规格的绑定情况。第二组在两个 Rust 基准上使用作者自建的达夫尼中间流程；该流程要求所有输出组件都提供达夫尼证明证书，并通过规格绑定门槛，再以隐藏测试评估规格与实际需求的一致程度。

Result: 第一组显示，在人类评测基准上，验证器接受率为 99.1%，但只有 1.9% 的样本带有绑定到规格谓词或函数的证明证书。第二组显示，即使样本已通过结构性验证，仍有 11.48% 的孟班普尔特 Rust 样本和 14.82% 的竞赛题 Rust 样本未通过隐藏测试。竞赛题子集上的端到端率为 7.79%（535/6870）；论文引用的外部结果为 5.51% 和 1.88%，但由于任务预算、修复流程和测试框架不同，这些差异仅作描述性比较，不能据此声称性能匹配或优越。

Conclusion: “已验证”不应被视为单一、充分的质量保证。评估应分别报告验证器接受、证明与规格的绑定，以及规格对真实需求的忠实度；结构上有效的证明仍可能因规格本身不完整或有误而无法保证程序正确。

Abstract: What should ``verified'' mean when evaluating LLM-generated code? Many existing pipelines report a single verification number. We argue this collapses three distinct properties measured in a conditional audit sequence: verifier acceptance (Surface A), \emph{syntactic specification binding (Surface B: does the proof obligation bind to the spec?), and \emph{semantic specification fidelity} (Surface C: does the spec capture the informal requirement?). Two empirical demonstrations show the surfaces separate. \textbf{First ($A \neq B$), via Li@Opus (a same-model reproduction of \citet{li2025dafny}'s Dafny-as-intermediate-representation (IR) pipeline under Claude Opus 4.7) on three Python benchmarks}: it reaches \textbf{99.1\% verifier acceptance} on HumanEval while only \textbf{1.9\%} carry a certificate binding to a spec-defined predicate or function. \textbf{Second ($B \neq C$), via VCG on two Rust benchmarks}: we use VCG (which is our own in-house Dafny-as-intermediate pipeline) on Rust because the Rust target lets us enforce fully-verifiable output (every component ships as a Dafny certificate, ensures-binding gate passing by construction). Even inside that construction, \textbf{11.48\% (MBPP-Rust) and 14.82\% (VeriContest LC slice) of structurally-verified samples still fail hidden tests}. On the LC slice of VeriContest, \textbf{VCG's reported End2End rate is 7.79\% (535/6870).} The cited external rates are 5.51\% (GPT-5.5) and 1.88\% (single-shot Opus 4.7). These give descriptive differences of 2.3 percentage points and 4.14$\times$, respectively; task-budget, repair and harness differences prevent a matched-performance claim.

</details>


### [202] [From Waste to Discovery: Recycling High-Scored Unexposed Candidates in Taobao Feed Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05997)
*Ge Fan,Kai Meng,Huiping Chu,Yuning Jiang,Han Zhu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该系统从“高分但从未曝光”的内容中筛选候选，在召回阶段低成本注入推荐流；线上实验提升了兴趣发现与内容丰富度，同时基本保持用户互动质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统需要帮助用户发现新兴趣，但常见做法是提高低探索内容的曝光，可能引入信息不足、排序模型难以准确评估的冷启动候选，既未带来有效发现，又可能损害用户互动。论文旨在以较低资源和工程成本解决这一探索与利用的权衡问题。

Method: 提出免训练的召回系统：收集排序得分较高但从未曝光的内容，利用这些内容兼具相关性与可预测性的特点，在三十天时间窗口内进行汇总，再于召回阶段重新注入。系统通过离线存储、分布式聚合和低延迟在线服务实现；每次请求只需一次常数时间查询，额外延迟低于百分之一，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在覆盖数千万日活用户、持续运行六个多月的线上应用中，兴趣发现类别提升百分之三点四七，内容丰富度提升百分之八点四七。完播视频观看量显著提升百分之二点六二；总观看量上升百分之零点三六，但统计上不显著。该方法的发现率为平台平均水平的十三点九倍，是最强近线基线的六点三倍，同时互动质量与平台平均水平相当。消融实验显示，改用一天窗口后发现增益降至百分之零点五五且不显著；不经得分筛选而随机注入则没有发现收益，互动表现还呈下降趋势。

Conclusion: 生产环境中的高分未曝光内容可作为有效的兴趣发现来源。通过较长时间窗口积累并进行得分筛选，系统能够在不重新训练模型、仅增加极少在线开销的条件下，显著改善内容发现与丰富度，且不损害整体互动质量。

Abstract: Helping users discover new interests is vital to the long-term health of industrial recommender systems, yet the usual remedy, boosting exposure of under-explored (discovery) content, often backfires in production. We report URED (Unexposed Recycling for Efficient Discovery), a training-free recall-stage system serving Taobao feed recommendation at Alibaba for over six months to tens of millions of daily active users. It rests on a production-validated insight: naive boosting fails because it injects cold, information-poor candidates that rankers systematically over-estimate, buying no discovery gain while risking engagement. Items scored highly but never exposed (signals industrial pipelines routinely discard) form a pool that is both relevant and reliably predictable. Over a 30-day horizon URED aggregates them and re-injects at recall time through a lightweight pipeline spanning offline storage, distributed aggregation, and low-latency online serving, adding under 1% latency via a single O(1) lookup per request with no model retraining. Online A/B tests show +3.47% discovered categories and +8.47% content richness with engagement preserved: completion video views rise significantly (+2.62%) and total views move positive (+0.36%, not significant). Ablations confirm both design pillars are necessary: a one-day window collapses the discovery gain to +0.55% (not significant), and random injection without score filtering yields no gain while trending negative on engagement (-0.17%). Across production recall channels, URED reaches 13.9x the platform-average discovery rate (6.3x the strongest nearline baseline) at engagement quality on par with the platform average. We share deployment details and lessons for practitioners facing the exploration-exploitation dilemma under tight resource constraints.

</details>


### [203] [Hybrid Spark: Adaptive Speculative Decoding That Lets the Content Choose the Drafter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05611)
*Ekagra Ranjan,Bharat Venkitesh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的推测解码框架，让模型型与无模型型草稿器在每一步协同工作，并按验证资源的机会成本选择更有收益的草稿链。在代码编辑任务中，输出吞吐量最高提升至基线的1.62倍；聊天任务中则基本不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码的收益取决于批量大小、上下文长度及输入内容。模型型草稿器适用范围广，但每次只能提出较短草稿；无模型型草稿器（如基于连续词元匹配的检索器）在代码编辑等重复输入场景中能以极低成本生成较长草稿。现有自适应验证方法只支持单一模型型草稿器，而无模型型草稿器缺少逐词元置信度，无法参与验证预算分配，导致部署时只能固定一种草稿器或拆分 GPU 资源。

Method: 该框架在每一步同时运行一个模型型草稿器和一个无模型型草稿器，并在自适应验证机制内为每个请求选择收益更高的草稿链。核心是为每个待验证草稿词元设定一个“价格”，即该验证槽位若分配给批次中的其他请求所能带来的吞吐收益。通过在通用文本上一次性拟合无模型型草稿器的接受概率估计，使两类草稿器的候选链能够在同一尺度上比较。系统完全在 GPU 上运行，以保留异步调度能力，并在推理服务框架中结合模型型草稿器与连续词元检索器实现。

Result: 在代码编辑任务中，相较于仅使用模型型草稿器，批量大小为4时输出吞吐量达到基线的1.10至1.62倍，批量大小为128时达到1.04至1.27倍。在温度为0的聊天任务中，吞吐量维持在基线的0.97至1.01倍，表明性能基本持平。

Conclusion: 通过校准无模型型草稿器的接受概率，并以验证资源的机会成本统一评估不同草稿链，该方法兼顾了重复性工作负载中的高效复制与通用场景下的模型草拟能力。它无需为不同工作负载单独部署或拆分 GPU，可在代码编辑中显著提升吞吐量，同时基本保持聊天性能。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) speeds up large language model (LLM) serving, but its gain depends on the workload's \emph{shape}, such as batch size and context length, and on its \emph{content}. A model-based drafter proposes a short block on any content, while a model-free drafter such as n-gram lookup proposes long copies at almost no cost when the output repeats its input, as in code editing. Adaptive verification spends a batch-wide verification budget on the draft tokens most likely to be accepted, but it supports only one model-based drafter. Model-free drafters provide no per-token confidence, so they cannot take part in budget allocation, and deployments must either fix one drafter or split their GPUs between drafters. We present \sys, a framework that runs a model-based and a model-free drafter on every step and gives each request the better of their chains, within adaptive verification and entirely on the GPU, which keeps asynchronous scheduling intact. The key idea is a price per verified draft token: the goodput that slot would earn elsewhere in the batch. Once the model-free drafter's tokens carry calibrated acceptance estimates, fit once on general text, this price puts both drafters' chains on one scale. Instantiated with \dspark and an n-gram drafter in vLLM, \sys achieves 1.10--1.62\x the output throughput of \dspark on code editing at batch size~4 and 1.04--1.27\x at batch size~128, while staying at 0.97--1.01\x of it on chat at temperature~0. A single deployment can therefore serve both workloads.

</details>


### [204] [ViBench 2.0: A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Software Building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05620)
*Daniel Furman,Preeya Kirani,Ibrahim Sheikh,Zhen Li,Peter Zhong,Michele Catasta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向长期软件构建的基准，用连续功能需求评估智能体能否交付完整可用的产品；结果显示，单项测试通过率高并不代表整套产品可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着用户通过不断提出功能需求、让智能体逐步构建完整产品，现有编程基准只评估单次且定义明确的任务，且部分基准已接近饱和，难以反映真实的软件开发能力。因此，作者希望评估智能体在长周期、多轮开发中交付完整产品的表现。

Method: 作者提出维基准二点零，包含十七个仿照常用软件设计的全栈网页产品，以及二百四十一项逐步提出的功能需求。产品只有在全部需求完成后才接受评估：浏览器智能体执行五百四十四套测试计划，任何一套未通过，该次构建就不算成功。作者还验证了严格评分协议的可靠性，并用该基准评测九种模型；公开其中八个产品及测试工具，另外九个作为留出测试集。

Result: 最强的三种模型虽然最多能通过百分之九十八的测试计划，但完整构建成功率最高仅为百分之六十五；没有一种开放权重模型交付了完全可用的产品。与此同时，同一模型系列的三个版本在公开产品上的单次成功率从百分之三升至百分之五十三，单次构建成本则下降了一半以上。

Conclusion: 评估长期软件构建时，应以最终产品是否完整可用为核心，而不能仅看单项测试通过率。研究揭示了测试表现与整套产品可靠性之间的明显差距，也显示模型能力提升和成本下降正在迅速改善实际交付水平；论文还总结了生产环境中的经验，并提供部分产品与评测工具以支持后续研究。

Abstract: On Replit, our agentic engineering platform, users now build entire products without writing code, through successive feature requests to an agent. Yet existing coding benchmarks evaluate single, fully specified tasks, and many are near saturation. We introduce ViBench 2.0, a benchmark for long-horizon software building that we use to select our platform's models. Its 17 full-stack web products, modeled on widely used software, grow over 241 feature requests and are judged only when finished, as a user would judge them: a browser agent runs 544 test plans, and a build passes only if every plan does, under a grading protocol validated to keep this strict score reliable. Evaluating nine models, we find a reliability gap: the three strongest pass up to 98% of test plans yet deliver a fully working product in at most 65% of builds, and no open-weight model delivers one. The gap is closing fast: across three releases of one model family, pass@1 on the public products rises from 3% to 53% while the cost per build falls by more than half. We release eight products with the harness and grader, hold out nine, and report lessons from production use.

</details>


### [205] [Assurance-Conditioned Continuous Authorization for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05632)
*Mehdi Shajari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种依据当前有效保障情况持续调整智能体权限的方法，并在交易场景中通过受控测试验证其实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主软件智能体能够调用工具、委派任务或更换模型与运行环境；与此同时，先前授予的授权可能仍在执行中。静态授权难以应对这些变化，因此需要根据当前有依据的保障水平及时调整权限，并防止过期操作造成后果。

Method: 提出保障条件化持续授权方法：区分证据是否有效与其是否适用于当前情境；仅依据符合条件的证据形成保障声明，并在主体声明的权限上限及适用策略约束下，计算多维有效权限范围。通过带类型的依赖图，对与授权相关的变化进行选择性重新评估。有效权限发生变化时更新权限纪元；另设可选的授权依据版本，用于重新验证特定操作的授权条件。在资源产生重要变化前，通过受保护的效果时点检查拒绝过期操作，但实际保障程度取决于可用的提交机制。领域治理配置文件用于表达领域语义，本身不授予权限。

Result: 研究将该方法实现为回归测试套件，并以交易领域配置文件开展受控场景测试。测试覆盖默认拒绝、证据变化导致的权限收缩与恢复、策略差异、选择性不响应，以及在本地网关处、操作到达模拟经纪商边界前拒绝过期操作。结果提供了该领域内的实现层面证据。

Conclusion: 该方法为动态智能体环境中的持续授权与过期操作拦截提供了一种可组合、可选择性重新评估的设计。但研究只在单一领域验证了实现，不能据此保证其能够跨领域防止有害行为；效果时点防护也受底层提交机制能力限制。

Abstract: Autonomous software agents can act through tools, delegate work, and change their models or execution environments while earlier authorizations remain in flight. We present Assurance-Conditioned Continuous Authorization (ACCA), an approach designed to be harness-independent, for maintaining operational authority according to currently justified assurance. It distinguishes evidence validity from contextual applicability, derives assurance claims from eligible evidence, and resolves a multidimensional effective-authority envelope within a principal-declared ceiling and applicable policy. A typed dependency graph supports selective re-evaluation of authorization-relevant changes. An authority epoch changes when effective authority changes; a distinct optional authorization-basis version supports revalidation of action-specific authorization conditions. Protected effect-time enforcement can reject stale actions before a consequential resource change, subject to the available commit mechanism. A compositional Domain Governance Profile expresses domain-specific semantics without granting authority. Controlled scenario tests implemented as a regression suite using a trading profile exercise default-deny rules, evidence-conditioned contraction and recovery, policy divergence, selective non-response, and stale-action rejection at a local gateway before the simulated-broker boundary. The study establishes implementation-level evidence in one domain, not a cross-domain guarantee of harmful-behavior prevention.

</details>


### [206] [Route to Remember: Adaptive Memory Selection for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05636)
*Zeyu Fang,Jun Yin,Yongsheng Mei,Xu Han,Chaitali Joshi,Zhongkai Sun,Jie Hao,Bin Guo,Chengyuan Ma,Benjamin Z. Yao,Chenlei Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探索将用户事实记忆内化到模型参数中，并通过自蒸馏与记忆路由，使参数记忆在长期事实问答中达到强文本记忆系统的效果，同时减少每次查询读取的文本量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产智能体主要依赖文本记忆：随着历史累积，查询时需要检索或注入更多内容，带来持续增长的成本。参数记忆可将知识写入模型，避免查询时携带上下文，但此前在用户专属事实等情景记忆和语义记忆上的研究较少，且事实召回表现与文本记忆系统仍有差距。

Method: 研究采用在策略自蒸馏：以同一模型读取用户记忆时的回答作为教师信号，训练一个不依赖上下文的学生模型，使其将事实记忆内化到参数中；同时设计“先验证、再重新生成”的路由机制，根据问题适配程度分配给参数记忆或文本记忆处理。

Result: 在相关记忆基准测试中，该方法达到或超过强文本记忆基线，包括完整上下文提示和检索系统，同时每次查询读取的词元更少。研究还系统分析了参数记忆的行为，以及不同训练方案对性能的影响。

Conclusion: 参数记忆可成为持久化、多轮智能体记忆的实用且低成本组成部分。将其与文本检索结合，并采用相应的训练和评估方案，有望兼顾事实召回能力与查询效率。

Abstract: The memory of production agents is increasingly becoming a bottleneck and is built almost exclusively from text: key points are extracted from out-of-limit context and retrieved into context at query time. Therefore, every new query incurs an additional cost that grows with the accumulated history. Weight memory is a complementary alternative that internalizes knowledge into model parameters and answers with no context at all, and has recently proven effective in self-evolving agents for internalizing skills and behaviors. However, its application in episodic and semantic memory, such as user-specific facts, remains comparatively unexplored, and a gap remains between earlier attempts at weight memory and text-based systems regarding recall performance. We study how to make weight memory competitive as a long-term factual store by applying on-policy self-distillation (OPSD). It trains a context-free student from a teacher, which is the same model conditioned on the user's memory, together with a verify-then-regenerate router that lets weight and text memory each cover the queries they handle best. On the HaluMem benchmark, OPSD matches or surpasses strong text-memory baselines, including full-context prompting and retrieval systems, while reading fewer tokens per query. We further conduct an extensive study that characterizes the behavior of weight memory and analyzes how different training recipes affect its performance.
